ciṟṟuyirkaḷik koṉṟu avaṟṟiṉ ūṉi uṇavāka aruntuvatu tīviṉiyākāteṉṟum, ākavē ūṉuṇavu koḷpavarkaḷ ataṉi nīkkuvatu vēṇṭappaṭāteṉṟum virittut tamakkut tōṉṟiya ētukkaḷi oruvar eṭuttuk kāṭṭiṉār. ivarip pōlavē iṅṅaṉam kūṟuvār iṉṉum cilar āṅkāṅku uḷarātaliṉ, avarkaḷ kūṟuvaṉavaṟṟi ārāyntu muṭivu kaṭṭa vēṇṭuvatu iṉṟiyamiyātatā yirukkiṉṟatu. ulakiṉkaṇṇuḷḷa enta matattavarum ūṉuṇavu koḷvati maṟuppatilliyeṉṟu collukiṉṟār. paṇṭik kālantoṭṭuc civa camayattavarkaḷ enta uyiriyuṅ kollutal ākāteṉṟum, koṉṟu ataṉ ūṉi uṇṇutal tīviṉiyāmeṉṟum aṟivuṟutti varukiṉṟaṉar. itaṟkuk kollāmaṟ koṉṟatit tiṉṉāmaṟ kuttiraṅ kōḷkaḷavu kallāmal eṉṉum paṭṭiṉattaṭikaḷ tirumoḻiyum, kollā viratam oṉṟu koṇṭārē nallōr eṉṉum tāyumāṉa aṭikaḷ tirumoḻiyumē yāvarum aṟinta cāṉṟākum. iṉit tēvāra tiruvācakaṅkaḷ, tirumantiram mutalāṉa civat tirumuṟikaḷilum, civa cittānta nūlkaḷilum kollāmi pulāluṇṇāmi ākiya aṟaṅkaḷi naṉku eṭuttu aṟivuṟuttum tirumoḻikaḷ aḷavillātaṉa. avaṟṟi virippiṟ perukum. civa camayattiṉkaṭ collappaṭṭavāṟē ataṉip piṉpaṟṟi oḻukum civa vēḷāḷarum, pārppaṉarum paṇṭik kālantoṭṭuk koliyum puliyum nīkki oḻuki varukiṉṟaṉar.
iṉi, mikap paḻiya kālattatākiya corāṭaratu camayattiṉ kaṇṇum āṭu, māṭu, kutiri, kōḻi mutaliyavaṟṟik kolvatu perun tīviṉiyākac collappaṭṭiruppatip pāracīkaratu avatā eṉṉum paṇṭi nūliṟ pōnta vēṇṭukōḷ oṉṟāl naṉku viḷaṅkum.
iṉip, putta camayattiṟkum muṟpaṭṭatākiya camaṇa camayamāṉatu kollāmi, pulāluṇṇāmi eṉṉum aṟattiṉkaṇ taliciṟantu niṉṟu varutalum ellārum naṉku uṇarntatēyām.
putta camayatti ākkiya kutamacākkiyar niruvāṇam aṭitaṟ kuriya eṭṭu muṟikaḷuḷ, tīṅkaṟṟa uṇavu koḷḷutali oṉṟāka vakuttuk kūṟiyirukkiṉṟār. mēlum, avar uyirkkolikku ulakamellām naṭuṅkukiṉṟatu: makkaḷellārum karaviṉik kaṇṭu naṭuṅkukiṉṟaṉar; uṉṉiṭattiṟ piṟa uyirkaḷ eṅṅaṉam naṭantu koḷḷa vēṇṭumeṉṟu nī virumpukiṉṟaṉiyō, aṅṅaṉamē nīyum avikaḷiṭattil naṭantu koḷḷal vēṇṭum; uyirkkoli ceyyātē; etaṉiyum cāvappaṇṇātē eṉṟu aṟivuṟuttiya aṟamoḻiyum puttacamaya nūlkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. ātalāṟ, puttar, kollāmi, pulā luṇṇāmi eṉṉum aṟattiṟṟali niṉṟavarākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. aṅṅaṉamāyiṉ, cantaṉ eṉṉun taṭṭāṉ oruvaṉ, cōṟṟōṭu kalantu koṭutta paṉṟi iṟicciyip puttar uṇavākak koṇṭatu eṉṉi yeṉiṉ, ulaka naṉmiyiṉiyē ennēramuṅ karutik koṇṭirunta avar cōṟṟiṟ kalantu koṭutta paṉṟi iṟicciyit terintu koḷḷāmal uṇṭāreṉṟu koḷvatē poruttamuṭittu. ataṟkumuṉ paṉṟi iṟicci mutaliya ūṉuṇavu uṇṭu paḻakkap paṭṭiruntavarāyiṉ, avaṉ tanta av viṟicciyi uṇṭataṉāl iṟantirukkamāṭṭār. avar ūṉuṇavu koḷḷātavarāy neṭuka uyirvāḻntu vantamiyāl, avaṉkoṭutta paṉṟiyiṟicciyiyuṇṭu ceriyāmiyāl, uṇṭa aṉṟikkē uyir tuṟantār. aṅṅaṉamāyiṉ, avar ūṉuṇavu koḷḷātavar eṉpati aṟintiruntum, at taṭṭāṉ avarkku uṇavu koṭuttatu eṉṉiyeṉiṉ, avaṉ kalviyaṟivu illāta vaṉāy aṉpilmaṭṭum ciṟantāṉātal vēṇṭum. mēlum avaṉ iḻikulattāṉākaliṉ, avar vaṭanāṭṭil aṟivuṟuttivanta aṟamoḻikaḷi aṟiyāṉāy avaratu perumiyi maṭṭum piṟar kūṟakkēṭṭu aṟintavaṉāy avari aṉpō ṭēṟṟut tāṉuṇṇum paṉṟi iṟicciyi avarkkuṅ koṭuttāṉākal vēṇṭum. allatu, avviṟicciyic cōṟṟil maṟittuk kalantukoṭuttāṉ eṉpataṉāl, avar ūṉ uṇṇār eṉpatit terintē tāṉuṇṇum ūṉuṇavic ciṟantatākak karuti, avarkkum ataṉi ūṭṭutal vēṇṭumeṉa aṉpu mēlīṭṭiṉāṟ cōṟṟiṉkaṇ vittu maṟittuk koṭuttāṉākal vēṇṭum. ivaṉ tanta ivvūṉuṇavu puttaratu muṭiviṟku ētuvāṉami paṟṟi avartam cīṭarkaḷ (māṇākkarkaḷ) avaṉ mēṟ peritum vekuṇṭaṉar eṉpataṉip puttaruṇarntu, avarkku āṟutal colli attaṭṭāṉuṭiya aṉpiyē viyantupēci uyirviṭuttaṉar. ātalāṟ, puttar kollāmi, pulāluṇṇāmi eṉṉum aṟattiṟ ṟaliniṉṟavar eṉpatu aiyuṟaṟpālataṉṟu. mēlum, ūṉuṇavu puttaratu muṭiviṟku ētuvāyiṟṟu eṉpataṉāl, ūṉuṇavu koḷvataṉ tīmiyum ivvaralāṟṟiṉāl naṉku viḷaṅkā niṟkum.
iṉṉum puttariyaṭuttut tōṉṟi ulakameṅkaṇum avartam aṟamoḻikaḷip paravacceytu vanta acōka maṉṉaṉ tāṉ cetukkiya mutaṟ kalveṭṭil, uṇavukkākavāvatu, vēḷvi vēṭkum poruṭṭākavāvatu enta uyiriyuṅ koliceytal ākātu eṉṟu poṟittiruttalum, eṭṭāvatu kalveṭṭil mūccuviṭum enta uyiriyuṅ koli ceyyappaṭutaliṉiṉṟum, paliyiṭappaṭutaliṉiṉṟum muṟṟum vilakkutalāl maṭṭumē camayamāṉatu vaḷarum eṉṟu poṟittiruttalum puttaruṭiya kollāmi, pulāluṇṇāmi aṟatti naṉku viḷakkik kāṭṭum.
iṉi, yūtarkkum kiṟittuva camayattavarkkum mutaṉūlākiya pipiliṉ mutalākamattiṟ kaṭavuḷ ulakaṅkaḷit tōṟṟuvittu makkaṭku mutaṟṟāy tantiyarip paṭittapiṉ avari nōkki, nilattiṉ mēluḷḷa vittukkaḷit tarum ovvoru pūṇṭiṉiyum, vittukkaḷōṭu paḻattiyun tarum ovvoru marattiṉiyum yāṉ umakkut tantirukkiṉṟēṉ. avi umakku uṇavākuka eṉṟu kūṟiyaruḷiṉār eṉpatu viḷakkamākac collappaṭṭiruttalāṟ, kaṭavuḷāl mutaṉ mutal makkaḷukku uṇavākak koṭukkappaṭṭavi puṟ, pūṇṭu, maram, ceṭi, koṭikaḷiṉ payaṉkaḷēyāmeṉpatu iṉitu peṟappaṭukiṉṟatu. mēlum, aicāya eṉṉum ākamattil uyirkaḷik koṉṟu paliyiṭuvōri viḷittu, nīṅkaḷ eṉṉi nōkkic ceyyum vēḷvikaḷ etaṉ poruṭṭu? naṉṟāy vaḷarkkappaṭṭa vilaṅkukaḷiṉ koḻuppiyum, cemmāṟiyāṭukaḷip poritta avi uṇaviyum eṉakku niṟiyak koṭuttīrkaḷ; āṉāl erutukaḷ, āṭṭuk kuṭṭikaḷ, veḷḷāṭṭuk kiṭāykaḷ ivaṟṟiṉ irattattil yāṉ makiḻcci yaṭiyamāṭṭēṉ. eṉṉetiril nīṅkaḷ varumpōtu eṉatu maṉṟatti mitippataṟku yār ivikaḷi ummiṭam peṟavirumpiṉār? eṉṟu coṉṉami naṉkeṭuttuk kāṭṭappaṭṭirukkiṉṟatu. mēlum, kaṭavuḷ mutaṟ ṟāytantiyarkkut tanta pattuk kaṭṭaḷikaḷuḷ nī etaṉiyum kollātē eṉpatu mutaṟkaṭṭaḷiyākak kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.
iṉik kiṟittuvak koḷkiyi nili niṟuttiya ēcunātarum avar māṇākkarkaḷum ūṉuṇavu koḷḷātavarkaḷeṉpatu avarkaḷip paṟṟik kūṟum varalāṟukaḷāl naṉku viḷaṅkum. ēcunātar nāṟpatu nāḷ paṭṭiṉi kiṭantu tavam purintār eṉpati kiṟittuvak koḷki nūlkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. avaruṭiya māṇākkarkaḷil oruvarākiya mattēyu eṉpār vittukkaḷ, koṭṭikaḷ, kāykaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟiyē uṇṭārallatu ūṉuṇavu uṇṭilar eṉa kilemeṉ eṉpavar eḻutiyiruk
kiṉṟār. kiṟittuvariṉ tirukkōyilukku mutaṟṟalivarākiya jēm muṉivar entak kālattum ūṉuṇṭatē illi eṉṟu ījicippa eṉṉum carittira (varalāṟṟu) k kārarum, ākaṭiṉ muṉivararum kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. iṅṅaṉam ēcunātar tam māṇākkarkaḷē ūṉuṇavu koḷḷātavarkaḷāy iruntatu tam āciriyar avvaruḷ oḻukkattil mikkavarāy iruntami paṟṟiyēyām. aṟṟēl, ēcunātar tammiṭam vantirunta kuṭimakkaḷ paciyāl varuntiyatu kaṇṭu avarkaṭku mīṉiyum appattuṇṭu
kaḷiyum vāḻttik koṭuttāreṉṟu kūṟuvateṉiyeṉiṉ, akkālattilirunta yūtarkaḷil, aṟiviṟciṟanta cilarit tavira, maṟṟip potu makkaḷellārum ūṉuṇavu koṇṭu uyirvāḻntavarkaḷēyāvar. nāḷēṟa nāḷēṟa av yūtamakkaḷ uyirkkoliyilum, ataṉvaḻiyē varum pala tīviṉikaḷilum perukivantamiyiṉ, avarkaḷit tirutti nalvaḻippaṭuttum poruṭṭē ēcu iṟivaṉaruḷāl avarkaḷiṭiyē piṟan taruḷiṉār. avarkaḷiṭiyiṟ piṟantu vaḷarnta avar mutalil tammit tūyarākkutal vēṇṭiyē nāṟpatu nāḷ paṭṭiṉi kiṭantu tavam ceytār. attavam muṟṟup peṟṟapiṉ, avar avyūta makkaṭku nallaṟivu pukaṭṭa muṉvantār. vantavar avarkaḷuṭiya paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaṭku muṟṟum māṟāy niṉṟu, avarkaṭkuk kollāmi mutaliya uyarnta oḻukkaṅkaḷik kūṟap pukuntiruntāl, avar coṟkaḷi evaruñ cevi koṭuttuk kēṭṭirār. pākaṉuk kaṭaṅkātu kaṭivāḷatti yaṟuttuk koṇṭu caṭutiyil virintōṭum oru kutiriyi annērattil uṭaṉē piṭittu aṭakkap pukuntāl atu ciṟituṅ kikūṭātu. piṉ atu virintōṭum vaḻiyē viṭuttu atu taṉ mummuram aṭaṅki ōynta piṉ ataṉip piṭittu aṭakkik koḷḷutal eḷitu. atu pōlavē, ūṉuṇṭu koṭiya vilaṅkukaḷip pōl uyir vāḻnta av yūtarkaḷiṉ paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷukku iyintu niṟpavarip pōṟ kāṭṭi avarkaḷit tiruttuvatē ēcuviṉ tiruvuḷḷak karuttu. ātalāl tamatu nalluri kēṭka vanta avyūtarkku avarkaḷ paṭṭiṉiyā yiruttal uṇarntu appattuṇṭukaḷiyum mīṉtuṇṭukaḷiyum uṇavākak koṭuttaruḷiṉār. iṅṅaṉaṅ koṭuttaviṭattum māṭu, āṭu, kōḻi mutalāṉa uyarnta uyirkaḷik koṉṟu avaṟṟiṉ ūṉik koṭāmal avikaḷuḷḷum tāḻnta paṭiyil uḷḷa mīṉ iṟicciyiyē koṭuttār. aiyaṟivuṭiya ciṟṟuyirkaḷilum āṭu, māṭu, kutiri, paṉṟi, kōḻi mutaliya vaṟṟik kollum tīviṉiyiviṭa mīṉkaḷi valivīcip piṭikkum tīviṉi kuṟintatākaliṉ avarkaḷuṭiya vaḻakkaṅ kaḷuḷ tīviṉi kuṟinta ataṟku oruvāṟu ottu naṭappārāyiṉar. kikkum maruntuṇṇāta kuḻavikku irakkamuṭiya aṉṉi karuppaṅkaṭṭiyil am maruntip potintu vittu ūṭṭi ataṉnōy tīrttalpōla, iyēcuvum tam aṟavurikaḷākiya marunti yuṭkoḷḷamāṭṭāta yūtarkku avarkku iṉikkum avartam vaḻakkaṅkaḷākiya cilavaṟṟuṭaṉ cērttuk koṭuttu avarkaḷi nalvaḻippaṭuttuvārāyiṉar. mēlum, tamakku naṉmāṇākkarit tērnteṭukkiyil avarkaḷi mīṉpiṭikkum cempaṭavar kuṭiyiṉiṉṟum eṭuttatu eṉṉi yeṉṟu āḻntu ārāyntāl, kollāmi yaṟattil niṉṟa avar tiruvuḷḷak karuttu naṉku pulaṉām. mīṉpiṭikkum oruvaṉi at toḻiliṉiṉṟum nīkki nalvaḻippaṭuttiṉāl, ettaṉiyō mīṉkaḷ uyirpiḻikkum! am mīṉkaḷit tiṉṟu uyirvāḻum ettaṉiyō makkaḷ attīviṉiyiṉiṉṟu vilakip piḻippar. itu paṟṟiyē, ikkālattirunta irāmaliṅka cuvāmikaḷum cempaṭavar palariyum ūṉviṟpār palariyum civarāk kiṉār.
mēlum, ūṉviruppuṭiyārkkup paṭṭiṉi kiṭantu uṭampi vāṭṭit tavam puriyum maṉa orumippāṭu uṇṭākātu. atupaṟṟiyē auviyār nōṉpeṉpatu koṉṟu tiṉṉāmi eṉṟum,
 paṭikoṇṭār  neñcampōl  naṉṟūkkātu oṉṟaṉ
   uṭalcuvi yuṇṭār maṉam	(kuṟaḷ 253)
eṉṟu tiruvaḷḷuvaṉārum aruḷic ceytirukkiṉṟaṉar. ūṉuṇaviṉāl uṭampu aḷavukku miñcik koḻuppēṟi nōyi varuvittalōṭu aimpoṟikaḷi aṭakkit tavampuritaṟkum iṭiyūṟu payakkum. ākavē, tavampurivā rellārum ūṉuṇavi aṟavē oḻittavarāka iruttal vēṇṭumeṉpatu tiṇṇam. ēcunātar ciṟuvarāyirunta pōtu tam tāy tantiyarāl ūṉuṇavu ūṭṭappaṭṭavarā yirukkalām. aṅṅaṉam ūṉuṇavu uṇṭu vaḷarnta tamatuṭampu tavavoḻukkattiṟ kiciyātu eṉṟu kaṇṭē avvuṇavāl vanta naccunīriyum, miku koḻuppiyum puṟaṅkaḻittaṟ poruṭṭu avar nāṟpatu nāḷ paṭṭiṉikiṭantu nōṉpiyaṟṟiṉār. aṅṅaṉam nōṉpiyaṟṟiya piṉṉart tam vāḻnāḷ muṭiyumaṭṭum avar eṅkum ūṉuṇavu koṇṭilar. avar varalāṟṟiṉik kūṟum nūlkaḷum avar ūṉuṇṭāreṉak kūṟukiṉṟila.
iṉik kirēkkaruṭ paḻiyakālattil aṟivāṉ mikka cāṉṟōrākiya pittākora eṉpavarum, piḷēṭṭō eṉpavarum ūṉuṇṇā aṟattiṉkaṇ niṉṟatōṭu piṟarum aṅṅaṉamē ataṉik kaṭippiṭittoḻukumpaṭi aṟivuṟutti vantaṉar.
iṉi, uṉuṇavu koḷvārkku nuṇṇuṇarvum pēraṟivum illāmaṟ pōvatōṭu uṭampum poliviḻantu pōkiṉṟa teṉpatip paṇṭikkālattirunta mēṉmakkaḷ cilar uṇarntiruntaṉa reṉpatu kiṟittuvar maṟiyākiya viviliya nūlil, tāṉiyēl eṉṉum maṟiyiṟ collappaṭṭiruttal koṇṭu naṉkaṟikiṉṟōm. tāṉiyēl eṉṉum periyavarum, avaric cārnta cilarum tammi varuvitta ōr aracaṉ tanta ūṉuṇaviyum, koṭimuntiric cāṟṟiyum uṭkoṇṭu tammi mācupaṭuttik koḷvataṟku iciyārāyt tāmuṇṇu taṟku vittukkaḷiyum, parukutaṟkut tūya taṇṇīriyumē tarumpaṭi kēṭṭaṉareṉṟum, atukaṇṭa av aracaṉiṉ ēvalāṭkaḷ aracaṉ kaṭṭaḷikku māṟāka aṅṅaṉaṅ koṭuppiṉ, avarkaḷ uṭampu iḷittu mukam vāṭip pōvārkaḷeṉṟu añci varunti niṟka, ataṉiyuṇarnta ap periyavar, emakku vittuṇavum, aracaṉic cērntārkku ūṉuṇavuṅ koṭuttup, piṟaku emmiyum avariyum oppiṭṭu nōkkuka eṉṟu kūṟa, avarkaḷ aṅṅaṉamē ceytapiṟaku, ap periyavarum avaric cārntārum pēraṟivum, ellāk kali uṇarvum nirampik kaṉavu nikaḻccikaḷiyum, maṟiporuṭ ṭōṟṟaṅkaḷiyum kāṇum pulamiyit tiruvaruḷāl tarappeṟṟup perum polivuṭiyarāyt tikaḻntaṉareṉṟum, av varacaṉ ūṉuṇavu koḷvārāyt taṉṉiṭattuḷḷa maṟimoḻiyāḷarum (mantirakkārarum) kōḷ nūlārum (cōtiṭarum) ākiya ellāriṉum pārkka ap periyavarum, avaraṭiyārum aṟivāṟṟalkaḷil pattu maṭaṅku ciṟantavarāy viḷaṅkutal kaṇṭu viyantaṉaṉ eṉṟum tāṉiyēl maṟinūliṉ mutaliyalil (atikārattiṟ) collap paṭṭirukkiṉṟatu.
iṉi, makamatu matatti (koḷkiyi) nili niṟuttiya makamatu muṉivar arēpiyā nāṭṭiṟ kaṭavuḷaruḷāl tōṉṟiyapōtu, aṅkirunta makkaḷellārum mikak koṭiyavarkaḷāyp pala vilaṅkukaḷiyum, ciṟṟuyirkaḷiyum koṉṟu avaṟṟiṉ ūṉik kuruti oḻukoḻukat tiṉṉum iyalpiṉa rāyp pala perun tīviṉikaḷic ceytu uyir vāḻntu vantaṉar. ak koṭiyavarkaḷi ak kolit toḻil, pulit toḻilkaḷi ṉiṉṟum viṭuvittaṟ poruṭṭu makamatu muṉivar evvaḷavō muyaṉṟu pārttum muṭiyāmiyāl, iyaṉṟamaṭṭumāvatu avarkaḷavaṟṟiviṭat takkapaṭi, tāṉē yiṟantaṉavum, paṉṟiyiṉ iṟicciyum, kurutiyum, kaṭavuḷallāta piṟarkkeṉṟu varintu vikkappaṭṭaṉavum, kaḻuttu nerittuk kollap paṭṭaṉavum, maṇṭi uṭittuk kollap paṭṭaṉavum, kīḻē viḻuntu kuttappaṭṭaṉavum, uṅkaḷāṟ kollap paṭāmal ēṉi maṟa vilaṅkukaḷāl tiṉṉappaṭṭaṉavum uṅkaḷāl vilakkap paṭuvaṉavākuka eṉṟu kaṭṭaḷi tantār.
kaṭavuḷukku eṉṟu ēṟpiyāmal avarkaḷ ūṉuṇavu koḷḷalākāteṉṟu vilakkiṉatu kaṭavuḷiyum, avar pērirakkattōṭu ellā uyirkaḷukkum uṭampukaḷip paṭittuk koṭuttiruttaliyum avarkaḷ karuttūṉṟi niṉikka niṉikka uyirkaḷik koṉṟu avaṟṟiṉ iṟicciyit tiṉṉuṅ koṭuñ ceyalit tāmākavē viṭṭuviṭuvar eṉpatu karutiyēyākum. iṉik kaḻutti nerittum maṇṭiyi yuṭittuṅ kollappaṭuvaṉa, vēḷvik kaḷattil kaḻuttu nerittuk kollappaṭum yāṭṭiyum, māṭaṭikkuṅ koṭṭilkaḷiṟ cammaṭṭiyāl maṇṭi yuṭittuk kollappaṭum māṭṭiyuṅ kuṟikkiṉṟamiyāl, aṅṅaṉam mikak koṭumiyāka av yāṭumāṭukaḷik kolluṅ kolit toḻiliṉiṉṟum makamatiyari vilakkutaṟ poruṭṭākavē avvāṟu kollappaṭum avaṟṟiṉ ūṉit tiṉṉalākāteṉṟu makamatu muṉivar mēṟ kūṟiyavāṟu kaṭṭaḷiyiṭuvārāyiṉar. mēluṅ, korāṉ vētattiṉ iraṇṭām pākattil aimpatteṭṭāvatu aṟivuriyāṉatu piṉvarumāṟu kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu:
avaṟṟiṉ ūṉum irattamum enta vakiyāluṅ kaṭavuḷic ceṉṟu cēramāṭṭā. āṉāl, uṅkaḷiṭam uḷḷa pēraṉpu maṭṭumē avaric ceṉṟu cārum.
ivvuriyiṉāṟ ciṟṟuyirkaḷik koṉṟu kaṭavuḷukku ēṟpikkum ūṉuṅkurutiyuṅ kaṭavuḷāl ēṟkappaṭamāṭṭā eṉpatūum, avaṉaṭiyārkaḷ uyirk koliyiṉṟi meyyaṉpōṭu ceyyum vaḻipāṭu oṉṟumaṭṭumē avarāl ēṟṟukkoḷḷappaṭum eṉpatūum iṉitu aṟivikkap paṭukiṉṟaṉa allavō? mēluṅ, korāṉ mutaṟpākattiṉkaṇ uḷḷa eṇpatām aṟivuriyāṉatu piṉvarumāṟu kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu:
ataṉpiṉ āṭavaṉ taṉ uṇavi nōkkak kaṭavaṉāka yām maḻiyip poḻivikkiṉṟōm; ataṉpiṉ kīṟattakka nilattik kīṟukiṉṟōm; ataṉ kaṇ viḷiporuḷkaḷi viḷivikkiṉṟōm; ataṉ piṉ muntirik koṭiyiṉiyuṅ, kuḷakiṉiyum, oliva marattiṉiyum, pērīntiṉiyum, aṭarnta tōṭṭaṅkaḷiyum, kaṉikaḷiyum, kīrikaḷiyum uṉakkum uṉṉuṭiya āṭumāṭukaḷukkum uṇavukkākat tantiruk kiṉṟōm.
eṉṟu itaṉkaṇ ellām valla iṟivaṉ āṭavaṉip paṭittu, avaṉukku uṇavāvaṉa iṉṉaviyeṉṟu variyaṟuttuk kūṟukiṉṟuḻip, payirpaccikaḷāl varum uṇaviyē vakuttuk kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉarallāl, vēṟu ūṉuṇavic ciṟitum eṭutturiyāmi karuttiṟ patikkaṟpāṟṟu. iṅṅaṉamāka makamatiyarkkuc ciṟanta vētamāṉa korāṉil makkaḷukku iṟivaṉāl uṇavākak koṭukkappaṭṭavi; marañ ceṭi koṭikaḷiṉ payaṉākiya paḻamum vittuṅ kīriyum pūvuṅ kāyuṅkiḻaṅkukaḷumēyātal teṟṟeṉap pulaṉākiṉṟataṉṟō? eṉṟu itukāṟuṅ kūṟiyavāṟṟāl ulakattiṉ kaṇ toṉṟu toṭṭup paraviyirukkum yūtam, civam, pāracikam, camaṇam, puttam, kiṟituvam, kirēkkam, makamatiyam eṉṉuñ ciṟanta  camayaṅkaḷum, accamayaṅkaḷi nilināṭṭiya cāṉṟōr kaḷum, avarkaḷ aruḷic ceyta vētaṅkaḷum ākiya ellām ottu orumukamāy niṉṟu kolit toḻilāl varum ūṉuṇavi muṟṟum vilakkic civa uṇaviṉiyē vaṟpuṟuttik kūṟutal iṉitu viḷaṅkā niṟkum eṉpatu.
5. civa uṇavukku māṟāṉa koḷkikaḷiṉ maṟuppu
iṉṉum orucārār, uyirkaḷik koṉṟu avaṟṟiṉ ūṉi viṟpavarkaḷukkē pāvam uṇṭām; ataṉi vāṅki uṇpavarkkup pāvam iṉṟu eṉak kūṟā niṟpar. vilikku viṟkum ūṉi vāṅkuvār evarumē ilarāyiṉ, ataṉi viṟpārum ataṉ poruṭṭu uyirkaḷik kollārum ilarātal ciṟu makārum uṇarvarallarō? ivviyalpiṉi naṉku teḷiya vittaṟkē teyvap pulamit tiruvaḷḷuva nāyaṉār,
tiṉaṟporuṭṭāṟ kollā tulakeṉiṉ yārum
vilipporuṭṭāl ūṉtaruvār il	(kuṟaḷ 256)
eṉṟu aruḷic ceytār eṉka.
iṉi, makkaḷiṉ paṟkaḷamippu ūṉ uṇavu koḷḷutaṟku ēṟṟatāka iruttalāl, avarkaḷ ūṉ tiṉpatu kuṟṟamākā teṉpār koḷkiyiṉi ārāyvām; muṉ paṟkaḷiṉ ōramāy irupuṟattum makkaḷ vāyil amintuḷḷa nāṉku nāyppaṟkaḷuṅ kūriyaṉavāy iruttalāl, avi ūṉik kiḻittut tiṉpataṟkuttāṉ karuviyāka amikkappaṭṭaṉa veṉpatu avaratu karuttu. ūṉiyē tiṉṟu uyir vāḻum nāy pūṉi ōnāy puli ciṅkam mutaliya vilaṅkukaḷiṉ paṟkaḷellām muṟṟuṅ kūrmiyāy irukka, makkaḷukku maṭṭum nāṉku kūrm paṟkaḷ iruttal ēṉ eṉṟu avar ārāyntu pārppārāyiṉ, avi civa uṇavup paṇṭaṅkaḷilēyē, muntirik koṭṭi vātumik koṭṭi tēṅkāy mutalāṉa vaṉporuḷkaḷ iruttaliṉ, avaṟṟik kaṭittu naṟukkit tiṉpataṟkuk karuvikaḷākavē annāṉku kūriya nāyp paṟkaḷum makkaḷ vāyiṉuḷ amikkappaṭalāyiṉa eṉṉum uṇmi viḷaṅkā niṟkum. makkaḷ ūṉuṇavu koḷḷutaṟkē piṟantavarkaḷāyiṉ, avarkaḷiṉ vāyil taṭṭippaṟkaḷē illāmal ellām nāyppaṟkaḷākavē yallavō amikkappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum? perumpālum makkaḷ vaṭiviṉi yottirukkum vālillāk kuraṅkukaḷum, peruṅkuraṅkukaḷuṅ, kāṭṭu maṉitaṉuṅkūṭa makkaḷukkuḷḷa taṭṭippaṟkaḷum ōrattiluḷḷa nāṉku nāyppaṟkaḷum uṭiyaṉa vāyiruntum, avi oru ciṟitum iṟicci tiṉṉāmaṟ kāy kaṉi kiḻaṅkukaḷ koṭṭikaḷ mutalāṉa civa uṇavup paṇṭaṅkaḷiyē tiṉṉak kāṇ kiṉṟōm. ākavē, makkaḷukkuḷḷa kūriya nāyppaṟkaḷ nāṉkum, marakkaṟiyuṇavilēyē vaṉpaṇṭaṅkaḷāy iruppavikaḷik kaṭittup piḷantu tiṉpataṟkāka vakuttu amikkappaṭṭaṉavē yallāmal, maṟṟic ciṟṟuyirkaḷik koṉṟu avaṟṟiṉ taciyi uṇpataṟkāka amikkappaṭṭaṉa allaveṉpatu naṉku pulaṉākiṉṟataṉṟō?
mēlum marakkaṟiyuṇavi uṭkoḷkiṉṟavarkaḷukkup paṟkaḷ ciṟitum paḻutupaṭāmal veṇmiyāy aḻakuṟṟu viḷaṅka, ūṉtiṉpavarkaḷukkuḷḷa paṟkaḷō palvaki nōykaḷ koṇṭu avartam paṟkaḷip paḻutupaṭuttak kāṇkiṉṟō mākaliṉ, makkaḷiṉ pallamippup pulāl tiṉpataṟkuc ciṟitum ēṟṟataṉṟeṉpatu naṉku uṇarappaṭum. aṅṅaṉamāyiṉ, civarāyirup pārilum palar palnōyāl tuṉpuṟak kāṇkiṉ ṟōmēyeṉiṉ; marakkaṟi yuṇavup poruḷkaḷilum nañcu uḷḷa kāppik koṭṭi tēyili kōkkō cuṇṇāmpu pukiyili mutaliyavikaḷic civarum pārppaṉarum mikutiyāy uṭkoḷḷak kāṇṭalāṉum, maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷil uṇavu camittal iḻiveṉak karutit turu kaḷimpu mutalāṉa koṭunañcu vāynta irumpu īyam cempu pittaḷi alumīṉiyam mutalāṉa tātup poruḷkaḷāl ākkiya kalaṅkaḷil avarkaḷ tamatu civavuṇavic camittu ataṉi nañcākka kāṇṭalāṉum, nōy koṇṭa mātariṉ cērkiyāṟ koṟukku, ki kāṟ piṭippu mutalāṉa naccuppiṇikaḷāṟ paṟṟappaṭṭu avarkaḷ irattam nañcākit tuṉpuṟak kāṇṭalāṉum civariṟ cilar palarum pārppāriṟ cilar palarum avvāṟu palnōy koḷkiṉṟāreṉka. eṉavē, palnōy varuvittaṟkuriya ētukkaḷ palavaṟṟiṟ, pulāl uṇpatu mutaṉmipeṟṟa toṉṟākum eṉṟu uṇarntu koḷka. airōppiya nāṭṭiluḷḷa veḷḷik kārar perumpālum pulāluṇavu koḷpavarkaḷāy iruttaliṉālēyē, ammakkaḷellārum palnōyāṟ peritun tuṉpuṟukiṉṟārkaḷ. annāṭṭiṟ palnōy paravi yiruttal pōl innāṭṭil annōy paravāmi, iṅkuḷḷār perumpālum marakkaṟiyuṇavu uṭkoḷpavarkaḷāy iruttaliṉālēyām eṉka.
mēlum, pukaḻpeṟṟa maruttu nūlāciriyarākiya hēk (Dr.A. Haig) eṉpavar civiṅkippuli, vālillāk kuraṅkukaḷiṉ vāyilum makkaḷ vāyilum aminta paṟkaḷit taṉittaṉiyē meḻukiṟ patiyavittu oppiṭṭu nōkka, ūṉtiṉṉum puliyiṉ paṟkaḷ kūrṅ kattiyiṉ muṉipōṉṟa pativum, paḻantiṉṉuṅ kuraṅkukaḷ makkaḷiṉ paṟkaḷ kūrmiyiṉṟi otta pativum uṭiyaṉavāy vēṟupaṭṭiruttalik kaṇṭār. atuvēyumaṉṟi ūṉuṇavu koḷḷāta vilaṅkukaṭkuk kālkaḷiṟ kuḷampukaḷ iruppa, ūṉuṇavu koḷpavaṟṟiṟkō avaṟṟiṟ kūriya nakaṅkaḷ amintirukkiṉṟaṉa. makkaḷukkō kūriya nakaṅkaḷuṅ kūriya paṟkaḷum illāmi karuttiṟ patikkaṟ pāṟṟu. 1kuviyar (Cuvier) eṉṉum āciriyarit talivarāyk koṇṭa maruttu nūlaṟiñar attaṉi peyaruntāntām ārāyntaṟinta ārāyccikaḷiṉ muṭipāka, ottu orumukamāy niṉṟu, makkaḷuṭampiṉ amippāṉatu - pallamippum uṭpaṭap paḻavakikaḷi uṇavākak koḷvataṟku ēṟṟatāvatu tiṇṇam eṉṟu uri nāṭṭukiṉṟār eṉa maruttunūṟ perum pulavarāṉa ṭrāl eṉpavarum eṭuttuk kūṟiṉār.
iṅṅaṉaṅ kāykaṟiyuṇavu koḷḷavēṇṭiya makkaḷ, ataṉiviṭuttu, naccunīrum, nāṟuṅkaḻivukaḷum, nōykaḷum, aḻukkukaḷum, aḻukalum niṟinta ellā vakiyāṉa iṟiccikaḷiyun tiṉkiṉṟaṉar. ivvāṟu tiṉpataṉāl avarkaḷuṭiya uṭampu nāṟi nalaṅkuliya uṭampiṉ uḷḷē aminta irattakkuḻāykaḷ av aḻukaṟcāṟukaḷāl aṭi paṭṭup pōka, uṭaṉē avarkaḷ vayiṟu kumaṭṭik kakkiyun tuppiyum caḷikaḷi umiḻntuṅ, kaṭuṅkāyccalum, vīkkamum vayiṟṟuvaliyum narampu valippuṅ koṇṭun tuṉpuṟṟut tuṭituṭikkiṉṟaṉar. āṉāl, ivvāṟu tuṉpuṟalāṉatu etaṉāl eṉṟaṟiyāmal tikittu, uṭaṉē maruttuvarit tampāl varuvikkiṉṟaṉar. am maruttuvarum iḵtu etaṉāl vantateṉṟa ṟiyāmal avarun tikikkiṉṟaṉar. maṟṟu, uṇmi yuṇarntavarkaḷō ivvaḷavu tuṉpamum ūṉ uṇṭamiyāl vantateṉpataṉi naṉkuṇarvar. kāykaṟip paṇṭaṅkaḷit tukaḷāka arittuk koṭukkun taṭṭip paṟkaḷ vāynta makkaḷ, ak kaṉikāy kiḻaṅkukaḷ vittukkaḷiyē uṭkoḷvārkaḷāyiṉ, avi appaṟkaḷāl naṉṟāy arikkappaṭṭut tīṉip piyiṟ ceṉṟavuṭaṉē eḷitiṟ cerittu, irattattiṟ kalantu uṭampi nallaniliyil vikkum; maṟṟu avviyaṟkikku māṟākap palvaki iṟiccikaḷiyun tiṉparāyiṉ, avartam paṟkaḷ avaṟṟic cevvaṉē kiḻittu nīrppatamākka māṭṭāmiyiṉ, avviṟicci ciṟu ciṟu tuṇṭukaḷāka vayiṟṟiṉuṭ ceṉṟu cerimāṉamākāmal niṉṟu mēṟkūriya koṭu nōykaḷi yellām varuvikkiṉṟaṉaveṉṟu mēṟkāṭṭiya ṭrāl eṉṉum āciriyarē ivvuṇmi nikaḻccikaḷi naṉkeṭuttu viḷakkiṉār. ākavē, makkaḷiṉ pallamippuc civavuṇavu koḷḷutaṟkē ēṟṟateṉpatu teṟṟeṉa viḷaṅkum. ivvuṇmiyiṉi mēlnāṭṭāciriyarip pōl naṉku ārāyntu pārkkamāṭṭātār, makkaḷiṉ pallamippup pulāluṇavu koḷḷutaṟkē ēṟṟateṉap pakuttaṟiviṉṟiyurittut tām maṟavilaṅkukaḷukku iṉamuṭiya rātalik kāṭṭuvarātaliṉ, avartam pulliya veḷḷaṟivuri yiṉi nampi evarum pulāluṇavu koḷḷun tīviṉip paṭukuḻiyil vīḻātirupparāka!
iṉip pakalavaṉ veppam mikunta nāṭukaḷil uḷḷārkkup pulāluṇavu vēṇṭappaṭātāyiṉum paṉiyuṅkuḷirum mikunta vaṭamuṉi teṉmuṉi kaḷiyaṭutta nāṭukaḷil uḷḷārkkup pulāluṇavu iṉṟiyamiyātatākum eṉṟu muḻakkam iṭuvār kūṟṟiṉic ciṟitu ārāyvām: innilavulakattiṟ puṟpūṇṭu marañ ceṭi koṭikaḷ mutalāṉa ōraṟivuṭiya uyirkaḷ mutal iraṇṭu mutal aintaṟivuṭiya ūrvaṉa nakarvaṉa tirivaṉa paṟappaṉavākiya ciṟṟuyirkaḷum āṟaṟivuṭiya makkaḷum mutaṉmutaṟ ṟōṉṟiya iṭam, innilattiṉ naṭu miyattic cārnta it teṉṉāṭṭi yaṭuttut teṟkuvaṭakkāyc ceṉṟa kumari nāṭēyākumeṉṟu mēṉāṭṭu iyaṟki nūl āciriyarkaḷ muṭivukaṭṭic colliyirukkiṉṟārkaḷ.2 ak kumari nāṭō ippōtu teṉkaṭaluḷ mūḻkik kiṭakkiṉṟatu. annāṭu kaṭalāṟ kavarappaṭṭa pallāyira āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉē tāṉ, annāṭṭil uyirvāḻnta vilaṅkukaḷum makkaḷum pala mukamāyp pirintu  vaṭakku vaṭamēṟku vaṭakiḻakkil uḷḷa nilaṅkaḷiṟ ceṉṟu kuṭiyēṟiyatākum. kumari nāṭṭil iruntavariyil ellā uyirkaḷum marañceṭi koṭikaḷiṉ payaṉāṉa ili pū kāy kaṉi kiḻaṅku vittukkaḷ eṉṉum ivaṟṟiyē uṇavākak koṇṭu uyirvāḻntaṉa. ēṉeṉṟāṟ, cūṭumikunta annāṭṭilum ataṉi yaṭuttuḷḷa piṟanāṭukaḷilum mēṟkūṟiya civa uṇavup paṇṭaṅkaḷ ērāḷamāyk kiṭittaliṉ annāṭṭi viṭṭup paṉiyuṅkuḷirum mikunta vaṭanāṭukaḷukkuc ceṉṟa uyirkaḷō, aṅkē puṟpūṇṭukaḷum illāmal ellām veṟunilamāka iruntamiyiṉ, tamatu koṭiya pacit tuṉpam poṟukkamāṭṭāmal oṉṟi yoṉṟu piṭittuk koṉṟu tiṉpavāyiṉa. maṟṟuk kumari nāṭṭi viṭṭuc ceṉṟa uyirkaḷuḷḷum nuṇṇaṟivu vāyntavikaḷ, kaṭuṅkuḷir mikunta vaṭa nāṭukaḷi nōkkic cellāmal, teṉpakutik kaṇṇuḷḷa maṟṟanāṭukaḷilēyē kuṭiyēṟic civa uṇavup paṇṭaṅkaḷiyē tiṉṟu uyir vāḻalāyiṉa. vaṭamuṉi nōkkic ceṉṟa uyirkaḷ ap pakkaṅkaḷil marakkaṟiyuṇavu kiṭikkap peṟāmiyiṉ oṉṟiyoṉṟu piṭittut tiṉṟu ūṉuṇavu koḷḷak kaṟṟukkoṇṭa piṉ, periya uyirkaḷāyiruntavi ciṟiya uyirkaḷik koṉṟu tiṉṟu cilakālam variyil tamatu koṭum paciyi āṟṟi vantaṉa. acciṟiya vuyirkaḷum nāḷaṭivil aṅṅaṉam aḻikkappaṭṭu illiyāy oḻintapiṉ, ūṉuṇaviṟ paḻakiya maṟṟip periya uyirkaḷiṟ perumpālāṉa mīṇṭun teṉpakutikkaṇ uḷḷa kāṭukaḷi nōkki varalāyiṉa. vantu iṅkuḷḷa kāṭukaḷil ērāḷamāyk kiṭikkum māṉ mari kaṭampi paṉṟi mutalāṉa melvilaṅkukaḷi iriyākak koṉṟutiṉṟu, ūṉ uṇṇum paḻakkatti viṭāvāy ataṉkaṇ nili peṟṟaṉa. ākavē vaṭamuṉi nōkkicceṉṟu āṅkuc cilakālam viki, mīṇṭun teṉpakutiyiṟ pukuntu kuṭiyēṟiya valvilaṅkukaḷālum nākarikam illā makkaḷālumē ūṉuṇavu paravalāyiṟṟeṉka.ivvāṟṟāl vaṭamuṉi nōkkic ceṉṟa ciṟṟuyirkaḷum nākarika aṟivillā makkaḷumē tām ūṉuṇavu koḷḷuṅ koṭuntīviṉiyi mutaṉmutaṟ kaṟṟuk koṇ ṭārkaḷ eṉpatum ataṟkumuṉ innilattiṉ naṭuvariyic cārnta ellā iṭaṅkaḷilum irunta ellā uyirkaḷum marakkaṟiyuṇaviṉiyē uṭkoṇṭu uyir vāḻntaṉa eṉpatum naṉku peṟappaṭṭaṉa.
iṉip, paṇṭināḷil vaṭamuṉiyi nōkkic ceṉṟa makkaḷ aṟivum nākarikamum illātavarkaḷākaliṉ, tām ceṉṟa an nilaṅkaḷiṟ kāy kaṉi kiḻaṅkukaḷ kiṭiyāmi kaṇṭu, ikkālattiṟ pōla avi tammip piṟanāṭukaḷiliruntu varuvittuk koḷḷum vakiyaṟiyārāyt, tammiṉun tāḻnta vilaṅkukaḷik koṉṟuñ, cila vēḷikaḷil tammilēyē 
oruvari yoruvar koṉṟum avvaḻiyāṟpeṟṟa iṟicciyi uṇavākak koḷḷa vēṇṭiyavarāṉārkaḷ. akkālattirunta aṟivillā ammakkaṭku ap paṉināṭukaḷiṟ pulāluṇavu iṉṟiyamiyātatāy iruntatu koṇṭu, nīrāvip poṟikaḷiṉ utaviyāl nākarikamum aṟivum nāḷukkunāḷ viñcum ikkālattil ap paṉi nāṭukaḷil iruppārkkum pulāluṇavu iṉṟiyamiyātatu eṉpār aṟivillāp pētimākkaḷē yaṉṟi, aṟivuṭiya mēṉmakkaḷ aṅṅaṉañ collār. oṉṟukkoṉṟu āṟāyiram nāḻikivaḻi vilakikkiṭakkum nāṭukaḷiluḷḷār ellām mika arukiliruppāri oppat tattam nāṭukaḷiluḷḷa viḷiporuḷkaḷi māṟṟik koṇṭu ūṉ ciṟitum uṇṇāmaṟ civavuṇaviṉālēyē eḷitāy iṉitu vāḻtaṟkāṉa icivukaḷ naṉku vāyttirukkum innāḷilum paṉi nāṭukaḷil uḷḷārkkup pulāluṇavu iṉṟiyami yātatākumō? aṟiñarkaḷē kūṟuṅkaḷ!
aṟṟaṉṟu. paṉi nāṭukaḷ kuḷir mikuntiruttalāl, aṅkuḷḷa makkaḷiṉ uṭampukaḷil vēṇṭiya aḷavukkuc cūṭuṇṭākkum uṇavē vēṇṭi yiruttalālum, aṅṅaṉam uṭampiṟ cūṭu piṟappippatu pulāluṇavē yallāmal marakkaṟiyuṇavu allāmi yālum avarkaṭku ūṉuṇavē ciṟantatāmeṉiṉ, atu poruntātu. uṭampiṟku āṟṟaliyuñ cūṭṭiyun tarutaṟkuriya pacimāvuñ carkkariyuṅ koḻuppuñ civa uṇavup paṇṭaṅkaḷilēyē mikuntiruttali, in nūliṉ tuvakkattilēyē naṉkeṭuttuk kāṭṭi viḷakkiyirukkiṉṟēm. tayir, pāl, nīr, kōtumi, uḻuntu, veṇṇey, ney, eḷney, kaṟkaṇṭu, cīṉi mutalāṉa uṇavup poruḷkaḷic cerikkum aḷavaṟintu uṇpavarkaḷukku, ettakiya muyaṟciyuñ ceyyakkūṭiya valivuñ cūṭun takkapaṭi uṇṭākum. ivaṟṟiṉ poruṭṭuc ciṟṟuyirkaḷiṉ iṟicciyi yuṇṇavēṇṭu meṉṟurippōr, uṭampukaḷiṉ kūṟukaḷi vaḷarkkum uṇavup paṇṭaṅkaḷ iṉṉaviyeṉṟu ārāyntu aṟiya māṭṭātavarāvar. ciṟṟuyirkaḷiṉ uṭampiluḷḷa carkkariyuṅ koḻuppum avvuyirkaḷāṟ pala muyaṟcikaḷiṉ poruṭṭup payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭavikaḷē yātalāl, avaṟṟiṉ ūṉiluḷḷa avi cāṟukaḻinta cakkikaḷē yākum; ac cakkikaḷi uṭkoḷvārkku āṟṟaluñ cūṭum eṅṅaṉam varum? maṟṟup, puṟpūṇṭu marañ ceṭi koṭikaḷiṉ payaṉāṉa ili, pū, kāy kaṉi kiḻaṅku vittukkaḷil uḷḷa carkkariyuṅ koḻuppumō ettakiya muyaṟciyiṉ poruṭṭum payaṉpaṭuttappaṭātaṉa ākum. ākavē, avi makkaḷuṭampil āṟṟaliyuñ cūṭṭiyum uṇṭākkutaṟku mutaṟṟaramāvaṉa vākum. ivvaḷavu ciṟanta ic civavuṇaviviṭṭu iraṇṭān taramāṉa ūṉ cakkikaḷē kuḷir nāṭukaḷil uḷḷārkku valiviyuñ cūṭṭiyun tarumeṉṟu accakkiyip piṭittuk kūṟuvār aṟivu veṟuñ cakkiyē yākumallāṟ cāṟuṭiyatākumō? kūrntu pārmiṉkaḷ aṟiñarkaḷē!
iṉip, paṉināṭukaḷil uḷḷārellām pulāluṇavē koḷkiṉṟā reṉpatum uṇmiyuṇarātār veṟṟuriyām. nammi yāḷum āṅkila naṉmakkaḷ vāḻum iṅkilāntu tēyattiṟku vaṭakkiṉkaṇ uḷḷatāṉa kāṭlāntu tēyam paṉiyuṅ kuḷirum mikkateṉpatu nila nūlaṟiñarum paḷḷikkūṭac ciṟuvaruṅkūṭa naṉkuṇarvar. annāṭṭiluḷḷa makkaḷ oṭ ṭariciyiṟ ceyta appamum uruḷikkiḻaṅkum pālum veṇṇeyumē uṇavākak koḷḷukiṉṟārkaḷ. ivarkaḷiṉ vāḻkki niliyik kaṇṭuṇarnta āṭam mit (Adam smith) eṉṉum poruḷ nūl āciriyar, kacāppuk kaṭikkāraṉ tarum iṟicci ciṟitum illāmalē, vittukkaḷuṅ kāykaṟikaḷum, pāl veṇṇey pāṟkaṭṭi eṇṇey mutaliyaṉavuñ cērttu mikavuñ ceḻumiyāṉa, mikavum nalantaruvatāṉa, mikavum uṭampi vaḷarppatāṉa, mikavum uṭampit tēṟṟuvatāṉa nalluṇavākumeṉpatu nām paḻakkattāṟ kaṇṭaṟintatākum eṉa moḻintirukkiṉṟār. airōppā amerikkā eṉṉun tēyaṅkaḷiṉ ūṭu ceṉṟu āṅkāṅku makkaḷ vāḻkkiyiṉ iyalpukaḷi naṉku kaṇṭaṟinta avvarṭ mālkam (Rev Howard Malcolm) eṉṉum kiṟittu camaya kuru yāṉ kaṇṭa mika nērttiyāṉa makkaḷuṭampiṉ amippukaḷil mikac ciṟantatu ayarlāntu tēyattiluḷḷa makkaḷiṉ uṭampēyākum; immakkaḷ orukālum ūṉuṇavic cuvittatēyilli eṉa moḻintirukkiṉṟār.3 ivvāṟu kuḷirum paṉikkaṭṭiyum niṟinta kāṭlāntu, ayarlāntu mutalāṉa nāṭukaḷiluḷḷa naṉmakkaḷ palaruñ civavuṇavē uṭkoṇṭu uṭampiṟ cūṭuñ curucuruppum uṭiyarāy iṉitu uyirvāḻntuvaral kaṇkūṭāy aṟiyappaṭṭiruttaliṉ, paṉi nāṭukaḷil uḷḷārkku ūṉuṇavu iṉṟiyamiyātatu eṉṟu urippār uṇmi yuṇarn tārāvarō? ciṟitum ākāreṉa viṭukka.
iṉi, nām uṇṇum uṇavilum parukum nīrilum eṇṇiṟanta ciṟṟuyirkaḷ nam kaṇkaḷukkup pulaṉākāmal irukkiṉṟaṉa; perukku kaṇṇāṭi (Microscope) yiṉutavi koṇṭu nōkkuvāmāyiṉ, avi ettaṉi kōṭik kaṇakkāy nam uṇavup poruḷkaḷilum pālilum nīrilum ēṉiyavaṟṟilum niṟintirukkiṉṟaṉa eṉpati aṟiyalām; nām uḷḷiḻukkum mūccilum ettaṉiyō kōṭikkaṇakkāṉa ciṟṟuyirkaḷ cērntu nammuṭampiṉ uṭcellukiṉṟaṉa; iṅṅaṉam ovvoru noṭiyum nām eṇṇiṟanta uyirkaḷik koliceypavarkaḷāy irukkiyil, iṟicci tiṉpatu oṉṟutāṉā kolik kuṟṟamākum? eṉṟu cilar viṉāvukiṉṟārkaḷ. namatu uyir vāḻkkiyil nammāl vilakkakkūṭiya nikaḻccikaḷum nammāl vilakkakkūṭāta nikaḻccikaḷum eṉa iru pakuppukaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. nammāl vilakkakkūṭiya nikaḻccikaḷiṟ kuṟṟamāvaṉa iruntāṟ pakuttaṟivuṭiya nām avaṟṟi vilakki yoḻukak kaṭamip
paṭṭirukkiṉṟōm. nām ceyyum oru toḻil piṟavuyirkkut tuṉpan taruvateṉṟum, ataṉi vilakki yoḻukuvatu avvuyirkkum namakkum naṉmiyiyē tarumeṉṟum, ataṉi aṅṅaṉam vilakkiyoḻukuvatu nammāl eḷitiṟ ceyyakkūṭiyatē yāmeṉṟum nām naṉkaṟintirukkiyil, nām aṟinta avvaṇṇam naṭavāmāyiṉ, atu nampāṟ peruṅkuṟṟamē yātal tiṇṇamaṉṟō? oruvariṭattuḷḷa oru kuṟṟattiyeṭuttu maṟṟoruvariṭattu oruvaṉ pēcuvati nām nāṭōṟum kāṇkiṉṟōm. iṅṅaṉam avaṉ pēcuvatāl avarip piṟar iḻivāka eṇṇa ataṉāl avar uḷam varuntukiṉṟār; ākavē, puṟaṅkūṟutalāṉatu piṟarkkut tuṉpan taruvateṉpatip puṟaṅkūṟuvōṉ naṉkaṟintiruk kiṉṟāṉ; puṟaṅkūṟu vōṉi makkaḷ ellārum aruvaruttuviṭutalāṟ, puṟaṅkūṟutal taṉakkut tīṅkutaruvateṉpati avaṉum uṇarkiṉṟāṉ; ap puṟaṅkūṟutali viṭṭup piṟari uyarttup pēci ellārkkum iṉiyaṉāy oḻukutalē taṉakkum piṟarkkum naṉmiyit taruvateṉpatum, avvāṟu nallavaṉāy naṭappatu eḷitiṟ ceyyak kūṭiyatēyāmeṉpatum avaṉ cevvaṉē aṟintum, avaṉ aṅṅaṉam aṟintapaṭiyē naṭavāṉāyiṉ, aḵtu avaṉmēṟ peruṅ kuṟṟattic cumattumaṉṟō? oruvarmēṟ piṟaroruvar puṟaṅkūṟuvatu vaḻakkamāyiruttalāṟ, puṟaṅkūṟuñ ceyal kuṟṟamākāteṉa evarēṉuṅ kūṟat tuṇivarō? tuṇiyāraṉṟē. atupōlavē, āṭu māṭu kōḻi kuruvi kokku kuyil mīṉ mutalāṉa ciṟṟuyirkaḷi avaṟṟiṉ iṟiccikkāka makkaḷ koli ceykiyil, avi evvaḷavu tuṭituṭittut tuṉpuṟukiṉṟaṉa! aṅṅaṉam avi tuṉpuṟak koṉṟu peṟṟut tiṉṉum iṟicciyāvatu taṉṉit tiṉpārkku nalantarukiṉṟatā? atuvumilliyē! iṟicci tiṉpārkku atu vayiṟṟuvali, malaccikkal, aḷavukku miñciya cati, irattakkuḻāykaḷiṟ koḻuppaṭittal, kikāṟpiṭippu, irumal, elumpurukki, kāmāli, piḷavikkaṭṭi, nīriḻivu, koṟukku, paṭi, kaṭuṅ kāyccal, perumpāṭu mutalāka eṇṇat toliyāta nōykaḷi varici variciyāyk koṭuttu avari vakimāḷac ceykiṉṟataṉṟō! iṅṅaṉamākat tāmum aḷavillāt tuṉpattiyaṭintu mēṟ kāṭṭiya ciṟṟuyirkaṭkun tuṉpattit tarutaṟku ētuvāṉa uyirkkoliyiṉ tīmiyi naṉku aṟintu vittum, atu vaḻakkamāka uḷḷatutāṉē eṉṟu pakuttaṟi villā vilaṅkukaḷip pōṟ pakuttaṟivuṭiya makkaḷuñ ceyyalāmō? ittakiya perum pollāṅkāṉa uyirkkoliyiyum pulāluṇaviyum aṟavē nīkki, marañ ceṭi koṭikaḷiṉ payaṉāṉa civa uṇavup paṇṭaṅkaḷi uṭkoṇṭu uyirvāḻtalāṟ piṟa vuyirkkum makkaḷākiya tamakkum evvaḷavu naṉmiyuṇṭām! mēlum, pulāluṇavi nīkkuvatu varuttamāṉa ceyalum aṉṟu; aḵtu eḷitiṉ muṭivatēyām. ēṉeṉṟāl, civa uṇavup paṇṭaṅkaḷik kalavāmal iṟicciyi maṭṭum vilaṅkukaḷip pōl tiṉpatu makkaḷāṟ ciṟitum ēlātu; āṉāl, iṟicciyillāmaṟ civa uṇavup paṇṭaṅkaḷi maṭṭum arunti iṉitu uyirvāḻtalō makkaṭku eḷitilē icivatākum; ataṟkāka mikunta poruṭcelavu ceyyavēṇṭuvatu milli. ivvāṟu eḷitiṟ kiṭippatum, piṟavuyirkkut tīṅkillātatun, taṉṉi uṇpārkku mikka uṭal nala maṉanalaṅkaḷit taruvatum āṉa civavuṇavi viṭuttu, aintaṟivu uyirkaḷit tuṭituṭikka kolluṅ kolit toḻilāl varum pulāluṇavi uṭkoḷḷutal pakuttaṟivuṭiya makkaḷukkut takutiyāmō?
nām uṭkoḷḷuñ civa uṇavup paṇṭaṅkaḷilum nīriluṅ kaṇakkaṟṟa ciṟṟuyirkaḷ nam kaṭpulaṉukkut teṉpaṭāmaliruntāl, ataṟku nām eṉ ceyyalām! nam maṉamārat terintavikaḷiyē nām kollāmal vilakki yoḻukak kaṭamippaṭṭirukkiṉṟōm. namakkut teriyātavikaḷip paṟṟi, nammāl vilakkak kūṭātavikaḷip paṟṟik kavaliyeṭuttalāl yātu payaṉuṇṭu? evarun tām aṟinta, tammāl vilakkak kūṭiya tīmikaḷit tām tām vilakkalām; tammāl aṟiyappaṭāmalum, aṅṅaṉam aṟiyap paṭāmiyāl tammāl vilakkak kūṭāmalum varum naṉmi tīmikaḷukku evarum poṟuppāḷiyākamāṭṭār. tamakkut teriyāmalun tammāl virumpappaṭāmalun tam vayiṟṟiṉuḷḷē eṇṇiṟanta ciṟṟuyirkaḷ nuḻivatu koṇṭu, āṭu māṭu mutalāṉa ciṟanta payaṉuḷḷa vilaṅkukaḷit tām vēṇṭiyapaṭi cennīr oḻukak koṉṟuṅ kolluvittum neñcāra avaṟṟiṉ ūṉi uṇpatu kuṟṟamākāteṉa enta aṟiñaṉēṉuṅ kūṟat tuṇivaṉō? marañ ceṭi koṭikaḷ puṟpūṇṭukaḷum uyir uṭiyaṉa veṉpatu uṇmiyē; āṉāl, iv ōraṟivuṭiya uyirkaḷi nām uṇavākak koḷvatu kuṟṟamākātatu koṇṭu, aintaṟivuṭiya āṭu māṭu mīṉ mutaliyavikaḷi uṇavākak koḷvatuṅ kuṟṟamākātu eṉṉalāmō? āṭu māṭu mīṉ mutaliyavikaḷi uṇṇātu nām uyirvāḻalām, āṉāl puṟpūṇṭukaḷiyum avaṟṟiṉ payaṉkaḷiyum uṇṇātu nām uyirvāḻtal kūṭumā? āṭu māṭu mutaliyavikaḷip piṟar kollap pukumpoḻutu, avi tam uyir tappippiḻikka evvaḷavō muyaṉṟu tuṭituṭikkiṉṟaṉa! atupōṟ puṟpūṇṭu kaḷiyum avaṟṟiṉ payaṉkaḷiyum nām kik koḷkiyil, avikaḷum tam uyir tappa muyaṉṟu ōṭukiṉṟaṉavā? ciṟitum illiyē. āṭu māṭu mīṉ mutaliyavikaḷik koṉṟu kikkoḷḷum iṟicci, irattam oḻukit tīya muṭināṟṟam vīcuvatupōṟ puṟpūṇṭu mutaliyavi kaḷum avaṟṟiṉ payaṉum irattañ cinti nāṟak kaṇṭatuṇṭō? īraṟivu mutal aintaṟivukāṟumuḷḷa ellāc ciṟṟuyirkaḷiṉ iṟicciyum irattamum muṭi nāṟṟamum vīci aruvarukkat takkatāyirukka, ōraṟivuḷḷa puṟpūṇṭukaḷum avaṟṟiṉ payaṉāṉa ili pū kāy kaṉi kiḻaṅku vittu mutaliyaṉavum evvaḷavu naṟumaṇaṅ kamaḻntu nāvukkuc cuviyum uṭampukku nalamum uḷḷattiṟku makiḻcciyun tarukiṉṟaṉa! ākavē, ittaṉi vēṟupāṭumuṭiya ūṉiyuṅ kāykaṟiyuṇaviṉiyum oṉṟeṉṟal pētimiyuṅ koṭumi yum irakkamaṟṟa vaṉṉeñcamum uṭiyōr pulluriyēyātal teṟṟeṉa viḷaṅkāniṟkum.
mēlum, nammāl vilakka muṭiyāta cila ciṟu kuṟṟaṅkaḷ nammiṭam iruppatu paṟṟi, nammāl vilakkak kūṭiya peruṅkuṟṟaṅkaḷiyum nām ceyyalām eṉṟu cerukkuṭaṉ kūṟuvār tam maṭami peritum añcaṟpālatām. evvaḷavu naṟumaṇaṅkamaḻuñ cuvippaṇṭaṅkaḷi nām uṇṭālum, aviyellām nam vayiṟṟiṉ kīḻ aruvarukkattakka malamāy māṟiviṭukiṉṟaṉa; ivvāṟuṇṭākum malam namatu malakkuṭalil illāta nēramē illi. iṅṅaṉam makkaḷatu aṭivayiṟṟiṉuḷḷēyē malam iruttal koṇṭu makkaḷellōrum malattiyē tiraḷitiraḷiyāka eṭuttuc cāppiṭalām eṉṟu kūṟa oruvar muṉvaruvarāyiṉ avaratu aṟiviyum avariyum ulakattār eṉṉeṉṟu koḷvar! avari aṟivu tirinta veṟiyareṉa ikaḻntu nakipparallarō! ettaṉi muṟi kuḷittālum namatuṭampil aḻukku iruttalāl, nām kuḷittalē vēṇṭāmeṉak kūṟalāmō? aṅṅaṉamē, nām ettaṉi muṟi ettaṉi nūlkaḷik kaṟṟālum, nām avaṟṟiṉ coṟporuḷkaḷi maṟantuviṭutalāl, nām kalvikaṟṟalē vēṇṭāmeṉak kariyalāmō? iṅṅaṉamellām nammaṉō riṭattil vilakkamuṭiyāta cila ciṟu kuṟṟaṅkaḷ iruppatu koṇṭu, avarkaḷ peruṅkuṟṟaṅkaḷic ceyyalāmeṉavum appaṭic ceyvatu kuṟṟamākātu eṉavum pakara evarēṉum oruppaṭuvarō? oruppaṭāraṉṟē! avi pōlavē, nām aṟiyāmalum nām virumpāmalum irukka, nam kaṭpulaṉukku akappaṭāta nuṇṇiya ciṟṟuyirkaḷ pala nām uṇṇuñ civa uṇavupporuḷkaḷiluṅ kalantu nammāl vilakka muṭiyātapaṭi nam vayiṟṟuṉuṭ celvatu kuṟittu, nampōl aintaṟivum uṭampiṉ amippum vāynta āṭu māṭu kōḻi kokku mīṉ mutalāṉa ciṟṟuyirkaḷiyum nām nam neñcāra virumpik koṉṟum kolvittum avaṟṟiṉ ūṉit tiṉpamāṉāl, atu maṉṉikkappaṭāta peruṅkuṟṟamāy muṭiyumeṉṟu uṇarntukoḷka.
aḵtu uṇmiyēyeṉṟāluñ, ciṟṟuyirkaḷilēyē oṉṟu maṟṟoṉṟip piṭittut tiṉṟu uyirvāḻtal iyaṟki nikaḻcciyāyk kāṇappaṭṭu varutalāl, makkaḷum piṟa ciṟṟuyirkaḷik koṉṟu avaṟṟiṉ ūṉi uṇpatu kuṟṟamākāteṉiṉ; naṉṟu coṉṉāy; ciṟṟuyirkaḷ oṉṟiyoṉṟu piṭittut tiṉṉal pōla, makkaḷun tammuḷ oruvariyoruvar koṉṟu tiṉṉal vēṇṭumeṉak kūṟutal vēṇṭum. kāṭṭumirāṇṭi vakuppiṉar cilar tammuḷḷē oruvariyoruvar koṉṟu tiṉṟuviṭukiṉṟaṉar. iṅṅaṉamākap pakuttaṟivillāk kāṭṭumirāṇṭikaḷ ceyyuṅ koṭuñ ceyalip pārttup pakuttaṟivuṭiya makkaḷum avvāṟu ceytal iyaṟki tāṉeṉṟu collit, tammuḷ oruvariyoruvar koṉṟu tiṉṉalāmō? atuvēyumaṉṟic, ciṟṟuyirkaḷiṉ vāḻkkiyē iyaṟkikku ottateṉṟu karuti, ataṉpaṭi naṭakkap pukuntāl, makkaḷ vāḻkkiyāṉatu tāḻnta kāṭṭuvilaṅkukaḷiṉ vāḻkkiyāy, ūrellāṅ kāṭukaḷāy māṟal vēṇṭum. vilaṅkukaḷippōl nākarika makkaḷum āṭiyiṉṟi ammaṇamāy ulavutal vēṇṭum; avikaḷ vīṭuvāytaliṉṟi maraṅkaḷiṉ kīḻum malikkukikaḷiṉuḷḷun taṅkiyiruttalpōla avarkaḷum vīṭuvāytalit tuṟantu maraṅkaḷ malik kukikaḷil taṅkal vēṇṭum; avikaḷuṭ cila maṟṟa uyirkaḷi aṭittuk koṉṟu avaṟṟiṉ catiyip pacciyāyk kiḻittuṇṭal pōla, avarkaḷum vilaṅkukaḷik koṉṟu pacciyāka avaṟṟiṉ iṟicciyit tiṉṉal vēṇṭum; avikaḷuṭ paṉṟi mutalāṉa cila vilaṅkukaḷ malattic cāppiṭutal pōla, avarkaḷum malatti viḻuṅkutal vēṇṭum; avikaḷellām kalvi kaṟkavum kaṭavuḷi vaṇaṅkavum aṟiyāmipōla, avarkaḷuṅ kalvi kallāmaluṅ kaṭavuḷi vaṇaṅkāmaluṅ kālaṅ kaḻittal vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉamellām vilaṅkiṉ iyaṟkikaḷōṭu oppa, nākarika makkaḷum nākarika vāḻkkiyiviṭṭu uyir vāḻal kūṭumāyiṉaṉṟō, avarkaḷ avikaḷiṟ cilavaṟṟippōl ūṉtiṉṟu uyir vāḻtal poruntuvatākumā? nākarika makkaḷiṉ vāḻkkiyāṉatu vilaṅkukaḷiṉ vāḻkkikku muṟṟum māṟupaṭṭatāyirukkiyil, ūṉ tiṉpataṟku maṭṭum avaṟṟiṉ iyaṟkiyi avarkaḷ piṉpaṟṟal vēṇṭumeṉac ciṟitum ārāyātu kūṟuvār kūṟṟiṉ maṭamip perukki eṉṉeṉṟu pukalvēm! aṟiñarkaḷē āḻntu pārmiṉkaḷ!
iṉit, taliyiṟ puḻukkum pēṉkaḷiyun, taliyaṇi pāy paṭukki mūṭṭukkaḷiṟ puḻukkum mūṭṭup pūccikaḷiyum, mōṭṭuvaḷiyiluḷḷa elikaḷiyum makkaḷ kolla vēṇṭiyavarkaḷāy iruttalāl, avarkaḷ tam uṭampu vaḷarcciyiṉ poruṭṭu uyirkaḷik kolla vēṇṭiyavarkaḷā yirukkiṉṟaṉareṉap pulāluṇavu koḷvār cilar pukalkiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷ tamatu nalattiṉ poruṭṭut tammōṭotta makkaḷik koṉṟuviṭutaluṅ kuṟṟamākāteṉac colvataṟkup piṉvāṅkār. aruḷoḻukkamuṭiyarāy uyir vāḻa virumpum mēṉ makkaḷō ittakiya koṭiyōr colluṅ koṭuñ coṟkaḷukkuc ciṟituñ cevi koṭuttalākātu. pēṉ, mūṭṭuppūcci, eli mutalāṉavikaḷ nām irukkum illaṅkaḷil uṇṭātaṟkum varutaṟkum iṭañ ceyyāmal iruppamāyiṉ, avaṟṟi nām kolla vēṇṭuvatum nērātu. taliyilum āṭiyilum aḻukkuppiṭiyātapaṭi nīrāṭik kaḻuvit tuppuravāy iruppamāṉāl, pēṉ uṇṭāka māṭṭātu; paṭukkikaḷiyum aṅṅaṉamē aḻukkum īramum aṇukāmal veyilil aṭuttaṭuttuk kāya vittut tūyaṉavāy vittuk koṇṭāl mūṭṭup pūcci uṇṭākātu; illattuḷḷa aṟikaḷil veḷiccamum kāṟṟum mikutiyāy ulavumpaṭi ceytu, iṇṭuiṭukkukaḷ vaḷikaḷ illāmal aṭittu, uṇavup paṇṭaṅkaḷip pēḻikaḷilum nilippēḻikaḷilum vittu mūṭik karuttāy naṭantāl elikaḷ varamāṭṭā. aṉṟi, avvāṟellām karuttāy iruntum, orōvorukāl nam illaṅkaḷuṭpukuntu nammi varutti nam uyirkkut tīṅku ceyyumāyiṉ, appōtu avaṟṟi nammiṉiṉṟum akaṟṟutal kuṟṟamākātu. kaṭikka varum pāmpiṉiyuṅ kolla varum puliyiṉiyuṅ koṉṟu namminām pātukāttal kuṟṟamākātatu pōlavum, namatuṇavukku iṉṟiyamiyāta neṟpayirip pātukāttaṟ poruṭṭu avaṟṟiṭiyē vaḷarntu avaṟṟi yaḻikkum palvakip pūṇṭukaḷik kaḷinteṟital kuṟṟamākātatu pōlavum, nammuyirkkut tīṅku tarum entac ciṟṟuyirkaḷiyum nammiṉiṉṟum akaṟṟutal ciṟituṅ kuṟṟamākā teṉka. eṉṟālum, ac ciṟṟuyirkaḷi nammiṉiṉṟu akaṟṟumiṭattum, avi tammi nammāṟ kūṭiyavariyiṟ kollāmal akaṟṟutalē nām irakka neñcamuṭiyarāy oḻukutaṟkuc ciṟanta neṟiyām eṉpati aṉivarum neñciṟ patittal vēṇṭum. vilakamuṭiyātapaṭi avaṟṟi aḻittu akaṟṟa nērntapaḻi, avi tīṅkilāta piṟaviyiṟ cērumpaṭi iṟivaṉi vēṇṭi maṉṉippukkēṭṭu avaṟṟi avvāṟu ceytal vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉamākac cila ciṟṟuyirkaḷi vilakka muṭiyāta paṭi cila nēraṅkaḷil makkaḷ aḻittuviṭa vēṇṭiyiruppatākiya oru pulliya kāraṇattik koṇṭu, avarkaḷ āṭu māṭu kōḻi kokku mīṉ mutalāṉa tīṅkaṟṟa uyirkaḷiyuṅ koṉṟu avaṟṟiṉ ūṉi uṇṇalāmeṉṟu muṉpiṉ ciṟitum irakkamillāmaṟ pēcum vaṉṉeñcarkaḷiṉ koṭumiyi eṉṉeṉpēm! pulāluṇpavar mūṭṭup pūccik kāraṇaṅ koṇṭu pulāluṇṇalām eṉṟu aḻivaḻakkup pēcuvā rāyiṉ, avar ammūṭṭuppūcci pēṉ kocuku mutalāṉavi kaḷiyun tiṉṉāmi eṉṉiyōveṉṟu kūṟiviṭukka.
iṉi, nam intiya nāṭṭavari ayalnāṭṭavariṉ aṭimit taṉatti ṉiṉṟum mīṭka muyalvār pōl āravārañ ceyvār cilar. nammaṉōrkkup pōr puriyum āṟṟalum āṇmittaṉmiyum uṇṭākal vēṇṭumāyiṉ, avar kaḷellārum pulāluṇavu koḷḷa vēṇṭumeṉa evaruṅ kāṇāp putiyatoru vaḻiyik kaṇṭupiṭittuc colli varukiṉṟār. ivarkaḷ, kaṭavuḷiyum illiyeṉṟu tuṇivāyc collum pāḻṅkoḷki yuṭiyavarkaḷātalāl, ivarkaḷ tammiyotta makkaḷoṭu pōrpurintu avarkaḷip pāḻpaṭuttaṟkut, tīṅkaṟṟa uyirkaḷiyum pāḻpaṭutta vēṇṭum eṉṟu kūvukiṉṟaṉar! puli karaṭi ōnāyip pōṟ piṟa uyirkaḷik kollutaṟku viḻintu muṉ niṟkum ittakiya tīviṉiyāḷar irukkuṅkāṟum ulakil amitiyum naṉmiyum irā. ik koṭiyavar toki perukumāyiṉ in nilam veṟuñ cuṭu kāṭāyviṭum! āṉāl, ellā irakkamum ellā vallamiyum uṭiya āṇṭavaṉ ik koṭiyavar tokiyip peruka viṭāṉ eṉpatu tiṇṇam. ēṉeṉṟāṟ, kolit toḻililum pulāluṇṇutalilum mutaṉmi peṟṟu niṉṟa mēṉāṭṭavar kaḷilēyē aṟivum ārāycciyum mutirnta mēṉmakkaḷ pallāyirakkaṇakkāy nāḷukkunāṭperukik, kolit toḻiliyum ataṉāl varum ūṉ uṇṭaliyum peruntīṅkeṉa uṇarntu, avaṟṟi maṟuttal iṉṟiyamiyāta teṉap pallāyirakkaṇakkāṉa nūlkaḷālun tuṇṭu viḷamparaṅkaḷālum makkaḷukku aṟivupukaṭṭi varukiṉṟārkaḷ. ellāruṅkāṇa avarkaḷ iṭiviṭātu ceytuvarum ārāyccikaḷi ṉutavikoṇṭu, ūṉuṇpārkku nōykaḷ pala uṇṭāki avartam uṭal nalatti aḻittalāl avarkku uṭalāṟṟal maṉavāṟṟalkaḷ illiyām; avi illiyākavē avar āṇmittaṉmi yuṭiyavarātalum illiyām eṉṟum, marakkaṟiyuṇavu koḷpavarkku uṭalnala maṉanalaṅkaḷ tāmākavē amitalāl avar iyaṟkiyākavē āṇmiyum amitittaṉmiyum uṭiyarāy ettakiya arumperu muyaṟciyiyuñ ceytu muṭikka vallunarāyiruk kiṉṟaṉareṉṟum pulāluṇṭaliṉ tīmiyiyum pulāl maṟuttaliṉ naṉmiyiyum naṉku viḷakkivarukiṉṟaṉar. am mēṉmakkaḷ ceytu pōtarum ārāyccikaḷiṟ cila muṉṉarē eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyirukkiṉṟēm.
mēluṅ, koṭumiyuñ ciṉamum illā amitiyuṭiyārkkē āṟṟalum aṟivum mikutippaṭa, avarkaḷē tamakkum piṟarkkum payaṉpaṭṭu oḻukuvareṉpatum, ūṉuṇṭu koṭumiyuñ ciṉamum aḷavupaṭāmal mikap peṟṟavar tamakkum piṟarkkum payaṉilarātaluṭaṉ, potu makkaḷiṉ iṉiya vāḻkkiyiyuṅ kulittuviṭu nīrarātaliyum nāṭōṟum āṅkāṅku nikaḻum nikaḻccikaḷil vittu naṉkaṟiyalām. aṭipiṭi caṇṭiyil muṉintu niṟpavarkaḷum, muṉpiṉpārāmal āṟaṟivuṭiya makkaḷic cennīr oḻukat tuṭituṭikka veṭṭikkolpavarkaḷum yāvareṉa ārāyntu pārkkac ciṟṟuyirk koliyit toḻilākak koṇṭu ūṉ viṟpavarkaḷum pulāluṇavu nirampak koḷpavarkaḷumē ak koṭun toḻilic ceypavarāyirukkiṉṟaṉareṉa uṇavārāycciyiṟ ṟērnta aṟiñarkaḷ kaṇakkeṭuttiruk kiṉṟārkaḷ. avarpōl attuṇi nuṇṇiya ārāycci ceyyamāṭṭāta nām, nammiṉun tāḻnta vilaṅkukaḷiṉ iyaṟkiyil vittu ivvuṇmiyi eḷitāka aṟintu koḷḷalām. ūṉiyē tiṉṟu uyir vāḻum puli karaṭi ciṅkam ōnāy mutalāṉa maṟavilaṅkukaḷiṉ iyaṟkiyiyum, marattiṉ kuḻikaḷiyum pulliyum vikkōliyumē tiṉṟu uyirvāḻum āṭu māṭu erumi kutiri kaḻuti yāṉi oṭṭakam mutalāṉa aṟavilaṅkukaḷiṉ iyaṟkiyiyum pakuttup pārmiṉkaḷ! ivviruvaki vilaṅkukaḷil evi valimiyiṟ ciṟantaṉa? puli mutaliyaṉavē valimi mikkaṉaveṉṟu nīṅkaḷ niṉikkalām; āṉāl, aḵtu uṇmiyaṉṟu; peruñcumi yēṟṟiya oru pāravaṇṭiyil iraṇṭu erutukaḷiyum aṅṅaṉamē cumiyēṟṟiya maṟṟoru vaṇṭiyil iraṇṭu pulikaḷiyum pūṭṭi avaṟṟi ōṭṭiṉāl, erutukaḷippōṟ pulikaḷ ataṉi nīṇṭavaḻi iḻuttuc cellumā? ciṟituñ cellā. maṟavilaṅkukaṭkuk kūriya nakaṅkaḷum paṟkaḷum iruttal paṟṟi avi valimiyuṭiyaṉa pōṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉavē yallāmal, māṭu kutiri yāṉi mutaliya aṟavilaṅkukaḷip pōl avi valimiyiṟ ciṟantaṉa alla. atuvēyumaṉṟi, māṭu kutiri mutalāṉavikaḷip pōṟ puli karaṭi mutalāyiṉa makkaṭkuc ciṟitēṉum payaṉpaṭuvatum illi. ivvāṟu ivviruvēṟu vilaṅkukaḷiṉ iyaṟkikaḷiyum uṟṟu nōkku talālēyē pulāluṇpavar tamakkum piṟarkkum payaṉpaṭattakka, āṟṟalum aṟivum ilarātal eḷitil viḷaṅkum. it tuṇitāṉum āyntu pārāmaṟ pēcuvārtam veṟṟuriyiṉi nampip, pulāluṇpārkkē, aṟivum āṇmiyum uṇṭāmeṉa eṇṇiviṭātīrkaḷ!
iṉi, iññāṉṟuḷḷa makkaṭpakuppiṉar cilarkkuḷḷa āṇmittaṉmiyiṉ kāraṇam yātu? eṉṟu ārāyntu pārppīrkaḷāyiṉ, aḵtu avar ōvātu ceytu pōtarum aṟivārāycciyē yāvatallāl avarkkuḷḷa uṭal valimi yaṉṟeṉpatūun teḷiya uṇarvīrkaḷ. muṉṉāḷil aracar iruvarkkuṭ pōr mūṇṭāl, avviruvarkkuriya paṭi makkaḷum oruvarōṭoruvar neruṅkip pōr purintu oruvariyoruvar veṭṭic cāyttu, irutiṟattārum peruntuṉpam eytuvar. maṟṟu, innāḷilō pattukkal irupatukal vilaki niṉṟu irupaṭikaḷum pōrpuriyattakka palatiṟak karuvikaḷ aṟiñarkaḷāṟ putitu kaṇṭupiṭittu amikkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. pattukkal irupatu kaṟkaḷukku appāl niṟkum paṭikaḷiyuñ cuṭṭuc cāykkum pīraṅkikaḷum, aimpatukal aṟupatu kaṟkaḷukku appāl niṟkum paṭikaḷiyum arimaṇi nērattiṟ paṟantuceṉṟu māykkum vāṉakkappalkaḷum, nīr mēl niṟkuñ caṇṭikkappalkaḷi evarum aṟiyāmal nīruḷ mūḻkic ceṉṟu takarkkum nīrmūḻkik kappalkaḷum, nam collaḷavil aṭaṅkā eṉṉum ettaṉiyō arumperuṅ karuvikaḷum ikkālattiṟ putiyaputiyavāyk kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭu nilavukiṉṟaṉavē! aviyellām uṭal valimiyāl ākkappaṭṭaṉavā? aṉṟi aṟivu valimiyāl ākkap paṭṭaṉavā? ellām aṟivu valimiyāl allavō ākkap paṭṭaṉaveṉṟu ciṟu makāruñ colvar! iññāṉṟu putumiyiṉum putumiyāyk kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭaṉa vellām ūṉ valimi yuṭiyavarkaḷāl vantaṉa alla; maṟṟup paḻamum pālum vittukkaḷumākiya ciṟṟuṇā uṇṭu, iravil iraṇṭu maṇinēraṅkūṭa uṟaṅkāta aṟiñar perumakkaḷālēyē kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭaṉa veṉpatu, avartam varalāṟukaḷi uṇarvār naṉkaṟivar. miṉcāra āṟṟaliṉ vakimikaḷi ulakamellām viyakkumpaṭi putiya putiyavāyk kaṇṭupiṭittu viḷaṅkum eṭicaṉ eṉṉum aṟiñarperumāṉ varalāṟē itaṟkuc cāṉṟām. ākavē, aṟivuvalimi paṭittārkkē ellā valimiyum uṇṭākumallāmal, vēṟu ūṉ potitāṅ kiṉārkku ētoru valimiyum uṇṭākāteṉṟuṇarntu koḷka. itupaṟṟiyē āciriyar tiruvaḷḷuva nāyaṉār,
aṟivuṭiyār ellām uṭiyār aṟivilār
eṉṉuṭiya rēṉum ilar	(kuṟaḷ 430)
eṉṟatūum, paḻiya caṅkakālattup pulavar oruvar,
ūṉittiṉ ṟūṉip perukkāmi muṉ iṉitē 
eṉṟatūu meṉka.
iṉik, kaṇṇappanāyaṉār tāmun tiṉṟu iṟivaṉukkum paṭitta vilaṅkiṉ iṟicciyi iṟivaṉ ēṟṟuk koṇṭami, avartam varalāṟu kūṟum nūlkaḷālum pāṭṭukkaḷālum naṉkaṟiyakkiṭattaliṉ, ūṉ uṇpatu iṟivaṉ karuttukku māṟākāteṉṟu kūṟuvārum uḷar. ivar kūṟṟup porutta muṭit takāmiyiṉic ciṟitu viḷakkuvām; kaṇṇappa nāyaṉārō vēṭṭuva kulattiṟ piṟantu, ak kulattiṟkuriya vēṭṭiyāṭun toḻililum, at toḻilāl varum ūṉi uṇavākak koḷḷum vāḻkkiyilum paḻakiṉavar; vēṭṭa māṭun toḻil vilaṅkukaḷukkup peruntuṉpan taruvateṉpatu, ākavē vilaṅkukaḷik koṉṟu peṟṟa iṟicciyi iṟivaṉukkup paṭittal kuṟṟameṉpatuñ ciṟitum aṟiyātavar. ittaṉmiyarākiya avar, mēṟpiṟavikaḷiṟ ceyta peruntavattiṉ muṭinta payaṉāy, ellāmvalla muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷāṉa civapirāṉiṭattil mutirnta aṉpu koḷḷap peṟṟār. oruvarpāl aḷavukaṭanta aṉpu koḷḷappeṟṟa piṟaroruvar, tamakkuriya ciṟanta poruḷkaḷiyum tām nukarum ciṟanta uṇavuppaṇṭaṅkaḷiyum tamakkeṉa varintu vittuk koḷḷātu, tām aṉpu vittavarkkē avi tammi yellāṅ koṭuttu, avar nukarntu miñciya miccattiyē amiḻtiṉuñ ciṟantatākak karuti makiḻntu tām uṭkoḷḷā niṟpar. iḵtu ulaka vaḻakkilum nūl vaḻakkilum vittuc cevvaṉē aṟiyappaṭuvatōr arumpēruṇmiyām. ivvuṇmikku iciyavē, kaṇṇappa nāyaṉār tamakkuc ciṟanta uṇavākat tām karutiya muḷḷampaṉṟi māṉ mari kaṭampi mutalāṉa vilaṅkukaḷiṉ iṟicciyi mutalil iṟivaṉukkup paṭittu, ataṉpiṉ, mikuntatit tām ayiṉṟu, iṟivaṉatu aruṭ kuṟiyiṉi akalātu uṭaṉiruntu aṉpiṉāl akaṅkarintu nīrāy urukiṉār. empāl aṉpu mītūrap peṟṟār oruvar, emakkuḷḷa aṟivu illārāy, yām virumpuvatu iṉṉa teṉṟum uṇarārāyt, tām ciṟantatākak kikkoṇṭa oru paṇṭam evvaḷavu emmāl aruvarukkap paṭuvatāyiṉum ataṉit tām aṟiyāmē, empāl ataṉi nekkurukum neñcattōṭum uyttu, ataṉi ēṟṟaruḷka eṉa emmik kuḻintu kuḻinturuki vēṇṭiṭuvarāyiṉ; attuṇi aṉpuṭiya avaratu vēṇṭukōḷi yām ēlātiruttal iyalumō! iyalātaṉṟē. ciṟṟaṟivuñ ciṟu toḻilumuṭiya em pōṉṟārkkē, pēraṉpuṭiyār ceyyum aṉpiṉ varicikaḷi ēlāmal maṟuttal iyalāteṉṟāl, aṉpiṉ uruvāyt tikaḻum āṇṭavaṉ taṉṉaṭiyavar taṉpāl vitta aṉpiṉ perukkāṟ ceyyum aṉpiṉ aṭiyāḷac ceyalkaḷi - avi aṉpillāta ēṉi ulakattavarkku ettuṇi iḻintaṉavāka, ettuṇi iciyātaṉavākak kāṇappaṭiṉum, ēlātoḻivaṉō? taṉpāl aṉpu mikka taṉ iṉiya makavu taṉ kiyiṟ kiṭitta ōr aruvaruppāṉa paṇṭattit taṉ vāyil vittuṇṇumāṟu vaṟpuṟuttukiyil, tāyāṉavar evvaḷavu makiḻvōṭu ataṉi vāṅki, aḵtu iḻintatēyāyiṉum uṇṇat takkatāyiruntāl uṇṭu viṭukiṉṟāḷ! uṇṇat takātatāyiruntāl ataṉi uṇpatu pōṟ kāṭṭi ataṉi appuṟam maṟittu am makaviṉi makiḻvittut tāṉum makiḻkiṉṟāḷ. iṅṅaṉamē, aṉpiṉ perukkāṟ kaṇṇappa nāyaṉār paṭitta ūṉ uṇā, iṟivaṉāl ēṟṟuk koḷḷappaṭun takutiyuṭittaṉṟēṉum, avaratu pēraṉpiṉ peṟṟi nōkki, avaṉāl ēṟṟuk koḷḷappaṭṭatupōl tōṉṟalāyiṟṟu. ākavē, aṉpiṉuruvāṉa aiyaṉ aṭiyārkku eḷiyaṉān taṉmi yaṉātalāl, avaṉ avar poruṭṭuc ceypavikaḷi yellām avaṉṟaṉ tiruvuḷattiṟku oppaṉavākak kūṟutal aṭāteṉṟuṇarka. tāykku ākātavikaḷik kuḻanti āvaṉavākak karuti aṉpiṉāṟ ceyyumāyiṉ, avi tammit tāyāṉavaḷ taṉakkum āmeṉak koṇṭaṉaḷeṉak kūṟutal okkumō, urimiṉkaḷ! aṉparkaḷē! atupōlavē, kaṭavuḷun tamatu tiruvuḷattukku iciyātavikaḷi āmeṉak karutit tam aṉparkaḷ karikaṭanta aṉpiṉ perukkāṟ ceykuvarāyiṉ, avitammi āṇṭavaṉ aruṇmikutiyāl ēṟpatupōṟ kāṭṭi avartammi makiḻvippaṉ eṉṟuṇartal vēṇṭum.
aḵtokkumāyiṉum, aṉpiṉ mutircciyāṟ kaṇṇappar paṭitta pulāli iṟivaṉ uṇmiyil ēṟṟaṉaṉ allaṉ eṉpataṟkum, pulāluṇavu koṇṭamiyāṟ kaṇṇapparkku uṇṭāṉa tīviṉiyik kaḻittē iṟivaṉ avarit taṉ tiruvaṭikkaṇ ēṟṟaruḷiṉaṉ eṉpataṟkum aṭiyāḷam uḷatōveṉiṟ, kāṭṭutum: piṟa uyirkkuk kaṭuntuṉpattit taruṅ kolit toḻilic ciṟu piḷḷiyāyirunta kālan toṭṭē paḻaki vantavarkkut, tām ceyyum attoḻilāl avvuyirkaḷ evvaḷavu tuṉpatti aṭikiṉṟaṉa  eṉṉum uṇarcci ciṟitum uṇṭākātu. iṅṅaṉat tām ciṟitum uṇarātē ak kolit toḻili vaḻakkamāyc ceytu varukuvarēṉum, akkoṭun toḻilāl varuntīviṉi avari viṭṭakalātu; piṟavikaṭōṟum avarit toṭarntu ceṉṟu palavāṟṟāṉum avarit tuṉpuṟuttāniṟkum. ittakiyōr iṟivaṉ tiruvaṭip pēriṉpattip peṟutaṟkuriya pēraṉpu mikap peṟṟārāṉālum, avartām ceytu pōnta kolit toḻilāṟ piṟa uyirukkuṇṭāṉa koṭuntuṉpatti uṇarntu, tāmum avvuyirpōṟ koṭuntuṉpam uḻantu tuṉpattiṟkut tuṉpam īṭukoṭuttāl allāmal, avar tammic cūḻntu koṇṭirukkun tīviṉiyi yoḻittuc civattiṉ aruḷip peṟamāṭṭār. ātalāṟ, kaṇṇappa nāyaṉār kaṭavuḷiṭattu aḷavupaṭāp pēraṉpu koṇṭārāyiṉum, avar atukāṟuñ ceytu pōnta kolit toḻilāṟ piṟavuyirkkuṇṭāṉa peruntuṉpattiṉ aḷaviyuṇarntu, at tuṉpattiṟkut tāmum perun tuṉpam eyti īṭucelutta vēṇṭuvatu iṉṟiyamiyātatāyiṟṟu; avvāṟu ceytāṟṟāṉ avarik kavinta peruntīviṉi akalum; at tīviṉi akaṉṟāṟṟāṉ avar civattiṉ aruḷit talik kūṭutal vāykkum. āṉatu paṟṟiyē, ellām valla iṟivaṉ kaṇṇappariṉ pēraṉpi ulakattārkkup pulappaṭuttal vēṇṭiyum avar atukāṟuñ ceytuvanta kolit toḻilāl viḷinta tīviṉiyik kaḻittal vēṇṭiyum taṉ tiruvuruvattiṉ valatu kaṇṇil nōy mikuntu nīr vaṭivatupōṟ kāṭṭiṉāṉ. ataṉik kaṇṭa kaṇṇappar ‘emperumāṉ ṟaṉ kaṇṇukku vanta innōyi eṅṅaṉan tīrppēṉ! eṉṟu eṇṇiyeṇṇi ēṅki āṟṟā rāyiṉār. muṭivāka, avarirunta vēṭṭuvavāḻkkiyil, uṇavāka iṭutaṟkum nōy tīrttaṟkum urittāvatu iṉṉatu tāṉ eṉpatu pulaṉāyiṟṟu; tāmun tammiyotta maṟṟa vēṭṭuvar uṭampum ūṉāṟ camintaviyātalāl, ūṉukku ūṉiyiṭutalē paci tīrttaṟkum nōy tīrttaṟkum icinta muṟiyākum eṉṟu kaṇṭaṟintār. avaratu vēṭṭuva vāḻkkiyiṉ aḷavukku avaratu aṟivu kaṇṭa muṟi at tuṇiyētāṉ. avvaṟivu tōṉṟiyavuṭaṉ avaraṭinta makiḻccikku ōr elliyē illi. emperumāṉukku vanta ik kaṇṇōyit tīrkka eṉ kaṇkaḷil oṉṟip piṭuṅki, nōykaṇṭa kaṇṇil appak kaṭavēṉ eṉṟu maṉantuṇintu eḻuntu, āṇṭavaṉ tiruvuruvattiṉ arukē ceṉṟu, tamatu oru kūriya ampiṉ muṉiyāl tamatu oru kaṇṇi aṭiyōṭu kalliyeṭukkalāṉār. pāruṅkaḷ, aṉparkaḷē! innērattil avar tam uṭampum uyirum evvaḷavu tuṉpuṟṟut tuṭituṭittirukkum! piṟavuyiri avaṟṟiṉ ūṉiṉ poruṭṭuk kūrṅ kaṇiyāṟ kuttikkoṉṟa kālaṅkaḷil, avi evvaḷavu tuṉpuṟṟut tuṭituṭittirukkum! avvaḷavu tuṉpattiṟkum ippōtu kaṇṇappar tamatu kaṇṇik kuttik kalli eṭukkiṉṟakāliyil tāmum tuṉpuṟṟu tuṭituṭittu īṭu koṭuttavarāyiṉār! aiyaṟivuṭiya ciṟṟuyirkaḷiṉ tuṉpa uṇarcciyāṉatu, āṟaṟivuṭiya makkaḷiṉ tuṉpa uṇarcciyiṉum palapaṭi kuṟintatu. ivvēṟupāṭu veṭṭuṇṭa ōr āṭṭiṉ uṭampukkuḷḷa tuṭituṭippiyum aṅṅaṉamē veṭṭuṇṭa ōr āṇmakaṉuṭampukkuḷḷa āṟṟoṇāt tuṭituṭippiyum kaṇṭa oruvarkkē teriyum; mēluñ, ciṟṟuyirkaḷāvatu piṟar kiyāṟ kollappaṭukiṉṟaṉa; iṅkuk kaṇṇapparō tamatu kiyāṟ ṟam kaṇṇik kiṇṭi eṭukkiṉṟār! iḵtu evvaḷavu koṭuntuṉpam! atuvēyumaṉṟi, oruvaṉ taṉ kaḻuttit tāṉākavē virintu veṭṭik koḷvāṉāyiṉ, avaṉṟaṉ uyirum uṇarvum virivil avaṉatu uṭampiviṭṭu akalum! ataṉāl tuṉpa uṇarcci neṭuka irātu; āṉāl, iṅkuk kaṇṇapparō tamatu uṭampiṉkaṇ tam uyirum uṇarvum nilipeṟṟirukkum niliyil tamatu kaṇṇit tōṇṭi yeṭukkiṉṟār; ā! ītu evvaḷavu toṭarpāṉa tuṉpattit taruñceyal! ivvaḷavu koṭun tuṉpattiyum poṟuttuk koṇṭu, avar kaṭavuḷpāṟ kāṭṭiya pēraṉpiṉ tiṟam pulliya em oru nāvāl viḷampalān taṉmittō! ivvaḷavilāvatu civapirāṉ avari viṭṭaṉarā! illi! illi! kaṇṇappar tamatu oru kaṇṇip piṭuṅki appiya vuṭaṉ, civapirāṉ tamatu oru kaṇṇiliruntu nōy tīrntu viṭṭa aṭiyāḷaṅ kāṭṭiṉāṉ. atu kaṇṭu kaṇṇappar meymmaṟanta kaḷippuṭiyarāy āṭiṉār; pāṭiṉār! āṉāl, maṟṟināḷ iṟivaṉṟaṉ maṟṟoru kaṇṇilum nōymikuntu atiliruntum nīrvaṭiyalāyiṟṟu. ippōtu kaṇṇapparkku atu tīrkkum maruntu tammiṭam iruppatu naṉku teriyumātalāl, atupaṟṟi mikutiyāyk kavali koṇṭilar. uṭaṉē tamatu maṟṟoru kaṇṇiyun tōṇṭi eṭukka muṉintaṉar. āṉāl, eñciyuḷḷa ivvoru kaṇṇiyuṅ kalli yeṭuttuviṭṭāl, iṟivaṉuruvattil uḷḷa kaṇṇil ati appuvataṟkut tamakkup pārvi teriyātē eṉṟu kalaṅkiṉār. ataṉpiṉ, tamatu kāṟperuvirali iṟivaṉuruvil nōykaṇṭa kaṇṇiṉ arukē aṭiyāḷamāka vittuk koṇṭāl tām piṭuṅkum maṟṟak kaṇṇiyum atil appiviṭalāmeṉṟu vaḻi terintār. terintatum akaṅkaḷittut tamatu iṭatu kāṟ  peruvirali aṅṅaṉamē aṭiyāḷamāka vittukkoṇṭu, tamatu maṟṟik kaṇṇiyuṅ kalliyeṭuttaṟku ampiṉ muṉiyi ūṉṟiṉār. avvaḷavil avartāñceyta kolit toḻilāl viḷinta tīviṉiyum oḻintatu; avar muṟṟun tūyarāṉār. karikaṭanta aṉpiṉāl uḷan tūyarāya kaṇṇappari mēlun tuṉpuṟutta aruṭperuṅ kaṭalāṉa āṇṭavaṉ tiruvuḷḷam iciyavilli; uṭaṉē,
nillu kaṇṇappa! nillu kaṇṇappa!eṉ
 aṉpuṭit tōṉṟal nillu kaṇṇappa!
eṉa mummuṟi kūṟic civaperumāṉ taṉatu tirukkiyiṉālē avaratu kiyip piṭittut taṭiceytu avarit taṉatu tiruvaruṭ pēriṉpattiṟ kalappittuk koṇṭaṉaṉ. pārmiṉkaḷ aṉparkaḷē! ellā uyirkkun tuṇivaṉākiya iṟivaṉ ciṟitum naṭunili tavaṟāṉāy, aṉpiṟ ṟaliniṉṟa kaṇṇappa nāyaṉāriyum, avar ciṟṟuyirkaḷik koṉṟa tīviṉikku avar tāmē tamatu kaṇṇik kalliyeṭukkumāṟu ceytum, avvāṟṟāl uyirkkoliyiṉ tuṉpam ittuṇik koṭi teṉṟuṇarntu ataṟku avar tāmun tuṉpuṟṟu īṭu koṭukkumāṟu tūṇṭiyum oṟuttu, ataṉ piṉṉarē avarit taṉ tiruvaṭip pēriṉpa vāḻkkiyil amarvittaṉa ṉeṉṟāṟ, kaṇṇappariṉ kariyikanta aṉpil ōr aṇuttuṇi tāṉum illāta maṟṟa makkaḷ piṟa uyirkaḷik koṉṟeṭukkum ūṉittiṉṟu perukkun tīviṉikkāka avarit tuṉpuṟuttātu viṭuvaṉō! uyirkkoli ceyvārellām ūṉ tiṉpā rellān tāñ ceyyun tīviṉikkup piṟavikaṭōṟum aḷaviṟanta tuṉpatti yaṭintu īṭukoṭuttē tīralvēṇṭum. ivvuṇmi paṭṭiṉattaṭikaḷālum,
koṉṟaṉi aṉittum aṉittumniṉik koṉṟaṉa
tiṉṟaṉi aṉittum aṉittum niṉit tiṉṟaṉa
eṉṟu aṟivuṟuttappaṭṭami kāṇka. ivvuṇmi yuṇarātār koṉṟataṉāl uṇṭākun tīviṉi, koṉṟa uyiriṉ ūṉit tiṉpataṉāṟ pōyviṭum eṉṟu cerukki yurikkiṉṟārkaḷ! iḵtu aṟiyāmiyēyākum; ēṉeṉṟāṟ, koṉṟa uyiriṉ ūṉit tiṉpataṉāṟ koṉṟa tīviṉi evvāṟu nīṅkum? oruvaṉik koṉṟavaṉi ēṉ tūkkiliṭṭuk kollukiṟārkaḷ? kolit tuṉpattiṟkuk kolit tuṉpamē īṭākum eṉpatu paṟṟiyaṉṟō? ōr uyirik koṉṟu ataṉ ūṉit tiṉṟu makiḻpavaṉ, tāṉ ceyta kolit tuṉpattiṟku īṭākat tāṉuṅ kolittuṉpam aṭintāṉ āvaṉō! ākavē, ciṟṟuyirkaḷi ūṉiṉ poruṭṭuk kolvā rellām, tām maṟupiṟaviyiṟ ciṟṟuyirkaḷāyp piṟakkat, tām koliceyta ac ciṟṟuyirkaḷ makkaḷāyp piṟantu avi tammik koṉṟu tiṉṟāṟṟāṉ ap pāvam nīṅkum eṉpatē, koṉṟāṟ pāvam tiṉṟāṟpōm eṉṉum paḻamoḻiyiṉ meypporuḷākumeṉṟu aṟimiṉkaḷ! eṉavē, iṟivaṉ aṉpiṉ mikka taṉ aṭiyavar uyirkkoli ceytirun taṉarāyiṉ, avari at tīviṉikkut takkapaṭi tuṉpa īṭu koṭukkac ceytē piṉṉart taṉ tiruvaṭikkaṇ ēṟṟaruḷva ṉeṉpatik kaṭippiṭit tuṇarmiṉkaḷ! ākavē, kaṇṇappar vilaṅkukaḷik koṉṟu ūṉittiṉṟa kuṟṟatti iṟivaṉ ēṟṟaṉaṉ alla ṉeṉpatūum, ak kuṟṟan tīrum poruṭṭākavē avartamatu kaṇṇit tāmē iṭantu appumāṟu ceytaṉa ṉeṉpatūum aṟintu koḷḷalvēṇṭum.
aḵtokkum, iṟivaṉi nērē kaṇṭu, mūṉṟāṇṭuc ciṟukuḻaviyā yirunta ñāṉṟē ellām ōtātuṇarntu, aruḷoḻukkattiyē valiyuṟuttuñ civacamayatti nilināṭṭiṉavarāṉa tiruñāṉacampanta mūrtti nāyaṉār tām aruḷicceyta tillippatikattiṟ paṟappippaṭut teṅkum pacuvēṭṭu eriyōm puñ, ciṟappar vāḻvatilli eṉṉuñ ceyyuḷil uyirkkoli vēḷvi ceyta tilli vāḻ antaṇaric ciṟappar eṉṟu uyarttup pāṭiyateṉṉi? ataṉāl, uyirk koli ceytal kuṟṟam aṉṟeṉpatu avarkkum uṭaṉpāṭupōlu meṉiṉ; aṟṟaṉṟu; aññāṉṟirunta tilliyantaṇarkaḷ civapirāṉ māṭṭu elliyaṟṟa aṉpuṭiyavarkaḷ. vaṭakkiruntu vanta āriyar ceyta vēḷvikaḷ muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷākiya civapirāṉi nōkkic ceyyappaṭāmal, intiraṉ varuṇaṉ mittiraṉ mutalāṉa piṟantiṟakkuñ ciṟu teyvaṅkaḷi nōkkiyē ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. atukaṇṭa tilliyantaṇarkaḷ, avvāriyap pārppaṉarkku nallaṟivu koḷuttum poruṭṭu, avar ceytu pōnta vēḷvikaḷuṭ ciṟantaṉa cilavaṟṟit tāmun taḻuvi, avi tammic civapirāṉ, oruvar mēlaṉavākavē vittu vēṭkalāyiṉar; aṅṅaṉam vēṭkumiṭattum, āriyarip pōl āyirakkaṇakkāṉa āṭu māṭu kutiri erumi mutalāṉa uyirkaḷik kollāmal, ēṉimakkaḷellām nāṭōṟum uṇavukkākap pallāyirakkaṇakkāyk kollum orucila āṭukaḷiyē mantiraṅkaḷāl uṇarviḻakkac ceytu koṉṟu vēṭpārāyiṉar. mantiraṅkaḷāl uṇarviḻanta avvāṭukaḷukkut tuṉpa uṇarcci ciṟitumē illiyākum. iññāṉṟum uṭampiṉ uḷnōy uṭiyār cilari aṟuttu nōy tīrkkum maruttuvar, mayakka maruntu oṉṟāl, annōyāḷikaḷi uṇarviḻakkac ceytu, avarkkuc ciṟitumē tuṉpam illāmal avartam uṭampi aṟuttu maruttuvañ ceytal pōlavum, yōka nittiri eṉṉum aṟituyili varuvikkap paḻakiṉavarkaḷ attuyili varuvikkum muṟiyāl aṅṅaṉamē nōyāḷi kaḷiyum piṟariyum uṇarviḻakkac ceytu avartam uṭampukaḷi avarkkuc ciṟitun tuṉpam uṇṭākāmal aṟuttu nōy tīrttal pōlavum, paṇṭikkālattu antaṇarkaḷ tām vēṭpataṟkuk koṇarvitta vilaṅkukaḷi mantiraṅkaḷāl uṇarviḻakkumāṟu ceytē ataṉpiṉ avaṟṟi veṭṭi vēṭṭaṉar. ivvāṟu vilaṅkukaḷukkuc ciṟitun tuṉpam uṇṭākāvaṇṇam avaṟṟi uṇarviḻakkac ceyyum aṟituyil muṟiyiṉi (Anesthesia) nam paṇṭimakkaḷ aṟittiruntaṉa reṉpatu, irukkuvēta mutal maṇṭilattiṉ, 162 ām patikattil īṟṟil,
nī kaṭavuḷ pāṟcellumpōtu niṉ uyirmūccu
	niṉakkut tiṇaṟutali uṇṭākkātirukka!
kōṭariyāṉatu niṉ uṭampi ūṟupaṭuttātatāka!
	tiṟamiyillātu virintupiḻipaṭum veṭṭukkāraṉ
		niṉ uṟuppukaḷip piḻipaṭap piḷavāṉāka!
uṇmiyākavē nī iṅkē cākiṉṟili! nī ētum
	ūṟupaṭṭut tuṉpuṟukiṉṟili!
nī cīrāṉa eḷiya neṟiyāl tēvarkaḷpāṟ cellu kiṉṟaṉi
eṉap pōnta mantira moḻiyāl naṉku pulaṉākum. naṉṟu coṉṉīr; irukkuvēta patikaṅkaḷ pāṭappaṭṭa attuṇip paḻiyakālattē, aṟituyil muṟikaḷip paṇṭi makkaḷ aṟintiruntaṉareṉpatu nampaṟpāla taṉṟāmeṉiṟ; kaṭaliṭiyēyuḷḷa tīvukaḷil vāḻuṅ kāṭṭumirāṇṭi makkaḷum iv vaṟituyil muṟiyiṉi naṉkaṟintirukkiṉṟaṉareṉa, at tīvukaḷiṟ ceṉṟu avartam vāḻkkiyiṉ iyalpukaḷi ārāyntu kaṇṭu vanta airōppiya aṟiñarkaḷ eḻutiyirukkiṉṟaṉar.4 nākarika aṟivillāk kāṭavarkaḷum im muṟiyiṉit terintuḷarāyiṉ, nam mutumakkaḷ itaṉit terintiruntaṉareṉpatiṟ putumiyum māṟukōḷāvatum ētumilliyeṉka. iṉi ettakiya uyirkaḷiyum aṟituyil muṟiyil tērccipeṟṟa oruvar oru noṭip poḻutil uṇarviḻakkac ceyyalām eṉpatum, iṉṉum itupōṉṟa aṟituyil nuṭpaṅkaḷum `yōkanittiri eṉṉum ematu nūlil virittu viḷakkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa; avitammi ataṉkaṭ kaṇṭu koḷka.
ivvāṟu aṟituyil muṟiyāl orucila āṭukaḷi uṇarviḻakkac ceytu civaperumāṉukku vēṭṭu, avvāṟṟāl āriyar ceytu pōnta pala ciṟu teyvaveṟiyāṭṭu vēḷvikaḷi niṟutti vanta tillivāḻantaṇar tam aṉpiṉ ceyal pārāṭṭaṟpālatā yiruntamiyiṉ, tiruñāṉacampantap perumāṉ avartammi aṅṅaṉañ ciṟappittup pāṭiṉār eṉpatu. aṟṟaṉṟu, āriyar ceytu pōnta kolimalivēḷviyi niṟutta virumpiya tilliyantaṇar, tām avipōṉṟa vēḷvi puriyātiruttalē naṉṟākumaṉṟit, tāmum avarpōṟ ceytal naṉṟāmāṟu yāṅṅaṉameṉiṉ; yāṉi piṭippavaṉ yāṉiyik koṇṭē ataṉip piṭippippaṉ; atupōl, āriyap pārppaṉaric civa voḻukkattiṟkut tiruppal vēṇṭiya tamiḻantaṇarum avarpōṟ ṟāmuñ ciṟitu ciṟitu naṭattik kāṭṭiṉālaṉṟi, avar tammit tamvaḻippaṭuttal ākātu; atu kaṇṭē, avar tammi avarōṭu iṉappaṭuttikkoṇṭu avvāṟu ceyyalāyiṉar. iṅṅaṉam ceyyātu viṭṭāl, āriyap pārppaṉar tamiḻantaṇarit tamakku muṟṟum puṟampāṉavarākak karuti, avar kūṟuñ civa aruḷoḻukkattik kippaṟṟātu akaṉṟu oḻukuvar. āriyap pārppaṉartam vēḷvi muṟikaḷi muṟṟum maṟuttamiyiṉālallavō putta camaṇa camayaṅkaḷ puṟaccamayaṅkaḷeṉa avar tammāl aṟavē vilakkappaṭṭu, avaṟṟiṉ ariya aruḷoḻukkak kōṭpāṭukaḷum avar tammāṟ ṟiṉiyaḷavun taḻuvappaṭātoḻintaṉa; oḻiyavē, apputta camaṇacamayaṅkaḷ innāṭṭil nilikkaḷaṉ kāṇātum maṟintu pōyiṉa. ākavē, innuṭpam uṇarnta paṇṭit tamiḻnāṭṭuc cāṉṟōrākiya tamiḻantaṇarkaḷ, āriyap pārp paṉarit tam vaḻippaṭuttutaṟ poruṭṭākavē, avartam vēḷvi muṟikaḷit tāmun taḻuvuvatu pōṟ ciṟitu ciṟitu ceytu kāṭṭip, piṉ kālañ cellac cellap paṭippaṭiyē evvaki vēḷviyun tamiḻnāṭṭil vēṭkappaṭātapaṭi avitammi aṟavē niṟuttiviṭṭaṉar. vēḷvi yāṟṟutali niṟuttiyapiṉ, āriyar tammi viṭṭu vilaki mīṇṭuñ ciṟuteyva vaṇakkattik kippaṟṟuvareṉak karutiyē, avar koṇarnta āriyac ciṟu teyvappāṭṭukaḷākiya irukku. yacur, cāmam, atarvam mutaliya vētaṅkaḷit tammuṭiya civapirāṉ tirukkōyilkaḷil ōtutaṟkum, āriyamoḻic coṟkaḷāṟ civapirāṉi vaḻipaṭutaṟkum, āriyac ciṟu teyvaṅkaḷāṉa intiraṉ varuṇaṉ mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ vaṭivaṅkaḷic civapirāṉ kōyil mūlikaḷil vittu vaṇaṅkutaṟkum, tamiḻnāṭṭil nikaḻnta iṟivaṉ tiruviḷiyāṭalkaḷiyum aṭiyār varalāṟukaḷiyum ellām āriyariṉ vaṭamoḻiyil eḻuti vittaṟkum, cariyi kiriyi yōkam ñāṉam eṉṉuṅ kaṭavuḷ vaḻipāṭṭu muṟikaḷiyellām ākamam eṉappeyar tantu vaṭamoḻiyil  eḻutivittaṟkum oruppaṭṭu niṉṟu, āriyar ceytu pōnta ciṟuteyva vaṇakkatti aṭiyōṭu niṟutti, avarellāñ civapirāṉākiya muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷ oṉṟiyē vaḻipaṭṭuk koli kuṭi pulāluṇavu nīkki muṟṟuñ civavoḻukkattiṉarākumpaṭi ceytu, tām koṇṭa mikavuyarnta nalleṇṇatti niṟivēṟṟiviṭṭaṉar. tuvakkattil mikavum paraparappōṭu virintu paraviya putta camaṇa camayaṅkaḷiṉ muṉivarkaḷālun tirutta muṭiyāmal, tammit tirutta muṉinta apputta camaṇa camayaṅkaḷiyum innāṭṭiviṭṭu ōṭṭiya āriyarit, tamiḻantaṇarkaḷ muṟṟun tirutti avar civa aruḷoḻukkattik kippaṟṟumpaṭi ceytaṉareṉṟāl, nam tamiḻantaṇartam aṟiviṉ nuṭpattiyum aruḷāṟṟaliyum eṅṅaṉam eṭuttiyampavallēm! ātalāl, aṉparkaḷē! tilli vāḻantaṇarkaḷ aññāṉṟu vēṭṭa vēḷvikaḷ āriyarit tiruttutaṟporuṭṭuc civapirāṉi nōkkic ceyyappaṭṭa karuttuṇarntē, tiruñāṉacampantap perumāṉ paṟappip paṭutteṅkum pacuvēṭ ṭeriyōmpuñ ciṟappar vāḻtillic ciṟṟampalam eṉṟu avartammi uyarttup pāṭiṉār. pallāyira uyirkaḷik kākkum poruṭṭu oru cila uyirkaḷik kollutal kuṟṟam ākāmi araciyal muṟikkum ottatē yākaliṉ, āriyarvēṭṭa pallāyirak kaṇakkāṉa uyirkkoliyi niṟuttutaṟ karuviyāyt tilli vāḻantaṇar ellām valla iṟivaṉi nōkkic cilluyir vēṭṭuc ceyta vēḷvikaḷuṅ kuṟṟamākā āyiṉa. āriyar ceṉṟa vaḻiyē ciṟitu tāmuñ ceṉṟu tamiḻantaṇarkaḷ avarit tiruttiṉamiyiṉālētāṉ, tamiḻnāṭṭiluḷḷa, āriyap pārppaṉar ellāruṅ kolipuli maṟutta civavoḻukkat tiṉarāy iṉṟu kāṟum vāḻntu varukiṉṟaṉar. maṟṟup puttamum camaṇamum mikap paraviyirunta vaṭanāṭṭiluḷḷa āriyap pārppaṉarō civavoḻukkañ ciṟitum illārāy iṉṟukāṟum pulāluṇavu koṇṭu kālaṅ kaḻippavarāyc civapirāṉi vaḻipaṭātavarāy irukkiṉṟaṉar; iḵtu etaṉāleṉṟu ārāyntu pārppiṉ, putta camaṇa mataṅkaḷ āriyaroṭu oruciṟitum ovvātu avarkku muṟṟum māṟāy niṉṟataṉālēyē, āriyar avari eṭṭuṇiyum piṉpaṟṟātu tām ceytu pōnta uyirkkolic ceyalilē niliniṉṟaṉa reṉpatu pulaṉākum. ivvāṟṟāl āriyarit tiruttiya tamiḻc cāṉṟōriṉ pēraṟivum, avarit tiruttamāṭṭātu tāmum maṟintoḻinta putta camaṇa camayattiṉartañ ciṟṟaṟivum evarkkum naṉku viḷaṅkum. eṉavē, tiruñāṉacampantar tillivāḻantaṇar tam oḻukalāṟṟi uyarttup pēciyatu vāyvatēyāmeṉka.
avvāṟu avar, avartam oḻukkatti uyartturippiṉum, tām tam vāḻnāḷ elliyil eṅkum vēḷvi ceytatumilli; atu ceyyumāṟu piṟari ēviyatumilli. tam tantiyār vēḷvi ceyya virumpi ataṉ celavukkākap poruḷ vēṇṭiya kāliyil, avar kaṭṭa poruḷit tantu avari viṭuttaṉarēyallāmal, tantiyār vēṭṭa vēḷvik kaḷattiṟkut tām ceṉṟilar. itaṉāl, vēḷvi vēṭṭalil tiruñāṉacampantap perumāṉukkuc ciṟitum uṭampāṭilli yeṉpatu teṟṟeṉap pulaṉātal kāṇka.
iṉik, kōḻi nīrkkōḻi tārā (vāttu) mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ muṭṭikkum, puṟpūṇṭukaṭkum avaṟṟiṉ payaṉāṉa kāy kaṉi vittuk kiḻaṅkukaṭkum vēṟupāṭu illāmiyiṉ, muṭṭiyi yuñ civa uṇavupporuḷkaḷil oṉṟāka vittu uṇpatu kuṟṟamākāteṉa orucārār kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. ārāy taliṉṟi yurikkum ivaraturi ciṟitum poruttamuṭittaṉṟu. oru muṭṭiyāṉatu uruvākāta oru kōḻikkuñcē yākum5 eṉṟu āṅkila iyaṟkipporuḷ nūlār ārāynturik kiṉṟamiyiṉ atu puṟpūṇṭukaḷi yoppatāmeṉṟal aṟiyā vuriyākum. kōḻiyāl aṭikākkappaṭum muṭṭikaḷiliruntu cila nāṭkaḷiṟ kōḻik kuñcukaḷ veḷivarutali evarum aṟivar. atupōl puṟpūṇṭukaḷiyum aṭikākka vittāl ettaṉi nāḷāyiṉum avaṟṟiliruntu kōḻi nīrkkōḻi tārā mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ kuñcukaḷ veḷivarak kaṇṭatuṇṭō? illiyē. ākiyāl, muṭṭikaḷiyum, puṟpūṇṭukaḷiyum oṉṟeṉṟal maṭavōruriyām. mēlum, muṭṭikaḷiṉ veḷḷikkaru mañcaṭkaruviṟ puṟpūṇṭukaṭ killā muṭi nāṟṟam iruttalāṉum, akkaruviliruntē cila nāṭkaḷil eṉpu tōl narampu irattam mūḷi mutaliya uṟuppukkaḷellāñ cevvaṉē aminta kōḻikkuñci ṉuṭampu veḷivarak kāṇṭalāṉum, atupōl niliyiyaṟ poru ḷākiya puṟpūṇṭukaḷ avaṟṟiṉ payaṉkaḷiliruntu iyaṅkiyaṟ poruḷkaḷākiya kōḻi kuruvi kokku mīṉ āṭu māṭu mutaliyaṉa uṇṭākak kāṇāmiyālum muṭṭiyāṉatu civa uṇavup poruḷkaḷil aṭaṅkumeṉṟal oruvāṟṟāṉum aṭātu. atuvēyumaṉṟic, cila uyirkaṭkup peritu payaṉ paṭumpaṭi iṟivaṉāl tarappaṭṭa uṭampukaḷ uruvāvataṟku nilikkaḷaṉākiya muṭṭikaḷi aḻippatu, avvuyirkaḷiṉ uṭampi aḻippatē yāvatallāṟ piṟitiṉmiyiṉ, atuvuṅ koṭiya kolip pāvamēyātal tiṇṇam eṉṟaṟika.
iṉi, iññāṉṟuḷḷārum maruttuvar cilarum muṭṭi tiṉpatu uṭampiṉnalam pēṇuvataṟkuc ciṟantatāmeṉṟu eṇṇuvatuñ colluvatum uṇmikku māṟupaṭṭaṉavāyirukkiṉṟaṉa. ēṉeṉṟāl, makkaḷuṭampiṟ cūṭṭiṉit tōṟṟuvittuk koḻuppiṉi vaḷarppataṟku iṉṟiyamiyātu vēṇṭappaṭum iṉippuṇā (Carbo - hydrates) eṉṉum poruḷ, muṭṭik karuviṟ ciṟitumē yilli yeṉṟum, narampu narampiṉṟaci mūḷi ciṟu narampukaḷeṉṉum ivaṟṟi valivēṟṟi vaḷarppataṟku iṉṟiyamiyātu vēṇṭappaṭum mutaluṇā (Protein) eṉṉum poruḷ muṭṭikkaruvil mikak kuṟivākavē irukkiṉṟateṉṟum, muṭṭiyuṇavu tīṉippiyil eḷitiṟ cerippatilli yeṉṟum6 āṅkila āciriyar naṉkārāyn turikkiṉṟamiyiṉ, muṭṭiyuṇavu makkaḷuṭampukku ēṟṟatallāta tāvatuṭaṉ, aḵtu ōruyiriṉuṭampi aḻikkun tīviṉiyiyum uṇṭākkuvatāmeṉṟu ōrntu koḷka. eṉavē, muṭṭiyuṇavum pulāluṇavēyāy vilakkaṟpālatā meṉka.

mutaṟ patippiṉ mukavuri
makkaḷ nūṟāṇṭu uyirvāḻtal eppaṭi? eṉap peyariya in nūli iṟṟikku irupattu nāṉku āṇṭu kaḷukku muṉ cumiya vikāciyil eḻutat tuvaṅkiṉēm. ceṉṟa muppattōrāṇṭukaḷāka emmāl naṭattappaṭṭu varum ematu ñāṉācākaram eṉṉun tiṅkaḷ veḷiyīṭṭiṉ aintām patumattu mutalitaḻilē itaṉ mutalattiyāyamākiya `nīṇṭa vāḻkki eṉpatu veḷivarat toṭaṅkiyatu. av vintām patumattiṉ maṟṟiyitaḻkaḷilum āṟu, ēḻu, eṭṭu, oṉpatu, pattām patumaṅkaḷiṉ iṭi iṭiyēyum veḷiyāki vanta itaṉ maṟṟi attiyāyaṅkaḷ. yōka nittiri. maṉita vaciyam eṉṉum emmuṭiya ēṉi nūlkaḷi appōtu virintu muṭikka vēṇṭi yeḻunta iṭiyūṟṟāl ap pattām patumattiṉ 2,3 ām itaḻkaḷil veḷivanta aḷavil niṟka, innūlum iṭiyē muṭivu peṟātu niṟkalāyiṟṟu. atuvariyil veḷivanta in nūliṉ pakkaṅkaḷ 216 ākum, in nūlukku appōtu iṭap paṭṭa peyaril `varuṣam eṉṉum vaṭa coṟ kalantirukkiṉṟatu. innūl mutaṉ mutal eḻutat tuvaṅkiya ñāṉṟu, vaṭacoṟ kalappāl tamiḻmoḻikku nērum pollāṅkiṉi yām uṇarātiruntamiyāṟ ciṟitēṟak kuṟiya innūliṉ mutal nūṟu pakkaṅkaḷiṟ ciṟcila vaṭacoṟkaḷ iṭiyiṭiyē kalantuviṭalāyiṉa.
ñāṉacākarap pattām patumattu 3ām itaḻil niṉṟupōya in nūṟpakuti mīṇṭum ñāṉacākaram patiṉin tām patumattu 3 āmitaḻilē tuvakkañ ceyyappaṭṭuc ciṟitēṟakkuṟiyat toṭarpāka ataṉ kaṇṇum. patiṉāṟām patumattu mutal nāṉkitaḻkaḷilum veḷiyāki. ippōtu appatumattiṉ iv 5,6ām itaḻkaḷil muṭivu peṟalāyiṟṟu. iṭiyilē māṇikkavācakar varalāṟuṅ kālamum  eṉṉum periya nūli ñāṉacākaram 10ām patumattiṉ 10ām itaḻiliruntu tuvaṅki ēḻāṇṭukaḷ peritārāynteḻuti, 14ām patumattiṉ īṟṟil muṭikka vēṇṭiya kaṭṭāyam nērntamiyāl, innūl ciṟitēṟak kuṟiya eṭṭāṇṭukaḷ variyil iṭiyē eḻutappaṭutaṟku iṭamillāmal niṉṟu pōyatu. eṉṟālum, ellām valla iṟivaṉ tiruvaruḷāl ip perunūl irupattu nāṉku āṇṭukaṭkup piṉṉarēṉum ippōtu iṉitu muṭivupeṟa lāṉamikku ematu pulliya vaṇakkamem perumāṉ ṟiruvaṭip pōtukaṭku uriyatākuka!
in nūli muṭittaṟku ittaṉi kālañ ceṉṟa tāyiṉum, itiṟ collappaṭṭa poruḷkaḷiyum muṟi kaḷiyum paṟṟiyām ōvātu ārāyntapaṭiyākavēyirun tēm. nōy varā vakikaḷiyum vanta nōy nīkkumuṟi kaḷiyum paṟṟittamiḻ āṅkila vaṭamoḻikaḷiṟ cāṉṟōr eḻutiya uyarnta nūlkaḷ paṟpalavaṟṟi yām payiṉṟaṟinta vaḷavil nillātu, avi tammi yellām emmiṭattē ceytu pārttum. emmic cārntāriṭattē ceytu pārttum. avaṟṟuḷ evi mikac ciṟantaṉavāyc celavillaṉavāy eḷitiṟ ceytu payaṉpeṟat takkaṉavāyt teḷiyappaṭṭaṉa vō avikaḷi in nūliṉkaṇ varin tirukkiṉṟēm.
ittakiyatoru payaṉ ciṟanta urinūl tamiḻ moḻikkaṇ illāp peruṅkuṟiyāl, āṇ peṇ pālāriṟ perumpakutiyiṉar tamatu vāḻkki yiṟ piḻimika oḻukip palvaki nōykaḷālum poruṭcelavālum vaṟumiyālum piṭiyuṇṭu, tām āṇṭu mutirā muṉṉarē iṟantoḻiva tallāmalun. tam vayiṟṟiṟ piṟakkum piḷḷikaḷum. avarkkup piṟakkum piḷḷikaḷum ellām eṅṅaṉamē peruntuṉpaṅkaḷukku vaḻivaḻi āḷāki maṭintu pōkavuñ ceytuviṭukiṉṟaṉar. tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ tuṉpam mikunta ik kuṟu vāḻkkiyik kaṇṭu ematuḷḷattil uṇṭāṉa irakkamē in nūliyiyaṟṟi veḷiyiṭumāṟu emmit tūṇṭalāyiṟṟu. makkaḷ vāḻkki yip paṟṟiya in nūlil, entap pakutiyiyum maṟitteḻututal ākāmiyālum. aṉṟi aṅṅaṉam maṟit teḻutiṉāl, iṉṟiyamiyātu terintu oḻuka vēṇṭiya muṟikaḷ viṭappaṭṭu. ataṉāl āṇ peṇ pālāri iḵtu aṟiyāmiyiṉiṉ ṟeṭukkamāṭṭātāyp payaṉiṉṟik kaḻiyumātalālum `āṇpeṇ cērkki, `makkaṭpēṟu `karu vilakkumutalāṉa pakutikaḷiṟ colla vēṇṭuvaṉa vellām oru ciṟitum maṟiyāmal tiṟappākavē varin tirukkiṉṟēm. makkaḷ vāḻkkiyiṉ uyirniliyāyuḷḷa ic ciṟanta poruḷ kaḷit teḷintu viḷakkātu viṭutalāl vaḻi vaḻip perukum peruntīṅkiṉi naṉkuṇarntē. tirumūlar mutalāṉa nam paṇṭiyāciriyarkaḷum ivi tammit tam nūlil maṟiyā teṭuttu viḷakki aṟivuṟut tirukkiṉṟaṉar. iññāṉṟi āṅkila amerikka āciriyar kaḷiṟ pukaḻ mikappaṭitta āṭavarum makaḷiruṅkūṭa ittuṟikaḷil maṟivu ciṟitu miṉṟiyē tiṟanteḻuti varumāṟṟi, itaṟkup piṉṉuḷḷa ematu āṅkila mukavuri yuḷḷum eṭuttup pēciyirukkiṉṟēm. ātalāl vāḻkki yiṉ maṟiporuḷ nuṭpaṅkaḷ muṟṟum, nōy nīkkum iyaṟki muṟikaḷ muṟṟum naṉkeṭuttu viḷakkum innūl āṇpeṇpālār irutiṟattiṉarkkum peritu payaṉ paṭṭu avari nīṭiṉitu vāḻvikkum eṉṉum nampikki mikutiyum uṭiyēm.
innūliṉ mutaṟpakutiyil viraviyirukkuñ ciṟcila vaṭacoṟkaḷiyum itaṉ iraṇṭām patippiṉkaṭ kaḷintu viṭutaṟku tiruvaruḷ viriviṟ kūṭṭuvatāka.

tiruvaḷḷuvar āṇṭu, 1963	maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ
mācittiṅkaḷ, 3 ām nāḷ

Preface
“On  a natural system of diet, old age would be our last and our only malady; he term of our existence would be protracted; we should enjoy life and no longer preclude others from the enjoyment of it.”
- Poet Shelly in “A Vindication of Natural Diet”
The following second large work of mine (my first large work being “The Life and Times of St Manickavachakar) in Tamil embodies the results of my careful study of Long Life problems, of my observations of symptoms in myself and in others in health and in disease, and of my experimental modes of living an dmethods of healing pursued continually for more than thirty years. Nothing but what has been found in all these thirty years  to benefit others and myself is set down in the following pages. Before recommending to others what I read and thought about how to mintin a healthy life and how to restore it in cases where it was impired, I tried them all on myself and on my people undergoing of course considerable trouble and expending large sums of money all through, until I came to hit upon the very simple, natural, correct, inexpensive modes of leading and mintining a healthy long life. Although it took me twenty four years to complete this treatise owing to interruptions occasioned by my other literary, religious and historical works, yet it has gined - much in my long varied experince, wide study and progressive thinking.
Here in India as elsewhere, it is generally believed at living long does not lie within the power of man. Most of the people in India attribute to the power of late  both a short life and a long one. Only a few hold to the view that man has infinite possibilities in store, if he would only care to exercise his will, and that according as he energizes it or leaves it dormant, does he either lengthen it or leaves it dormant, does he either lengthen his life’s duration or shorten it to a span. These few are mostly the adherente of the yoga system of philosophy. In ancient times not only but so in Greece, the followers  of Kapila and Patanjali, and Pythagoros practised strict yogic modes of living and thinking and eventually enjoyed a long life extending to hundred years and more.
	But in recent times when the practice of yoga and therewith the natural ways of living and the loftier ways of thinking had the more fallen into disuse, as the people became more and more illiterate, ignorant and aggressively selfish indulging in animal passions and aggressively selfish indulging in animal passions and morbidly craving fpr innatural fllds and drinks, the human life also has become hideously short. Most horrible forms of disease have multiplied and great sufferings and countless premature deaths do occur. Still they pass unnoticed simply as ineviable occurrences. The yogic mode of living has even come to be looked upon as belonging exclusively to the ascetic few.
	But, towards the end of the last century Saint amalinga in Southern India and Saint RamaKrishna and his great and illustrious disciple Swami Vivekananda in Northern India had, by their impressive teachings and thrilling lectures, revived the study and practice of Yoga to an appreciable degree and showed how all who are willing might share in the benefits of Yoga.
	Still earlier in far- off countries Newton the great discoverer  of scientific laws, struck deeply by the fact lf the wonderfully long life led by some men-especially by Thlmas Parr who lived 153 years, and Henry Jenkins 169, devoted his attention to a study of the habits and diets of the long lived persons and found out the great law existing between the purity and simplicity of the former and the long life of the latter. He then wrote a “luminous and the eloquent essay” on Defence of Vegetable Regimen expounding the principles that underlie the perpetuation of life, and thus gave a new impetus to the investigation  of laws governing the length of human life. After the time of Newton, the question of clean food and its intimate relation to a healthy long life, touched the finer feelings of so great an  angelic soul as the poet Shelley and so much excited his compassion for human and animal life that he promptly wrote a thoughtful, sound and brilliant essay entitled. “ A Vindication of Natural Diet” and disseminated his precious ideas all over the foreign countries.
	From the time of Shelly this important subject began to engage the attention of many great scientists such as Louis Kuhne, Dr. A. Haig, Prof. Metchnikoff, Prof. Loeb, Dr. Albert Matthews, and Dr. J. H. Kellogg and of such great thinkers and philosophers as Prentice Mulford, Helen Wilmans, Harry Gaze and others. Dr. Haig’s book on “Uric Acid” and Dr. Kellogg’s “The New Dietetics” are teally epoch-making works in as much as they bring to clear light the hitherto hidden fact relating to food and life. The number of learned people who are greatly benefited by the teachings of these eminent authorities is dily increasing by tens and hundreds of thousands in foreign countries, while here in Southern India those who know even the names of these savants are extremely rare.
	When this vital knowledge is in such a deplorable condition even among the English-educated people of this country, is it to be wondered at for its being mjch more so among others who are almost illiterate and who only speak Tamil but know not either to read or write any thing in it? That the increasing number of the English- educated persons should, after completing their university course, go hunting after posts in Government Offices and ilsewhere, mostly not with any honourable intention but with an avidity to scrape up as much as they can the people’s money, that they should vie with each other in spending it as much in adorning their females with costly diamond jewels and silk cloths of every high grade as in other vin luxuries, that they should take no thought of the poor, suffering and prematurely dying millions in the country, of their language and religion and their social amelioration, is really a heart-rending spectacle. 
It was this lamentable state of affairs that at first roused in me a feeling of boldness to layout the limited sources of my income in the acquisition and propagation of knowledge pertining to the science of health, although this to a certin extent interfered with my study of English, Tamil and Sanscrit literatues, of the different systems of philosophy and different religious systems of the world. Even though the following work gives an elaboratge treatment to health - subjects, yet it is not done in the cold light of purposeless materialistic science. On the other hand, ideas common to allreligions and philosophies are brought to enliven and ennoble the subject and I trust that, after reading it once, one would find his or her mind greatly refreshed and exalted.
A work of this kind is quite new to the Tamil people and it may even seem strange to many a seemingly religious person that it should give such an explicit treatment to such avoided subjects as those that concern sex-functions and birth-control. Perceiving the evil of concealing such vital matters from the knowledge of people, even women of high attinments, and even men of great religious distinctions have, in the foreign countries, come forward to speak and write on those subjects without any reserve. Marie Stopes, and E.A. Rout’s deservingly famous writings are familiar instances in point. And there is not a greater and stronger upholder of the needs of birh-control than the illustrious Dean Inge of St. Paul’s, nor is there a deeper and more logical thinker and exponent of the science of eugenics than Dr. F.C.S. Sehiller of Oxford. “Eugenists believe” says Dean Inge “that unless civilisation is guided on scientific principles, it must come to ruin. We are ready to give up all our theories, if we can be proved to be in the wrong; but we stand by scientific as aginst emotional or sentimental ethics” (outspoken Essays, Second  Series). And Dr. Chiller lays greater emphasis on this fact by affirming However powerful, therefore, a society may be, and however great its resources, it is doomed if it so organizes itself is to breed the wrong sort of men and to favour the survival of the worthless at the expense of the more valuable” (Eugenics and Politics).
Ages ago our own country was not wanting in wise men who perceived the evil of indiscriminate procreation. St. Thirumoolar who existed in the 5th century A.D., not only speaks of what ought to be the right sort of sex - functions, but also indicates the conditions under which a limited number of choice offspring is producible. From this it just be clear that, in treating explicitly of sexual functions and birth - control methods, I have not don4e anything offensive aginst delicacy of taste but have simply voiced forth that the great men of the past and the present thought and aid on the subject.
Another prominent feature of this work is its strong advocacy of nature - cure methods. If man can learn to make a proper use of air, light, heat, water and dwelling places, if he can live only upon such vegetable foods is contin all the elements required for the building up of his body, if he can keep clean the excretory organs without allowing the used - up matter to remin in them long, if he can fast occasionally so as to give rest to his over-wrought organ the stomach, if he can keep his blood free from impurities and contamination, and lastly if he can exercise his mind and body to an extent beneficent but not fatiguing, there is no reason why he should not live a helahty long life and why he should abandon the use of medicines altogether. One who has acquired the habit of living according to the principles lid down in this book need no longer fear either disease or untimely death. If, by unforeseen circumstances, anything goes wrong with him, all that he must do is simply to adopt the simple, inexpensive and natural methods of healing shown in the last chapters of this book and the cure will positively follow as a matter of course. With these few words  I send out this most useful work of mine for the benefit of the Tamil people and offer my humble prayer to God for having enabled me to complete it after so long.
The Sacred Order of Love,	Vedachalam
Pallavaram,
13th Feb. 1933.
