mācittiṅkaḷ, 3 ām nāḷ

Preface
“On  a natural system of diet, old age would be our last and our only malady; he term of our existence would be protracted; we should enjoy life and no longer preclude others from the enjoyment of it.”
- Poet Shelly in “A Vindication of Natural Diet”
The following second large work of mine (my first large work being “The Life and Times of St Manickavachakar) in Tamil embodies the results of my careful study of Long Life problems, of my observations of symptoms in myself and in others in health and in disease, and of my experimental modes of living an dmethods of healing pursued continually for more than thirty years. Nothing but what has been found in all these thirty years  to benefit others and myself is set down in the following pages. Before recommending to others what I read and thought about how to mintin a healthy life and how to restore it in cases where it was impired, I tried them all on myself and on my people undergoing of course considerable trouble and expending large sums of money all through, until I came to hit upon the very simple, natural, correct, inexpensive modes of leading and mintining a healthy long life. Although it took me twenty four years to complete this treatise owing to interruptions occasioned by my other literary, religious and historical works, yet it has gined - much in my long varied experince, wide study and progressive thinking.
Here in India as elsewhere, it is generally believed at living long does not lie within the power of man. Most of the people in India attribute to the power of late  both a short life and a long one. Only a few hold to the view that man has infinite possibilities in store, if he would only care to exercise his will, and that according as he energizes it or leaves it dormant, does he either lengthen it or leaves it dormant, does he either lengthen his life’s duration or shorten it to a span. These few are mostly the adherente of the yoga system of philosophy. In ancient times not only but so in Greece, the followers  of Kapila and Patanjali, and Pythagoros practised strict yogic modes of living and thinking and eventually enjoyed a long life extending to hundred years and more.
	But in recent times when the practice of yoga and therewith the natural ways of living and the loftier ways of thinking had the more fallen into disuse, as the people became more and more illiterate, ignorant and aggressively selfish indulging in animal passions and aggressively selfish indulging in animal passions and morbidly craving fpr innatural fllds and drinks, the human life also has become hideously short. Most horrible forms of disease have multiplied and great sufferings and countless premature deaths do occur. Still they pass unnoticed simply as ineviable occurrences. The yogic mode of living has even come to be looked upon as belonging exclusively to the ascetic few.
	But, towards the end of the last century Saint amalinga in Southern India and Saint RamaKrishna and his great and illustrious disciple Swami Vivekananda in Northern India had, by their impressive teachings and thrilling lectures, revived the study and practice of Yoga to an appreciable degree and showed how all who are willing might share in the benefits of Yoga.
	Still earlier in far- off countries Newton the great discoverer  of scientific laws, struck deeply by the fact lf the wonderfully long life led by some men-especially by Thlmas Parr who lived 153 years, and Henry Jenkins 169, devoted his attention to a study of the habits and diets of the long lived persons and found out the great law existing between the purity and simplicity of the former and the long life of the latter. He then wrote a “luminous and the eloquent essay” on Defence of Vegetable Regimen expounding the principles that underlie the perpetuation of life, and thus gave a new impetus to the investigation  of laws governing the length of human life. After the time of Newton, the question of clean food and its intimate relation to a healthy long life, touched the finer feelings of so great an  angelic soul as the poet Shelley and so much excited his compassion for human and animal life that he promptly wrote a thoughtful, sound and brilliant essay entitled. “ A Vindication of Natural Diet” and disseminated his precious ideas all over the foreign countries.
	From the time of Shelly this important subject began to engage the attention of many great scientists such as Louis Kuhne, Dr. A. Haig, Prof. Metchnikoff, Prof. Loeb, Dr. Albert Matthews, and Dr. J. H. Kellogg and of such great thinkers and philosophers as Prentice Mulford, Helen Wilmans, Harry Gaze and others. Dr. Haig’s book on “Uric Acid” and Dr. Kellogg’s “The New Dietetics” are teally epoch-making works in as much as they bring to clear light the hitherto hidden fact relating to food and life. The number of learned people who are greatly benefited by the teachings of these eminent authorities is dily increasing by tens and hundreds of thousands in foreign countries, while here in Southern India those who know even the names of these savants are extremely rare.
	When this vital knowledge is in such a deplorable condition even among the English-educated people of this country, is it to be wondered at for its being mjch more so among others who are almost illiterate and who only speak Tamil but know not either to read or write any thing in it? That the increasing number of the English- educated persons should, after completing their university course, go hunting after posts in Government Offices and ilsewhere, mostly not with any honourable intention but with an avidity to scrape up as much as they can the people’s money, that they should vie with each other in spending it as much in adorning their females with costly diamond jewels and silk cloths of every high grade as in other vin luxuries, that they should take no thought of the poor, suffering and prematurely dying millions in the country, of their language and religion and their social amelioration, is really a heart-rending spectacle. 
It was this lamentable state of affairs that at first roused in me a feeling of boldness to layout the limited sources of my income in the acquisition and propagation of knowledge pertining to the science of health, although this to a certin extent interfered with my study of English, Tamil and Sanscrit literatues, of the different systems of philosophy and different religious systems of the world. Even though the following work gives an elaboratge treatment to health - subjects, yet it is not done in the cold light of purposeless materialistic science. On the other hand, ideas common to allreligions and philosophies are brought to enliven and ennoble the subject and I trust that, after reading it once, one would find his or her mind greatly refreshed and exalted.
A work of this kind is quite new to the Tamil people and it may even seem strange to many a seemingly religious person that it should give such an explicit treatment to such avoided subjects as those that concern sex-functions and birth-control. Perceiving the evil of concealing such vital matters from the knowledge of people, even women of high attinments, and even men of great religious distinctions have, in the foreign countries, come forward to speak and write on those subjects without any reserve. Marie Stopes, and E.A. Rout’s deservingly famous writings are familiar instances in point. And there is not a greater and stronger upholder of the needs of birh-control than the illustrious Dean Inge of St. Paul’s, nor is there a deeper and more logical thinker and exponent of the science of eugenics than Dr. F.C.S. Sehiller of Oxford. “Eugenists believe” says Dean Inge “that unless civilisation is guided on scientific principles, it must come to ruin. We are ready to give up all our theories, if we can be proved to be in the wrong; but we stand by scientific as aginst emotional or sentimental ethics” (outspoken Essays, Second  Series). And Dr. Chiller lays greater emphasis on this fact by affirming However powerful, therefore, a society may be, and however great its resources, it is doomed if it so organizes itself is to breed the wrong sort of men and to favour the survival of the worthless at the expense of the more valuable” (Eugenics and Politics).
Ages ago our own country was not wanting in wise men who perceived the evil of indiscriminate procreation. St. Thirumoolar who existed in the 5th century A.D., not only speaks of what ought to be the right sort of sex - functions, but also indicates the conditions under which a limited number of choice offspring is producible. From this it just be clear that, in treating explicitly of sexual functions and birth - control methods, I have not don4e anything offensive aginst delicacy of taste but have simply voiced forth that the great men of the past and the present thought and aid on the subject.
Another prominent feature of this work is its strong advocacy of nature - cure methods. If man can learn to make a proper use of air, light, heat, water and dwelling places, if he can live only upon such vegetable foods is contin all the elements required for the building up of his body, if he can keep clean the excretory organs without allowing the used - up matter to remin in them long, if he can fast occasionally so as to give rest to his over-wrought organ the stomach, if he can keep his blood free from impurities and contamination, and lastly if he can exercise his mind and body to an extent beneficent but not fatiguing, there is no reason why he should not live a helahty long life and why he should abandon the use of medicines altogether. One who has acquired the habit of living according to the principles lid down in this book need no longer fear either disease or untimely death. If, by unforeseen circumstances, anything goes wrong with him, all that he must do is simply to adopt the simple, inexpensive and natural methods of healing shown in the last chapters of this book and the cure will positively follow as a matter of course. With these few words  I send out this most useful work of mine for the benefit of the Tamil people and offer my humble prayer to God for having enabled me to complete it after so long.
The Sacred Order of Love,	Vedachalam
Pallavaram,
13th Feb. 1933.

ōm
1. nīṇṭa vāḻkki
	eṇṇariya piṟavitaṉiṉ māṉiṭap piṟavi tāṉ
	   yātiṉum aritaritu kāṇ
eṉa āṉṟōr moḻintavāṟu inta makkaṭ piṟaviyik kāṭṭiṉuñ ciṟantatu vēṟilli. kaṭavuḷālē inta nilavulakat til vikkap paṭṭirukkum ellā naṉ poruḷkaḷiyum nukarntu iṉpuṟutaṟkum. namatu aṟivi vaḷarac ceytu avvaṟiviṉ oḷiyālē nammip paṭitta avvellām valla kaṭavuḷiṉ tiruvaruṭ pēriṉpak kaṭalil muḻuki eṉṟum aḻiyāp pēriṉpatti eyti iruttaṟkuṅ karuviyāyiruppatu inta makkaṭ piṟaviyēyām. im maṇṇulakattil aḷaviṉṟip peruki yirukkum palavaki iṉpaṅkaḷiyum nukara aṟiyāmal, iṉpam allātavaṟṟi iṉpamākap piṟaḻa uṇarntu avaṟṟi aṭitaṟkup pukuntu immakkaṭ piṟaviyi iḻantōrum iḻakkiṉṟōrum iḻap pōruṅ kaṭal maṇali aḷaviṭiṉum aḷaviṭappaṭār! maṇṇulaka iṉpatti uḷḷavāṟu aṟintu ataṉi yaṭiyātu uyir iḻantōr. viṇṇulaka iṉpattiyum iḻantu, ataṟkum mēṟpaṭṭa kaṭavuḷiṉ tiruvaruḷiṉ pattiyum iḻantuviṭu vareṉpatu tiṇṇam.
atumaṭṭumā! avar tāṅ keṭuvatallāmalun tammic cērnta maṉivi, makkaḷ, uṟṟār, uṟaviṉar, naṇpar mutalāyiṉāriyuṅ keṭuttuviṭukiṉṟaṉar! evvāṟeṉiṉ. uṭampiṉkaṇ uḷḷa elumpu, tōl, narampu, cati, cennīr, mutalāṉa karuvikaḷ cevviyāṉa niliyil iruppatuṭaṉ. avvuṭampiṉ uḷ niṉṟu ulāvum uyiriṉ niṉivukaḷun tūyaṉavāyiruntāl. oruvaṉ ciṟitēṟak kuṟiya nūṟu āṇṭu uyirōṭiruttal tiṇṇam. aṅṅaṉamiṉṟi avi nili piṟaḻntu pōṉāl avaṉ ciṟu paruvattilēyē uyir tuṟappaṉ. uṭampiluḷḷa karuvikaḷ cīr kulintupōṉatu teriyāmal, allatu terintālum ataṉāṟ piṉ viḷiyun tīmi iṉṉateṉṟu pakuttaṟiyāmal oruvaṉ oru peṇṇi maṇañceytu koḷvāṉāṉāl, avaṉ taṉatu cērkki yāl ap peṇṇiṉ uṭampiyum uyiriyum nilikuliyac ceytu viṭukiṉṟāṉ. avvāṟu avaṉum avaḷum nili kulintu niṟkuṅ kālattil avarkaṭkup piḷḷi piṟantāl. ataṉāl antap piḷḷiyiṉ uṭampum uyiruñ cīrkeṭṭa niliyi yaṭintu. ataṉāṟ palavaki nōykaṭkum āḷāyk kuḻantip paruvattilō, atu tavaṟiṉāl iḷamip paruvattilō, atuvun tavaṟiṉāṟ kaṭṭiḷamip paruvattilō māṇṭu pōkiṉṟatu. ivvāṟu piḷḷiyun tuṉpuṟṟu iṟakka ippiḷḷiyip peṟṟārun tuṉpuṟṟu iṟakka, ivarkaḷic cērnta uṟṟār, uṟaviṉar, naṇpar eṉṉum ivarellām maṉanōy mikap peṟu kiṉṟaṉar. maṉa nōy mikap peṟavē. avarkaḷ uṭampiṉ uḷ ōṭuñ cennīrum nañcāka ellāk karuvikaḷum nilikeṭṭup pōkiṉṟaṉa. ākavē. avarkaḷum nalamiḻantu kālam mutirā muṉṉarē māḷkiṉṟaṉar. kalappālilē kuṟṟiṉa ciṟu pirit tuḷiyāṉatu ap pāl muḻumiyum māṟupaṭuttit tayirākku tal pōlat, tūya nalvāḻkki yuṭiya makkaḷ naṭuvilē oruvaṉ aṭinta cīr kēṭāṉatu am makkaḷ ellāriyuñ cīrkeṭap paṇṇum āṟṟaluḷḷatā yirukkiṉṟatu. ākiyāl tāṉ keṭuvatal lāmalun taṉṉāṟ piṟar keṭuvatiyum uṇarntatāyiṉum ovvoruvarun tam muyiriyum uṭampiyun tūya naṉṉiliyil vittu. ataṉāṟṟam vāḻnāḷi nīḷac ceytu, nōyaṟṟa tiṇṇiya yākki yiṉarāṉa makkaḷip peṟṟu. palarkkum payaṉpaṭuvārāy. immiyil ivvulaka iṉpaṅkaḷi muṟṟavum aṭintu maṟumiyiluṅ kaṭavuḷiṉ tiruvaruṭ pēriṉpap perukkiṟ ṟiḷittiruttalaṉṟō immakkaṭ piṟavi peṟṟataṟkup payaṉām? āṉāl, māntarkaḷ nūṟāṇṭu variyil tiṇṇiya nal yākkiyuṭaṉ uyir vāḻtal kūṭumō eṉṟu cilarkku aiyan tōṉṟalām. ciṟu paruvattilēyē kālaṉukku iriyākum ikkālattār palarkku itu putumiyāyt tōṉṟiṉum, āṇṭiṉ mutirnta cilariṭam avarkaḷ itip paṟṟik kēṭṭāl, itu muṉṉellām eṅkum paraviyirunta toru nikaḻcciyātal pulappaṭum. ettaṉiyō peyar nūṟāṇṭu variyil uyirōṭiruntārkaḷ; ippōtum nūṟāṇṭuḷḷa mutiyōr palarik kāṇalām; nūṟāṇṭukkumēl uyir vāḻvār cilariyum kāṇalām. amerikkā tēyattil nūṟāṇṭum nūṟṟukku mēṟpaṭṭa āṇṭum uyir vāḻum mutiyōr palar cērntu oru kaḻakam vittirup patāka nām aṭuttaṭuttuk kēḷvippaṭukiṉṟōm. makkaḷiṉ vāḻnāḷ nīṭṭatti uṇmiyāṉ ārāyntu aṟintu nūl eḻutiṉa jēm īṭaṉ1  eṉpār oruvar nūṟāṇṭum ataṟkumēlum uyir vāḻnta āyirattu eḻunūṟṟup paṉṉiraṇṭu periyāriṉ varalāṟukaḷi varintiruk kiṉṟār
jōnātaṉhatā2  eṉṉum āṅkila turimakaṉ oruvar nūṟṟumuppattōrāṇṭu uyir vāḻntaṉarām. avar aintu muṟi maṇañ ceytu koṇṭu ēḻu piḷḷikaḷiyum. irupattāṟu pērap piḷḷikaḷiyum, nūṟṟu nāṟpatu pērap piḷḷikaḷiṉ piḷḷikaḷiyum viṭṭu iṟantaṉarām. tāmapār3  eṉṉum maṟṟumōr āṅkilēyar nūṟṟu aṟupattiraṇṭu āṇṭu uyirō ṭiruntaṉarām. ivar tamatu nūṟṟirupatām āṇṭilē iraṇṭāvatu maṇañ ceytu koṇṭaṉarām. rāparṭ jeṉkiṉ4  eṉṉum vēṟoru turimakaṉ nūṟṟimpatu āṇṭuvariyil uyir vāḻntaṉa reṉṟum, nuṟṟu nāṟpatu āṇṭukāṟum vayalil uḻavu vēli ceytaṉa reṉṟum aṟikiṉṟōm. kāpṭaṉ kāṭarṭ ṭimaṇṭu5 eṉpavar `nīṇṭa nāḷ uyir vāḻvateppaṭi eṉṉum nūloṉṟu eḻutit tammuṭiya vāḻkki muṟikaḷip palarkkuṅ kaṟpittu vantatōṭu tāmum nūṟṟup paṉṉiraṇṭāṇṭu uyir vāḻntaṉarām. pirāñcu tēyattil ṣeṉsi apalavā6 eṉṉun turimakaḷ orutti nūṟṟeṇpa tāṇṭu uyirōṭu irunta tallāmalun, taṉatu muṭivukālam variyil taṉ illap paṇi muḻutun tāṉē pārttuvantaṉaḷām. ivvāṟē iṉṉum neṭunāḷ uyirōṭiruntōr peyar kaḷik kuṟippiṭap pukuntāl, itumikavum viriyumātalāl iṅkē eṭuttuk kāṭṭiya peyarkaḷē, nām colvatu uṇmi yeṉpataṉiyum atu veṟuñ cellāyp pōkāmal iṉṉum meyyāka uḷḷa teṉpataṉiyum niliniṟuttutaṟkup pōtumāṉavi kaḷākum.
iṉṉum muṉṉamē palavakiyāl tamatu uṭampic cīraḻittu aḻintupōm niliyilirukkum perumpālār, av vaḻiyiṉiṉṟun tappit tamatuṭalatti urampeṟac ceytu nīṇṭanāḷ uyir vāḻvataṟku vaḻivakikaḷ uṇṭō illiyō eṉṟu aiyuṟṟut tikippaṭiyalām. uṇmi yāka nōkkuṅkāl avarkaḷ avvāṟu tikip paṭiyak kāraṇamilliyeṉpatu emakkut tōṉṟukiṟatu. airōppiyar oruvar kāmaṅ kaṭkuṭi mutalāṉa tīya tuṟikaḷiṟ pukuntu. nāṟpattēḻu nāṟpat teṭṭu āṇṭukāṟun tamatu uṭampi mikavum nili kuliyac ceytu. iṟutik kālatti etir pārttirukkun taṟuvāyil, oru pēraṟiñar uṭampi urappaṭuttum vakiyaṟivit teḻutiya nūl oṉṟu avar kikkeṭṭiyatu. an nūlip paṭitta nēran toṭaṅki av virōppiyar tan tīya oḻukkaṅkaḷi yellām aṟavē viṭṭu, an nūliṟ collappaṭṭa muṟikaḷi ellām vaḻuvātu taḻuvi naṭakkat talippaṭṭār. atu mutal avaratuṭalam maṟupaṭiyun tiruttam aṭintu iṟuki nōy mutalāṉa evvakik kuṟṟaṅkaḷum aṇukap peṟāmalum. aṇukiṉālum avi neṭunāḷ niṟkap peṟāmalum nalamāka nīṇṭakālam vāḻntaṉarām. itippōṉṟa nikaḻccikaḷ iṉṉam ettaṉiyō uṇṭu. ākiyāl, āṇṭu mutirn tamiyāl, uṭampu taḷarntanilikku vantālum, atupaṟṟi maṉakkiḷarcci kuṟiyāmal in nūlil nammāṟ collappaṭum muṟikaḷi vaḻuvātu piṉpaṟṟi naṭappār kaḷāṉāl. tirumpavun tamatuṭampi urampeṟac ceytu ellā makkaḷum iṉitu vāḻvareṉpatu tiṇṇam
itu koṇṭu cilar vēṟu vakiyākavum niṉittal kūṭum. āṇṭu mutirnta piṟarum uṭampi uram peṟac ceytal kūṭumākiyāl, iḷam paruvattil nām virumpiyapaṭi yellāṅ kāmaṅ, kaṭkuṭi mutaliyavaṟṟil vēṇṭu maṭṭum muḻuki, ataṉpiṟaku yākkiyi valivākka muyalalām eṉṟāl. avvāṟu eṇṇupavarkaḷ antō ēmāntu pōvārkaḷ! oruvaratu uṭampiṉnili maṟṟoruvaratu uṭampiṉ niliyip pōl irātu, kāmaṅ kaṭkuṭi mutaliya tīya tuṟikaḷil muḻukiyuñ cilaruṭampu muṟṟum valivu kuṉṟāmalikkum; cilaruṭampu at tuṟikaḷiṟ pukunta aḷaviṉālē avaṟṟiṉ mikuti poṟāmal annoṭiyē aḻiyiṉum aḻiyum. cilaruṭampu mutalil am mikutiyit tāṅkuvatu pōṟ kāṭṭippiṉ caṭutiyilē atu tāṅka māṭṭāmal aḻiyiṉum aḻiyum. ātalāl  oruvaṉ taṉṉuṭampiṉ niliyit tāṉē tiṇṇamāka aṟintu koḷḷutal aritiṉum aritākum! evvaḷavutāṉ tīya neṟiyil yāṉ oḻukiṉālum. eṉ uṭampu ciṟitun taḷarā teṉṟu uṟutiyāyc colla evaṉukkum nāveḻātu. ēṉeṉil, aṅṅaṉañ colliya cilar tāmaṟiyāmaṟ caṭutiyilē māṇṭupōyatik kaṇkūṭākak kaṇṭiruk kiṉṟōm. vaḻiyiṟ cellumpōtē ētu kāraṇamumiṉṟit tiṭīreṉak kīḻviḻuntu iṟantōr ettaṉi! aṟa maṉṟaṅkaḷil vaḻakkārāyntu koṇrukkumpōtē uyir tuṟantōr ettaṉi! muṉ nāḷ iravu uṟaṅkap pōyp paṭukkiyiṟ kiṭantu, piṉṉāḷ atiliruntu eḻāmaṟ piṇamāyk kiṭantōr ettaṉi! uṇavu koḷḷappōyiruntu oru piṭiyuṇṭu piṉṉorupiṭiyum uṇṇātu māṇṭōr ettaṉi! ivikaḷiyellām eṇṇum pōtu,
uṟaṅkuvatu pōluñ cākkāṭu uṟaṅki
viḻippatu pōlum piṟappu	(kuṟaḷ 339)
eṉṟum,
puṉṉuṉimēl nīrpōl niliyāmi yeṉṟeṇṇi
iṉṉiṉiyē ceyka aṟaviṉi
eṉṟum, makkaḷ vāḻkkiyiṉ niliyāmiyiṉic colliya cemmoḻikaḷ uṇmiyē yeṉpatu tōṉṟa villiyā?
avvāṟāṉāl nām nīṇṭa nāḷ uyirvāḻa muyalvatu payaṉiṉṟāy muṭiyum pōlumeṉiṟ, caṭutiyilē cāvu nērntālum ac cāviṟkuk kāraṇamāy muṉṉamē oruvaṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa tīya ceyalkaḷum oḻuṅkiṉmiyum palavā yirukkum. avaṟṟi avaṉ aṟintu vilakka muyalāmal nām eṉṟum ivvāṟē yiruppōmeṉṟu eṇṇit tāṉ muṉ ceytavaṟṟiyē piṉṉuñ ceytu varuvāṉāyiṉ, taṉṉiya ṟiyāmalē tāṉ caṭutiyil iṟantu paṭuvāṉ. ākiyāl ovvoruvarun tamatu ciṟu paruvam mutaṟ koṇṭē naṟceyal kaḷic ceytu oḻuṅku tavāṟamal naṭantu varuvārkaḷāṉāl avvāṟu avarkaḷ caṭutiyil iṟantupōkac ciṟituṅ kāraṇamē yilli. nīṇṭa kālam uyirōṭiruntuvarum mutiyōr tam oḻukalāṟumitaṟku ottatākavē yirukkiṉṟatu.
avvāṟāṉāl nammuṭiya ceyalākavallāmal. iṭi miṉṉal, maḻi, tī, nila atircci, pāmpu, puli mutaliya maṟṟip poruḷkaḷālum uyirkaḷālum etirpārāta cākkāṭu nikaḻak kāṇkiṉṟōmē; itaṉi vilakkal eṅṅaṉaṅ kūṭumeṉṟāl, nammaṉōr naṟceyalum nalleṇṇamum uṭiyarāy naṭantu; aruntal, poruntal, uṟaṅkal, uṭuttal mutaliya vaki kaḷilellām oḻuṅkākavun tuppuravākavum naṭattalik kikkoṇṭu varuvār kaḷāṉāl, attakiyōr aṟivu ivvulakamellām paṭittuk kāttu varukiṉṟa kaṭavuḷiṉ tiruvaruḷoḷiyil iyalpākavē aḻunti niṟkum; aṅṅaṉam aruḷoḷiyil aḻuntiniṟkum aṟivilē, piṉvarappōkun tīya nikaḻccikaḷ tiṇṇamāy muṟkoṇṭē viḷaṅkit tōṉṟum. itaṟku uṇmiyāṉa nikaḻccikaḷ kaṇakkillāmal nikaḻntirukkiṉṟaṉa. ari nūṟṟāṇṭukkumuṉ maturiyil oru turimakaṉār aṅkayaṟkaṇ ammiyiṭattil aḷavillāta aṉpuṭiyarāyun tūya niṉivuṭiyarāyum oḻukivantār. avar oriravu tam makkaḷ maṉiviyaroṭu tamatu vīṭṭiṉ mēṉmāḷikiyil uṟaṅkik koṇṭirunta pōtu. aḻakiya uruvattōṭuṅ kūṭiyoru ciṟu peṇ tōṉṟi. `inta mēṉmāḷiki iṭintu viḻappōkiṉṟatu, nī niṉ maṉivi makkaḷōṭuṅ kīḻ iṟaṅkippō eṉṟu collik kiyip piṭittiḻuppatākak kaṉavu kaṇṭu, uṭaṉē at turimakaṉār viḻitteḻuntu avar ellārōṭuṅ kīḻ iṟaṅki appuṟam pōṉa anta nēramē ammāḷiki iṭintu viḻuntatu. avarkaḷ ellārum uyir tappip piḻittārkaḷ.
iṉi, emmuṭiya vāḻnāṭkaḷil iṅṅaṉamē nērntu varum nikaḻccikaḷ pala uṇṭu. orumuṟi yām palamaṉēri yeṉṉum ūriṟ kuṟuṅkāṭṭiṉ naṭuviluḷḷa māḷiki oṉṟiṟ ṟaṅkiyirukkumāṟu nērntatu. orunāḷ iravu atil yām paṭuttu uṟaṅkukiyil oru pāmpu vantu ematu kiyiṟ kuvuvatākak kaṉavu kaṇṭu, caṭutiyil viḻittup pārkka, uṇmiyākavē oru karumpāmpu ematu valatukim mēṟ puṟattik kuvip palliaḻuttam niliyiliruntatu. uṭaṉē ataṉit tappi appuṟam eḻuntupōy uyir piḻittēm, maṟṟoru muṟi tirunelvēliyil oru viṭṭil takkār palarōṭu nalluri pēcik koṇṭu cuvarmēṟ cāyntu uṭkārntiruntēm. avvāṟirukkiyil, antac cuvariṉ aṭiyil oru tēḷ iruppatāka emakkuc caṭutiyilē oru niṉivu tōṉṟiṟṟu; uṭaṉē pēcci niṟutti appuṟam nakarntu tirumpip pārkkap, periya karuntēḷ oṉṟu emakkup piṉaṉē iruntatu; ataṟkun tiruvaruḷutaviyāl tappip piḻittēm, iṅṅaṉam yām tappip piḻittu varukiṉṟa nikaḻccikaḷ pala uṇṭu. avaṟṟiyellām iṅku virit teḻutal vēṇṭā kūṟalākum, ivvāṟē maṟṟiyōr palarkkum muṉṉaṟi vippukaḷ tōṉṟi avarkaḷi uyir piḻippittirukkiṉṟaṉa. ākiyāl, nammi ivipōṉṟa iṭarkaḷiṉiṉṟum viṭuvittup pātukāttaṟku ellām valla iṟivaṉ ṟiruvaruḷāṉatu kāttuk koṇṭirukkiṉṟatu. ākavē, nām ceyya vēṇṭuṅ kaṭamikaḷi vaḻuvātu ceytu varutaluṭaṉ, iṟivaṉṟiruvaruḷ nammip pātukāttu varukiṉṟatu eṉṉum oru muḻu nampikkiyum namakku iṭiyaṟātu uṇṭākumāṉāl, nām caṭutiyil varum iṭarkaḷukkut tappip piḻippō meṉpatu tiṇṇam
avvāṟāṉāl ellāruḷḷumiruntu uṇartti varukiṉṟa tiruvaru ḷāṉatu cilarkku maṭṭum piṉvarum iṭarkaḷi muṉṉaṟivippatum, palarkku avvāṟu aṟivi yātatum ēṉ eṉiṉ; ellāruḷḷum at tiruvaruḷ oḷi viḷaṅkik koṇṭiruntāluñ, cilar maṭṭum ataṉi akan tirumpip pārkkiṉṟārkaḷ, palar ataṉi aṅṅaṉan tirumpip pārk kiṉṟārkaḷilli. ñāyiṟṟiṉoḷi muṉṉē kaṇṇillāta kuruṭar nāṭōṟum iruntālum ataṉi avar kāṇamāṭṭu vārallar kaṇṇuṭiyavarkaḷē ataṉik kaṇṭu payaṉaṭi vārkaḷ. atupōla, makkaḷiṟ perumpālār uḷḷoḷi oṉṟirukkiṉṟa teṉṟukūṭa niṉiyāmaṟ, palari ēmāṟṟip poruḷ tokuppatiluṅ, kāmaṅ kaṭkuṭi mutalāṉa tīyavoḻukkaṅkaḷit taḻuvuvatiluñ, cōmpaluṟṟuk kiṭappatilun, tīya ceytikaḷiyē niṉippatilun tamatu uṇarvik kaḻiyaviṭukiṉṟā rātalāl, avar tamatu akak kaṇ kuruṭupaṭṭu avvuḷḷoḷiyik kāṇātu pōkiṉṟatu. tammuṭiya niṉivuñ ceyalun tīya neṟiyiṟ cellāmal aviyiṟṟi aṭakki, uḷ niṉṟa aṟivoḷiyi nōkka muyaṉṟu varupavarkaḷukku akakkaṇ tiṟakku mātalāl, avarkaḷ ataṉ utaviyāṟ piṉvarum nikaḻccikaḷi muṉṉē uṇarntu iṉitu vāḻvārkaḷ. iv vakakkaṇṇit tiṟappataṟkuc ceyya vēṇṭum paḻakkaṅkaḷi in nūliṉ piṟpākattil viḷakkik kāṭṭuvām.
iṉi, nīṇṭanāḷ uyirvāḻntiruttaṟkuk kippaṟṟa vēṇṭiya muṟikaḷ uṭampip paṟṟiyaṉavum uyirip paṟṟiyaṉavum eṉa iraṇṭu vaki uṇṭu. avaṟṟuḷ muntiyatu uṭampip paṟṟiya muṟiyātalāl. am muṟiyuḷ mutal niṟpatāṉa pirāṇavāyuvip paṟṟi aṭutta iyaliṟ pēcap pōkiṉṟām.
aṭikkuṟippukaḷ
1.	James Easton
2.	Jonathan Hatha
3.	Thomas Paar
4.	Robert Jenkins
5.	Captin Goddard Dimond
6.	Shensi Abalava
2. pirāṇa vāyu : uyirkkāṟṟu
nām inta ulakattil neṭuṅkālam uyirvāḻa vēṇṭumāṉāl namatu uṭampiṟku iṉṟiyamiyātu vēṇṭiya nukar poruḷkaḷit tarutal vēṇṭum. avi ellā vaṟṟilum mikac ciṟantatu pirāṇavāyuvēyākum. taṇṇīr aruntāmaluñ cōṟu uṇṇāmalum, vēṟu tiṉpaṇṭaṅkaḷ uṭkoḷḷāmalum makkaḷum maṟṟa uyirkaḷum palanāḷ uyirōṭiruttal kūṭum. aṉāl, uyirkkāṟṟu illāmalō enta uyirum arinimiṭaṅ kūṭa uyirōṭiruttal muṭiyātu. uṭampip pātukāttaṟku vēṇṭiya mutaṟ poruḷkaḷ attaṉiyum pirāṇa vāyuvilēyē irukkiṉṟaṉa. maṟṟetaṉiyumē uṭ koḷḷāmal uyirkkāṟṟi maṭṭum uṇavākak koṇṭu ettaṉiyō kōṭi uyirkaḷ uyir vāḻntu varukiṉṟaṉa, tava muyaṟciyil mutircci peṟṟa tuṟavikaḷ kāṟṟi uṇavāka uṭkoṇṭu uyirōṭiruttali nām paṉmuṟiyuṅ kēḷvippaṭṭirukkiṉṟōm aṉṟō? nalla pāmpāṉatu vēṟu iri tiṉṉāmaṟ kāṟṟi maṭṭum uṭkoṇṭu āṟu tiṅkaḷukkumēl uyirōṭirunta tiṟam yām ettaṉiyō muṟi kēḷvippaṭṭirukkiṉṟōm.iv vuṇmikaḷi nōkkuṅkāl uyirkkāṟṟē namatu uyir vāḻkkikku mikavum iṉṟiyamiyāta teṉpatu iṉitu viḷaṅkum.
āṉāl, nammavarkaḷiṟ perumpālār uyirk kāṟṟiṉ ciṟappiyum, atu namatu uṭampiṟku evvaḷavu iṉṟiyami yāta teṉpatiyum, ataṉi enta vakiyāṟ payaṉpaṭuttik koḷḷal vēṇṭumeṉpatiyum  aṟintu koḷḷāmaṟ pala vakippaṭṭa nōykaḷukku āḷāki āṇṭu mutirā muṉṉarē iṟantupōkiṉṟārkaḷ. nuriīralip paṟṟiya nōykaḷum. mārpu nōyuñ ceriyāmiyum, īḷi yirumalum, iṉṉum ivi pōṉṟa koṭiya nōykaḷum uyirkkāṟṟip payaṉpaṭuttik koḷḷum vaki aṟiyāta varkaḷukku varukiṉṟaṉa. in nōykaḷāl ettaṉikōṭi makkaḷ māṇṭupōṉārkaḷ! evarum uyirkkāṟṟi maṭṭum naṉṟākap payaṉpaṭutti varuvārkaḷāṉāl, avarkaḷ tūya nalluṭampu uṭiyavarkaḷāki aḷavil kālam uyirvāḻn tiruppa reṉpatu tiṇṇam.
nākarikam mikuntu varuvatākac collappaṭum ikkālattil makkaḷ ceytu varuñ ceykikaḷ ellām perumpālum ulaka iyaṟkikikku māṟupaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa. ivvāṟu ivarkaḷ ceyki māṟupaṭṭu varutaliṉāṟṟāṉ kālaṅkuṟukip palavakip piṇikaḷukkum aḷāki māṇṭu pōkiṉṟārkaḷ. āṉāṟ, pallāyira āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ nammuṉṉōrkaḷ maraṅkaḷ mēṟ kaṭṭiya kuṭicikaḷilum, marappontukaḷilum, malik kukikaḷilum uṟintu koṇṭu, kāy kaṉi kiḻaṅkukaḷi uṇavāka aruntiyum, aruvinīr ūṟṟunīr parukiyum, veyiliṟ ṟirintum, maḻiyil naṉintun, tūyakāṟṟi uḷḷiḻuttum uyir vāḻnta kālattil, avarkaḷ evvakippaṭṭa nōyum ilarāy neṭuṅkālam uyirpiḻit tiruntaṉar. iṅṅaṉam ulaka iyaṟkikkup porunta vāḻkki celuttiya avarkaḷatu uṭampu kāḻpōl valivaṭintu uṭalvali maṉavaliyuḷḷa makkaḷi īṉṟatu. am muṉṉōr kāl vaḻiyil vantavarāṉa nāmō avviyaṟki yiṉiṉṟum vaḻuvi, ataṟku muṟṟum māṟāṉa vaḻikaḷiṟ kālaṅkaḻittu varukiṉṟōm. nam muṉṉōr irunta iṭaṅkaḷ āḷ nerukkam illāta kāṭukaḷum, malikaḷum, pāli veḷikaḷuṅ kaṭaṟkarikaḷumātalāl, avarkaḷ aṅkē tūyatāyuḷḷa uyirkkāṟṟi uḷḷiḻuttu vantārkaḷ, ippōtu nāmellām irukkum iṭaṅkaḷ āḷ nerukkam uḷḷaṉavāy iṇṭum iṭukkum pontum cakatiyum aḻukkam niṟintu. iṭiveḷi muḻutum aḻukkēṟap peṟṟiruttalāl nām aṅkēyuḷḷa naccukkāṟṟi uḷḷiḻuttu namatu uṭampiluḷḷa irattamellām nañcākumāṟu ceytu koḷkiṉṟōm.
namatuṭampiṉuḷḷē yiruntu kāṟṟi iḻuttalum viṭutaluñ ceyyum uṟuppu nuriyīral eṉṟu peyar peṟum. inta nuriyīraliṉ muḻuppākamum acintu uyirkkāṟṟi iḻuttu nirappi veḷiyiṭumāṉāl namatu uṭampiṟku nōyē varātu. nam muṉṉōrkaḷ irunta kālattila avarkaḷiṉ mārpiṉuḷḷēyirunta inta uṟuppiṉ muḻuppākamum acintu koṇṭē iruntatu. intap putu nākarika kālattilō veṉṟāl makkaḷiṟ perum pālārkku inta uṟuppu muḻutum acivatē illi; kāṟpaṅkō arikkāṟ paṅkōtāṉ acintu varukiṉṟatu. ataṟku ētu eṉṉeṉṟāl, tūya uyirkkāṟṟu niṟintu niṟkum iṭaṅkaḷil nām ak kāṟṟi mikutiyāka uḷḷiḻukka, nuriyīralāṉatu avvaḷavu kāṟṟukkum iṭaṅkoṭuttu akaṉṟu muḻumiyum acikiṉṟatu. naccuk kāṟṟu uḷḷa iṭaṅkaḷilō nām iyaṟkiyilēyē akkāṟṟi uḷḷiḻukkak kūṭāta nilimiyi aṭiva taṉāl, namatu nuriyīraluṅ kuṟinta kāṟṟiyuṭ koṇṭu muḻutum viriyātu kāṟpaṅku maṭṭumē acikiṉṟatu. ataṉāl aṅkuḷḷavaṉ tūyakāṟṟi iḻukkāmal varuntutalum nāṭōṟum naṅ kaṇṇetirē kāṇalām. paṉi nīr maṇam niṟinta iṭattil ovvoruvarum maṉam makiḻntu uyirk kāṟṟi mikutiyāka iḻukkiṉṟārkaḷ; pulāl nāṟṟamum malattiṉ nāṟṟamum niṟinta iṭattil uḷḷavarkaḷ aṅkuḷḷa kāṟṟi uḷḷiḻuppataṟku maṉam iciyātavarkaḷāy mūkkip piṭittuk koḷkiṉṟārkaḷ. nallatu, keṭṭa nāṟṟam vīcukiṉṟa iṭattil mutaṉmutaṟ pōṉāl maṭṭum atu pulappaṭu kiṉṟatē yallāmal, avviṭatti lēyē mikutiyāyiruppavar kaḷukku anta nāṟṟan tōṉṟā miyāl nuriyīral aṅku muḻutumē aciyāteṉṟu kūṟutal eṉṉiyeṉiṉ; tīya nāṟṟattil ennēramum paḻakutalāl annāṟṟittiṉi aṟiyum āṟṟal maṭṭum oruvaṉukku illāmaṟ pōkiṉṟatu; avaṉuṭiya nuriyīralō veṉṟāl avaṉ mutaṉ mutal anta nāṟṟak kāṟṟi iḻukka māṭṭāmal varuntiyapōtu, tāṉum akaṉṟu aciyāmaṟ kāṟpaṅkē acintu anta nāṟṟak kāṟṟukku iṭantantatu; ataṉpiṟakum aṭuttaṭuttu avaṉ aṅkē uḷḷa kāṟṟi uḷḷiḻuttapōtu ituvum aṭuttaṭuttuk kāṟ paṅkē acintu varalāyiṟṟu. avaṉ ataṉi yaṟiyā viṭṭālum, avaṉatu nuriyīral maṭṭum mikunta kāṟṟukku iṭantarāmaṟ curuṅkiyē pōkum ituvē paḻakkattiṉāl nuriyīralukku vanta tīṅku ākum. ivvāṟu nuriyīral curuṅki aciyap peṟṟa oruttiyiṉ vayiṟṟil  tōṉṟiya karuvāṉatu tūyakāṟṟu ulavāta tāyiṉ karup piyil iruttalāl, akkuḻaviyiṉ nuriyīralum viriyātu curuṅkik kuṟinta mūccaṭiyatā yirukkum. ikkuṟinta mūccacoṭupiṟanta piḷḷikaḷ īḷi irumal mutaliya nōykaḷāṟ paṟṟappaṭu kiṉṟaṉar; atu niṟka.
mūccuk kuṟiviṉāl nōyvarum eṉṟu colvatu eṅṅaṉam poruntumeṉiṉ; ataṉiyiṅkē viḷakkic colluvām; namatu uṭampiṉ uḷḷēyirukkiṉṟa ovvōr uṟuppiṟkum uṇavik koṇṭupōyc cērttu avaṟṟi valivupeṟac ceyvatu nalla civappāṉa irattamēyākum. inta irattamāṉatu uṇkiṉṟa uṇaviṉiṉṟum vaṭittup piritteṭukkap paṭukiṉṟa pāliṉatu tiripāl uṇṭākiṉṟatu, uṇṭa uṇavellān tīṉippiyuḷ viḻuntavuṭaṉē, aṅkēyuḷḷa mika nuṭpamāṉa karuvikaḷāl arikkapṭṭu avi teḷivuḷḷa pālākavun teḷivaṟṟa cakkiyākavum pirikiṉṟaṉa; teḷivuḷḷa pālāṉatu narappuk kuḻāykaḷiṉ vaḻiyāka mēlē neñcat tāmarikkuc celuttappaṭukiṉṟatu; aḵtu aṅṅaṉam neñcap pikkup pōṉavuṭaṉē irattamāka māṟutal aṭikiṉṟatu. teḷivaṟṟa cakkiyō malakkuṭaliṉ vaḻiyākavuñ ciṟunīrk kuṭaliṉ vaḻiyākavuṅ kīḻē veḷippaṭu kiṉṟatu. iṉi, neñcattāmariyiṉ neñcappikkup pakkattil nuriyīralirukkiṉṟatu, neñcappikku vanta uṇaviṉ vaṭipāl irattamāka māṟiyavuṭaṉ, avvirattamē namatuṭampu vaḷarccikkup pōtumāṉataṉṟu. avvirattam uyirk kāṟṟōṭu cērāta variyil, aḵtu uyirillāta uṭampu pōṟ payaṉpaṭamāṭṭātu. mēlum, uyirkkāṟṟōṭu cērkkappaṭṭāl maṭṭum irattattiṟ cūṭu uṇṭākum cūṭillāviṭṭāl irattam uṟintu kaṭṭiyākap pōy, uṭampu muḻutum paravamāṭṭātu. ākiyāl, uṇaviṉ vaṭipāl irattamāka māṟiyavuṭaṉē ataṟku amiḻtamum veppamuñ cērum poruṭṭu uyirkkāṟṟi uḷḷiḻukkum nuriyīralāṉatu neñcap piyiṉ pakkattilē amikkap paṭṭirukkiṉṟatu. veḷiyēyiruntu namatu uṭampiṉ uḷḷē celluṅ kāṟṟu uyirppu eṉappaṭum amiḻtak kūṟum veppiṉ kūṟum uṭiyatāka irukkiṉṟatu. atu nuriyīraliṟ pukuntavuṭaṉē ak kāṟṟiluḷḷa uyirp poruḷ irattattiṟ kalakkiṉṟatu. ivvāṟu uyirp poruḷoṭu kalanta irattam palakōṭi narampukaḷiṉ vaḻiyē ōṭi nam muṭampiluḷḷa ellā uṟuppukaḷiyum vaḷarttu varukiṉṟatu.
ivvaḷavu pērutaviyāṉa toḻilic ceyyum nuriyīral eṉṉuṅ karuviyāṉatu kāṟṟi uḷḷiḻuttu uyirpporuḷi uṭampiṟ paravac ceyvatu oṉṟu maṭṭumaṉṟu; aḵtu aṅkaṅkē irattattiṟ kalanta naccup poruḷiyum veḷippaṭutti utavi ceykiṉṟatu. uyirpporuḷic cumantu koṇṭu uṭampeṅkum paraviṉa irattamāṉatu, avvavvuṟuppiṟkum avvuyirp poruḷi ūṭṭiyavuṭaṉ, avvuṟuppukaḷ aṅkaṅkē kakkiṉa naccup poruḷi eṭuttuc cumantu koṇṭu maṟupaṭiyum neñcap pikku ōṭi varukiṉṟatu. aṅku vantatum ataṉ pakkattēyuḷḷa nuriyīralāl an naccup poruḷ irattattiṉiṉṟum pirikkappaṭuṅ kāṟṟu vaḻiyāka mūkkiṉ ṟuḷivaḻiyē veḷippaṭṭup pōkiṉṟatu. ivvāṟu namatu uṭampiliruntu mūkkut tuḷiyiṉ vaḻiyāka veḷippaṭuṅ kāṟṟu naccup poruḷ uṭiyatāyiruppati nīṅkaḷ terintu koḷḷa vēṇṭumāṉāl ataṟku eḷitāka vaḻiyoṉṟu kāṭṭu kiṉṟām. amiḻta iyalpuṭiya uyirkkāṟṟuc cuṇṇāmput taṇṇīrōṭu kalakka māṭṭātu; nañciṉiyal puḷḷa karikkāṟṟō cuṇṇāmput taṇṇīroṭu kalantu taṭippāṉa cuṇṇāmpi uṇṭākkavallatākum. orupaṭi taṇṇīril ciṟitu cuṇṇāmpik karittut teḷiya vittāl cuṇṇāmpellām aṭiyilē taṅkiviṭum; mēlē tēḷivāka niṟkun taṇṇīri maṭṭum ōr ēṉattil iṟuttu vittuk koṇṭu, mūkkiṉ vaḻiyē veḷivaruṅ kāṟṟi oru ciṟu mūṅkiṟ kuḻāyiṉ vaḻiyāka avvēṉatti lirukkuñ cuṇṇāmput taṇṇīr mēṟpaṭumpaṭi puṟaṅkaḻitta uṭaṉē at taṇṇīrmēṟ pālēṭu paṭarntāṟ pōlac cuṇṇāmpu kaṭṭum; ippaṭiyē oruvar veḷiviṭuṅ kāṟṟellām at taṇṇiriṉmēl vīcik koṇṭirukku māyiṟ ciṟitu nēratti lellām atiliruntu ciṟitu kaṭṭic cuṇṇāmpē eṭukkalām. ākavē, naccuk kāṟṟu uṇṭeṉpati aṟintu koḷḷuvataṟkuc cuṇṇāmput taṇṇir mikavum utavi ceyyum eṉka. itaṉāl, makkaḷum viḷaṅkukaḷ paṟavikaḷ mutalāṉa maṟṟa uyirkaḷum veḷi viṭuṅ kāṟṟil naccuk kāṟṟu uṇṭeṉpatu naṉku viḷaṅkum. oruvar tūya kāṟṟu nirampiya iṭattiliruntāl avar aṅkē  ak kāṟṟiluḷḷa uyirp poruḷi ataṉ vaḻiyāka uḷḷē iḻuttu, nuri īraliṉutaviyāl irattattiṟ kalappittu, ataṉi uṭaleṅkum paravuvittu, avvuṭampil noṭika ṭōṟuṅ kakkappaṭṭu varum aḻukkēṟiya naccuk kāṟṟi irattattiṉ utaviyāl neñcap pikku varuvittu, aṅkē ataṉoṭu poruntiyuḷḷa nuri īraliṉ utaviyāl irattattiṉiṉṟum an naccuk kāṟṟip pirippittut tirumpavum mūkkut tuḷiyiṉ vāyilāka ataṉi veḷip paṭuttukiṉṟār. iṅṅaṉam avar mūcciṉ ōṭṭatti naṭappikkiṉṟataṉāl avar varuvataṟkumuṉ tūyatāṉa uyirkkāṟṟu nirampiyirunta iṭam avar vantirunta cila nāḻikikkup piṟaku aḻukkēṟiya naccuk kāṟṟiṉāl niṟikkappaṭum.
ākavē, oruvar irukkum iṭam evvaḷavu tūya kāṟṟu uṭiyatā yiruntālum, avar aṅkirunta cila nāḻikip poḻutiṟkuḷḷellām atu naccuk kāṟṟāl nirappap paṭukiṉṟa miyāl,aṅṅaṉam aṭikkaṭi veḷivantu aḻukkup paṭuttuṅ kāṟṟu avviṭattilēyē nillāmal miku virivil appāṟpōkavum, vēṟu tūyakāṟṟu tāmirukkum iṭattil vantu niṟiyavuñ ceytu koḷḷal vēṇṭum. nām irukkum vīṭukaḷil oruvarukku mēl makkaḷ palar iruttalāl avvīṭukaḷil uḷḷa tūya kāṟṟu evvaḷavu virivil nañcāyp pōkum eṉpati nām muṉ varinta pakutikaḷil naṉku viḷakkiṉāmātalāl, atip paṟṟi iṉṉum mikutiyāka yām vaṟpuṟutteḻutal vēṇṭā. makkaḷ palar irukkum vīṭukaḷil appōtikkappōtu cērum naccuk kāṟṟi veḷippaṭuppit taṟkun tūyakāṟṟi uḷ nuḻivittaṟkuñ cāḷaraṅkaḷ pala amittal vēṇṭum. veḷḷikkārar irukkum vīṭukaḷip pāruṅkaḷ! av vīṭukaḷil entap pakkattiṟ pārttāluñ cāḷaraṅkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. veḷiyē iṭiveḷiyil iyaṅkun tūya kāṟṟāṉatu ac cāḷaraṅkaḷiṉ vaḻiyē uḷḷē taṭiyiṉṟi vantulavi avviṭaṅkaḷi mikavun tuppuravu ceykiṉṟatu. av vīṭukaḷil irukkum avarkaḷ evvaḷavu cemmiyāka irukkiṉṟārkaḷ! nīṅkaḷ pārttatilliyā! aṉpuḷḷa em tamiḻ nāṭṭavarkaḷē! niṅkaḷ kaṭṭukiṉṟa vīṭakaḷilō veṉṟāṟ perumpāluñ cāḷaraṅkaḷē nīṅkaḷ vippa tilli; aṉṟi vittālum mikac ciṟiyatāy oṉṟu allatu iraṇṭukku mēl vippatilli. ataṉāl nīṅkaḷ aṭivatu yātu? mūcciṉāl veḷiyē viṭṭa naccuk kāṟṟiṉiyē tirumpavum mūcciṉāl uḷḷiḻuk kiṉṟīrkaḷ; uḷḷē ceṉṟa naccuk kāṟṟi irattattiṟ kalap pikkumpōtu nuriyīraliṉ perumpākañcuruṅkip pōta lāl an nuriyīral valiviḻantu pōkiṉṟatu; naccuk kāṟṟō irattatiṟ kalantu uṭampeṅkum paravi ellā vaṟṟiyum nañcākkuvatoṭu taliyiluḷḷa mūḷiyiṉ valiviyuṅ kuṟittu aṟiviyum maḻuṅkac ceykiṉṟatu; itaṉāl uṭampeṅkum pala vakiyāṉa nōykaḷ uṇṭākiṉṟaṉa. nuriyīral curukkam aṭiya aṭiya mūcciṉ oṭṭaṅ kuṟikiṉṟatu. ataṉāl, īḷi, elumpu rukki nōy, nīriḻivu mutalāṉa pollāta nōykaḷ kiḷittu uṅkaḷiṟ perum pālāri iḷañ cākkāṭṭiṟku uḷḷākkukiṉṟaṉa. nīṅkaḷ maṭṭumā? uṅkaḷ vayiṟṟiṟ piṟakkum oṉṟum aṟiyā iḷaṅkuḻavikaḷum allavō uṅkaḷ nōykaḷit tāmum peṟṟup peritun tuṉpuṟṟu iṟakkiṉṟaṉa! yārāvatu nallaṟivuṭiyōr uṅkaḷiṭam vantu, `vīṭukaḷi akalamākavun tirutta mākavuṅ kaṭṭuvittuc cāḷaraṅkaḷ palappala amittu nalla kāṟṟut taṭiyiṉṟi ulavumpaṭi ceyyuṅkōḷ, eṉṟu coṉṉāl avarkaḷ naṟcollip poruḷ paṇṇāmaṟ pārāmukañceytu `eṅkaḷukku avvāṟu vīṭukaṭṭukiṟa vaḻakkamilli. atu vaḻakkattukku māṟākum eṉṟu muḻumaṭattaṉamākac colli avariyum ēḷaṉam paṇṇu kiṟīrkaḷ. itaṉāl vīṭukaḷi mikavum aḻakākak kaṭṭa vēṇṭumeṉpatu ematu karuttaṉṟu. kuṟinta celavilēyē tuppuravākavum akalamākavum, nalla kāṟṟut taṭiyiṉṟi vantu ulavum vakiyākavuñ cāḷaraṅkaḷ pala amittu eḷitiṟ kaṭṭalām. cāḷaraṅkaḷ illāta aṟiyil oruvari vippatu avari eḷitākak koli ceyvataṟkē īṭākum.avari av vaṟikkuḷḷēyē vittuk katavi iḻuttap pūṭṭi viṭṭāl, mutaliṟ cila nēraṅkaḷ variyil avar aṅkuḷḷa kāṟṟiyellām uḷḷiḻuttup piṟaku avvaṟi muḻutum naccuk kāṟṟi niṟippar; an naccuk kāṟṟup pōvataṟku vaḻi yillāmiyāl avar tirumpavum ataṉiyē uṭ koḷḷa nuriyīral curuṅki; aciyāmal niṉṟu pōkum; uṭaṉē avar tikkumukkāṭi iṟantu pōvatutiṇṇam. `itaṟku ṭilliyil aracu purinta koṭuṅkōṉ maṉṉaṉāṉa cirājū ṭulā eṉpavaṉ ceyvitta koṭuñ ceykiyē pōtumāṉa cāṉṟākum; avaṉ veḷḷikkārarōṭu pakikoṇṭu avarkaḷil nūṟṟu nāṟpattāṟu peyarip piṭittup patiṉeṭṭu aṭi nāṟpakka aḷavuḷḷa ōr iruṭṭaṟiyil oru nāḷiravu ciṟiyiṭuvittāṉ; maṟunāṭ kāliyil avvaṟik katavit tiṟantu pārkkiyil irupattu mūṉṟupēr maṭṭuṅ kottuyiruṅ koli yumirumāyk kiṭantārkaḷ; maṟṟa nūṟṟu irupattu muṉṟu peyarum veṟum piṇamāyk kiṭantārkaḷ1. itaṉi āḻa niṉittup pārk kiyil, makkaḷ uyir vāḻvataṟku vēṇṭiya uṟiviṭam evvaḷavu akalamākavun, tūya uyirkkāṟṟu ulavuvatākavum irukka vēṇṭumeṉpatu pulappaṭavilliyā?
mēlum mikutiyāṉa kāṟṟu ulavap peṟāta oṭukkiṭaṅ kaḷiluñ ciṟiya aṟikaḷilum nammavarkaḷ palar nerukkamākap paṭuttut tūṅkutalum uṟitaluñ ceykiṉṟārkaḷ. iṅṅaṉañ ceyvataṉāl av viṭaṅkaḷiṟ kuṟivāka uḷḷa nalla kāṟṟum avarkaḷatu mūcciṉāl mikuviriviṟ keṭṭu nañcāyp pōtalāl, avarkaḷ anta naccuk kāṟṟip piṟakellām uḷḷiḻuttut tamatu uṭampi nōykku iriyākku kiṉṟārkaḷ.
makkaḷiṉ mūccu ōṭṭattāl uṇṭākum inta naccuk kāṟṟāṉatu pakaṟ kālattik kāṭṭilum irāk kālattil mikutiyākap paravum. etaṉā leṉṟāṟ kūṟukiṉṟām: pakalavaṉ veḷiccam uḷḷa pakaṟkālattiṟ puṟpūṇṭu marañ ceṭi koṭi mutalāṉa niliyiyaṟ poruḷkaḷ naccuk kāṟṟi uḷḷē iḻuttut tūya uyirkkāṟṟi veḷiyē viṭukiṉṟaṉa; veyil veḷiccam illāta irāk kālattilō puṟpūṇṭu mutaliyaṉa uyirk kāṟṟi uḷḷi ḻuttu naccuk kāṟṟi veḷiviṭukiṉṟaṉa.2  ākavē, puṟ puṇṭukaḷiṉ mūccu ōṭṭamāṉatu pakaṟkālattil oru vakiyākavum irākkālattil maṟṟoru vakiyākavum muṟṟum māṟupaṭṭu naṭattal niṉivu kūraṟpālatākum. maṟṟu, makkaḷiṉ mūccu ōṭṭamō ekkālattum orē taṉmiyāka  naṭipeṟukiṉṟatu. pakal irā eṉṉum iru kālattum uyirkkāṟṟi uḷḷiḻuttu naccukkāṟṟi makkaḷ veḷiviṭukiṉṟārkaḷ. pakaṟkālattil ivarkaḷ veḷiviṭum naccukkāṟṟi marañ ceṭi koṭi mutaliya niliyiyaṟ poruḷkaḷ tām uḷḷiḻuttut tūya uyirk kāṟṟi veḷiyē kakkukiṉṟaṉa; iṅṅaṉaṅ kakkappaṭṭa tūya uyirk kāṟṟi makkaḷ uḷḷiḻuppataṉāl mikavuñ cemmiyāṉa uṭampu uṭiyavarā kiṉṟārkaḷ cuṟṟilum maraṅkaḷ aṭarnta iṭaṅkaḷil vīṭukaṭṭi iruppavarkaḷ mikka nalamuḷḷavar kaḷāyiruppatu inta ētuviṉālētāṉ; pakaṟkāla muḻutum ivarkaḷ veḷiviṭum naccukkāṟṟi ivarkaḷ vīṭṭic cuṟṟiyuḷḷa maraṅkaḷ iḻuttu viḻuṅkit tūya uyirkkāṟṟi veḷiviṭa, aṅṅaṉam veḷiviṭappaṭṭa tūya kāṟṟi aṅkuḷḷa am makkaḷ uṭkoṇṭu tūya irattam vāykkap peṟṟavarkaḷāyc cemmiyā yirukkiṉ ṟārkaḷ. āṉāl, irākkālattilō makkaḷum naccuk kāṟṟi veḷiviṭa maraṅkaḷum aṅṅaṉamē naccukkāṟṟi veḷiviṭa, eṅkum naccukkāṟṟu nirampi yiruttalāl, akkālattilētāṉ makkaḷ mikavuṅ karuttāka iruttal vēṇṭum. itaṉālē tāṉ, nōyāḷikaḷukku irāk kālattil nōy mikutip paṭukiṉṟa teṉṟu collukiṟārkaḷ. irāk kālattil maraṅkaḷiṉkīḻē paṭukkalākātu eṉṟu ācārakkōviyiṟ collappaṭṭatum 3ivvētu paṟṟiyē tāṉ. itaṉ uṇmi teriyātavarkaḷiṟ cilar iravil maraṅkaḷiṉkīḻē kiṭantu uṟaṅki, irattaṅ kakki uyir tuṟantu piṇamāyk kiṭantati maṟunāḷiṟ kaṇṭavarkaḷ avarkaḷ pēyaṭittu iṟantārkaḷ eṉṟu colluvatuṇṭu. aṉāl, uṇmi aṅṅaṉamaṉṟu. irākkālattil maraṅkaḷ tūya uyirkkāṟṟi uṭkoṇṭu naccukkāṟṟi veḷittaḷḷu kiṉṟaṉa vāki yāl, avaṟṟiṉkīḻ aṟiyāmaṟ paṭuppavarkaḷ an naccuk kāṟṟi mikutiyāka uḷḷiḻuttu irattaṅ kakki iṟantu pōkiṉṟaṉar. makkaḷ uṭampiṉuḷ uyirkkāṟṟu nuḻiyā viṭṭāl, naccukkāṟṟē iṭiviṭāmal nuḻintāl, neñcat tāmariyiliruntu pala viṭaṅkaḷukkum ōṭum irattamāṉatu taṉ iyalputirintu kariyatāki mūkkiṉ vaḻiyāka veḷippaṭṭu viṭum. inta nuṭpan teriyap peṟātavarkaḷ `pēyaṭittu iṟantāṉ eṉṟu colluvar. avvāṟāyiṉ vīṭṭic cuṟṟi maraṅkaḷ vaḷarttu vippatu pakaṟkālattil maṭṭum payaṉpaṭumē yaṉṟi, irākkālattiṟ payaṉpaṭāmal iṭar viḷippatāy māṟumē eṉiṉ; iravil maraṅkaḷiṉkīḻ uṅṟakuvatutāṉ iṭar payakkumē yallāmal, avaṟṟi vilaki vīṭṭuḷ uṟaṅkuvatu iṭar payavātu. avvāṟāṉālum, iravil maraṅkaḷ kakkukiṉṟa naccukkāṟṟāṉatu vīṭṭuḷḷum nuḻiyum ākiyāl, atu nallatāvatu yāṅṅaṉameṉṟāl; tūya kāṟṟu ulāvumpaṭi cāḷaraṅkaḷ pala amikkappaṭṭiru rukkumāṉāl iṭiveḷiyil ulavum uyirk kāṟṟāṉatu vīṭṭiṉuṭ pukuntu marattiṉāluṇṭākum naccukkāṟṟiyum makkaḷāl uṇṭākum naccuk kāṟṟiyum akaṟṟi, vīṭu muḻumiyum appōtikkap pōtu tūyatākumpaṭi ceyyum. ātalāl, aḵtu oru ciṟitum iṭarpayavāteṉṟu aṟika. eṉavē pakaṟkāla mellām makkaḷukkut tūya uyirkkāṟṟit tarukiṉṟa maraṅkaḷ vīṭṭic cuṟṟiyiruppatu iṉṟiyami yātatāmeṉka. irākkālattil ammaraṅkaḷiviṭṭuc ciṟitu akaṉṟu vīṭṭiḷuḷḷirup pōrkku, avaṟṟāṟ ciṟitun tuṉpa muṇṭāka māṭṭātu. maraṅkaḷ irukku miṭaṅkaḷiṟ tūya kāṟṟu mikutiyāka  vīcuvatu ulaka iyaṟkiyātalāl, aṅṅaṉam mikutiyāka vīcun tūya uyirk kāṟṟāṉatu maraṅkaḷ kakkukiṉṟa naccukkāṟṟiyum makkaḷ vīṭṭuḷ veḷiviṭukiṉṟa naccu kakāṟṟiyum pōkkit tuppuravu ceyvatu tiṇṇam. ākavē, maraṅkaḷ ek kālattum vīṭṭic cuṟṟiyiruppatu uṭampiṉ nalam pēṇutaṟku iṉṟiyamiyāta toṉṟāmeṉṟu uṇarka.
iṉṉum irākkālattil vīṭṭiṉuḷḷē naccuk kāṟṟi mikutip paṭuttukiṉṟa poruḷkaḷiyellām nīkkal vēṇṭum., irākkālattil mutaṉmiyāy uṟaṅkutaṟkup pōkuntaṟu vāyil; vīṭṭil erintu koṇṭirukkum viḷakkukaḷi ellām niṟutti viṭuvatu nallatākum. ēṉeṉṟāl, viḷakkukaḷ erivataṟkut tūya uyirkkāṟṟu mutaṉmiyāka vēṇṭap paṭukiṉṟatu; uyirk kāṟṟi uriñci erikiṉṟa viḷakkukaḷāṉavi aṅṅaṉam erintu koṇṭirukkum pōtellām aḻukkēṟiya naccukkāṟṟi veḷippaṭuttu kiṉṟaṉa. ākavē, makkaḷ uṟaṅkumpōtu viḷakkukaḷ eriyumāṉāl avarkaḷatu uyir vāḻkkikku iṉṟiyamiyāta tūya kāṟṟi avikaḷ iḻuttuk koṇṭu avarkaḷukkut tīmiyi viḷikkum naccukkāṟṟi mēṉmēl veḷi viṭumātalāl ataṉi uḷḷiḻuttu avarkaḷ tamatuṭampiṉ nalatti iḻantu pōvārkaḷ. itaṉāṟ pōlun `tali māṭṭil viḷakku eriyaviṭalākātu eṉṟu vīṭukaḷ tōṟum vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟārkaḷ. mūcciṉ ōṭṭam mukattil amikkappaṭṭa mūkkiṉ tuḷivaḻiyāka naṭipeṟutalāl ataṉ arukil viḷakku erivatu uyirkkāṟṟu māṟi nañcāyp pōvataṟku iṭamākum eṉpatutāṉ ataṉāṟ pōnta karuttākum.
iṉṉum vīṭṭiṉuḷḷē yuḷḷa puḻakkaṭi muṟṟattiṟ cēṟuṅ kumpiyum illāmal avaṟṟi muḻutuṅ kaḻuvi avviṭattit tuppuravāka vittal vēṇṭum. kuppi kaḷum aḻukkut tuṇikaḷum illāmal avaṟṟiyum akaṟṟal vēṇṭum. vīṭṭuc cuvarkaḷiṉ mēl enta vakiyāṉa aḻukkum paṭiyāmal avaṟṟiyun tuppuravāka vittiruttal vēṇṭum. itaṟkāka aṭikkaṭi ac cuvar kaḷiṉmēṟ cuṇṇāmpu tīṟṟal vēṇṭum. cākkaṭi kaḷiluñ cuṇṇāmpik karittut teḷittuvaral vēṇṭum. naccukkāṟṟi uriñciviṭak kūṭiya āṟṟal cuṇṇāmpiṉiṭattē amintirukkiṉṟatu. iraviṟ paṭukkap pōkum poḻutu viḷakkukaḷi ellām aṇittu viṭṭu, avaṟṟāl uṇṭāṉa naccukkāṟṟup pōkumpaṭi cāḷaraṅkaḷit tiṟantu nalla kāṟṟu uḷḷē ulāvumpaṭi ceyvittu, ataṉpiṟaku tuyil koḷḷac cellal vēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉan tuyil koḷḷumiṭattum oruvarō ṭoruvar cērntupaṭuttal ākātu. ēṉeṉil, oruvarviṭum naccuk kāṟṟi maṟṟoruvar uṭkoḷḷumpaṭi nērumākiyāl, aḵtu uṭampiṉ nalattiṟku mikavum keṭutiyi viḷippatākum. ākavē, oru vīṭṭiṟ palar iruntāl ovvoruvarun taṉit taṉiyē vilakippaṭuppatu tāṉ uṭampiṉ nalam pātukāppataṟku uriya naṉmuṟiyākum eṉka.
iṉit, tuyil koḷḷuṅkāl maṟṟoru mutaṉmiyāṉa poruḷuṅ karuttiṟ patikkaṟpālatākum. naccuk kāṟṟut taṭippuḷḷatāyum, uyirkkāṟṟu noyya tāyum irukkiṉṟaṉa. taṭippāṉa poruḷkaḷi yellām nilamāṉatu taṉṉiṭattē iḻukkun taṉmi yuṭimiyiṉāl, naccuk kāṟṟāṉatu eppōtum nilamaṭṭattil ulavik koṇṭirukkum; uyirk kāṟṟō noyya poruḷā kiyāl nilamaṭṭattiṟku mēl ulavik koṇṭirukkum. inta ētuviṉāl, nilattiṉ mēṟ paṭuppatik kāṭṭiluṅ kaṭṭiliṉmēṟ paṭuppatē mikavum nallatākum. nilattiṉmēṟ paṭuttirukkuṅkāl tām paṭut tirukkumiṭattil tūyakāṟṟu mikutiyāka vīcāviṭṭāl ataṉmēṟ paṭintirukkum naccukkāṟṟi uḷḷiḻuttu aṅ kuḷḷōr tamatuṭampiṉ nalatti iḻappar. kaṭṭiliṉmēṟ paṭuttirup pavarkaḷō veṉṟāl veḷikkāṟṟu vīcā viṭṭālum, naccukkāṟṟuk kaṭṭiliṉkīḻē yiruttalāl, ataṉi uṭkoḷḷāmaṟ kaṭṭiliṉ mēlē ulāvum uyirkkāṟṟi maṭṭum uṭkoṇṭu nalamāyiruppar. āṉataṉāl, nilattiṉmēṟ paṭuppati viṭak kaṭṭiliṉmēṟ paṭuppatu paṉmaṭaṅku nalaṉṟaruvatā meṉka. āyiṉun, tūyatāṉa veḷikkāṟṟu ennēramum vantu ulavik koṇṭirukkum iṭaṅkaḷiṟ nilattiṉmēṟ paṭuppatu kuṟṟamākātu; ēṉeṉṟāl, aṅkuḷḷa naccuk kāṟṟellām aṭikkaṭi vantulavum nalla kāṟṟiṉāl appuṟam pōkkap paṭumātalāmeṉka.
aṭikkuṟippukaḷ
1.	See History of India, by Mr. M. Prothero, M.A. & Dr. Satis Chandra Vidyabhusana, pp. 431, 432.
2.	Read Dr. H.E. Roscoe’s chemistry primer p. 23-30
2.	irāmaramuñ cērār 13. ācārakkōvi.
3. pirāṇāyāma appiyācam: 
mūccup paḻakkam

makkaḷuṭiya uyir vāḻkkikku iṉṟiyamiyāc ciṟappiṉatākiya uyirkkāṟṟiṉ naṉmiyiyum, ataṟku māṟāṉa naccuk kāṟṟiṉ tīmiyi yum mēli iyalkaḷil oruvāṟu virittu viḷakkik kāṭṭiṉēm. iṉi, inta iyalil avvuyirk kāṟṟi muccuviṭat terintu paḻakum vakiyi eṭuttuk kūṟappōkiṉṟēm. nūṟu āṇṭum ataṟkum mēlum uyirvāḻa virumpukiṉṟavarḷukku inta nūliṟ collappaṭum ovvoru poruḷum viliyiṭutaṟkariya māṇikkaṅkaḷākavē tōṉṟum, inta nūlil emmāl eṭuttuc collappaṭum poruḷkaḷ eḷitiṟ kiṭippaṉa alla; ākayāl neṭuṅ kālam uyirōṭiruntu payaṉpeṟa vēṇṭum naṇparkaḷ ivaṟṟi maṉattiṉkaṇ ūṉṟip peṟutaṟkariya perum payaṉip peṟuvārkaḷāka! piṉṉē collappaṭum poruḷ mikavum uṉṉikkaṟ pālatoru muḻu māṇikkamākum. naṉṟāka uṟṟuṇarka.
makkaḷ ovvoruvaruñ ciṟitu nēram uṉṉip pārkaḷāṉāl, tamatu mūkkut tuḷiyil varum uyirk kāṟṟu māṟi māṟi ōṭuvati viḷakkamākat terintu koḷḷal kūṭum. uyirkkāṟṟuc ciṟitu nēram valatu mūkkil ōṭi varum; appuṟañ ciṟitu nēram iṭatu mūkkil ōṭivarum; appuṟam oru ciṟitu nēram iraṇṭu mūkkilum oppa ōṭivarum. iṅṅaṉam itu māṟi māṟi ōṭi varutalum oppa oṭi varutalum ēṉ eṉṟu ituvariyil nīṅkaḷ ārāyntu pārāviṭṭālum iv vuṇmiyi yām terivitta piṟakēṉum iḵtu ētō oru perum payaṉ tarutaṟkākavē ivvāṟu oṭi varukiṉṟateṉa uṅkaṭkut tōṉṟal vēṇṭu maṉṟō? ēṉ iḵtu ivvāṟu māṟi māṟi oṭi varal vēṇṭum? orē vakiyāka ōṭalākātā? eṉiṉ; uyirvāḻkki yellām ovvoru noṭiyum māṟi māṟi naṭakkum uyirāṟṟaliṉ ōṭṭattiṉālētāṉ nilipeṟu kiṉṟatu. inta uyirāṟṟalāṉatu oru ciṟitunēram oyntu viṭumāṉāl makkaḷ ētu? maram ētu? ulakam ētu? uyir ētu? itaṉi aṟivataṟku neṭitu cellal vēṇṭā. pakaliṟ katiravaṉum iraviṟṟiṅkaḷum māṟi māṟi iyaṅkik koṇ ṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. ivvāṟu māṟi māṟi varāmaṟ pakalavaṉē eppōtum viḷaṅkik koṇṭiruntāl evvāṟu irukkum! allatu tiṅkaḷē tulaṅkik koṇṭiruntāl evvāṟu irukkum! aṉṟi iv viraṇṭumē yillāmal veṟiya iruḷē paraviyiruntāl eṅṅaṉam irukkum! itaṉic ciṟitu niṉittup pāruṅkaḷ! avvaḷavu neṭukattāṉ pōvāṉēṉ? nīṅkaḷ eppōtumē tūṅkāmal vēliceytu koṇṭirun tāl evvāṟu irukkum! allatu eppōtumē tūṅkik koṇṭiruntāl eṅṅaṉamirukkum! avi ciṟitum virumpat takkaṉavā yirukkumā? irāvē, cilakāṟ pakaluñ cilakāl iravuñ, cilakāṟ katiravaṉuñ cilakāṟ ṟiṅkaḷuñ, cilakāl viḻippuñ cilakāl uṟakkamum māṟi māṟi varumāyiṉaṉṟō uyir vāḻkkiyum ulakamum nili peṟum? iv vuṇmikaḷāl māṟutalē uyirvāḻkki yām eṉṟum, aḵtiṉmiyē māytalām eṉṟum nīṅkaḷ eḷitil aṟintu koḷḷalām. ākavē, namatu mūkkut tuḷikaḷilē uyirkkāṟṟu māṟi māṟi varuvatu namatu uyir vāḻkkiyiṉ poruṭṭākavēyā meṉṟu teḷivāka uṇaral vēṇṭum. namatu uṭampiṉ valatu pākamāṉatu evvakit toḻiluñ ceytaṟku eḷiyatāy valivu koṇṭiruttaliṉ atu ñāyiṟṟu maṇṭilam eṉṟum, iṭatu pākamāṉatu eḷiyavāṉa toḻilkaḷi maṭṭuñ ceytaṟku uriyatāy valivu ciṟitu kuṟintiruttaliṉ atu tiṅkaḷ maṭṇaṭilam eṉṟuñ collappaṭum. itukoṇṭu valatu mūkkil ōṭuṅ kāṟṟāṉatu pakaṟkali eṉṟum, iṭatu mūkkil ōṭuṅ kāṟṟāṉatu matikkali eṉṟuñ collappaṭum. valatu mūkkilē kāṟṟu ōṭumpōtu uṭampil veppamum, iṭatu mūkkilē ōṭumpōtu kuḷirum okkap paravik koṇṭi rukkum. ennēramum veppamē paravik koṇṭiruntālum uṭampu aḻintu pōm; en nēramuṅ kuḷirē paravik koṇṭiruntālum uṭampu aḻintupōm. orukāl veppamum orukāṟ kuḷircciyum māṟi māṟip paravik koṇṭirukku māṉāṟṟāṉ uṭampukku aḻivu varātu. iṅṅaṉam valatu puṟattilum iṭatu puṟattilum māṟi māṟip paravik koṇṭirukkuñ cūṭuṅ kuḷircciyum ulakattiṟ kāṇappaṭuñ cūṭuṅ kuḷircciyum pōlvaṉa  alla. avi mikavum nuṇṇiyavāya uṭampiṉ uyir niliyāṉa iṭaṅkaḷip paṟṟik koṇṭu naṭakkum veppamuṅ kuḷirumākum. inta nuṇṇiya veppamē miṉṉoḷi eṉṟum vintu eṉṟum tava nūlkaḷiṟ collappaṭum; nuṇṇiya kuḷircciyē nilavoḷi eṉṟum nātam eṉṟuñ collappaṭum.
iṉi, ūṉuṭampākiya namatu yākki ciṟṟulaku eṉṟum, ivvūṉuṭampu iruttaṟku iṭamākiya in nilam pērulaku eṉṟum nūlkaḷiṟ collappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. ciṟiya ulakamākiya namatu yākkiyi ṉuḷḷē nuṇṇiya veppaṅ kuḷirkaḷāṉa iv vintu nātaṅkaḷ iṭi viṭātu ulāvik koṇṭiruppatu pōlavē, periya ulakamākiya innilavulakat tilum pariya cūṭuṅ kuḷirumāṉa ñāyiṟṟuk kali tiṅkaṭ kali ōyāmal, ulavik koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. ākavē, namatu uṭampiṉ nalam paḻutu paṭāmal irukka vēṇṭumāyiṉ ulaka vuṭampiṉ nikaḻcciyi naṉku aṟintu, ataṟku iciya namatu yākki yulakiṉ nikaḻcciyi oḻuṅku ceytu koḷḷal vēṇṭum. veḷiyē vemmi mikuti yāyirukkumpōtu namatu uṭampiṉ uḷḷēyum vemmi mikutippaṭumāyiṉ uḷḷē aminta karuvikaḷ ellām erintupaṭa uṭampu aḻintuviṭum ākiyāl, veḷiyē cūṭu mikumpōtellām uṭam piṉuḷḷē ataṉi etirttu niṟpataṟkup pōtu māṉa nuṇṇiya kuḷircci uṇṭākal vēṇṭum. aḵteṅṅaṉam uṇṭāku meṉṟāl, namatu mūcciṟ ṟiṅkaṭkali mikutiyāku māṟu ceytu koṇṭāl nuṇṇiya taṭpam mikuntu vēṉiṟkāla veppattiṉāl varuntuṉpatti oḻikkum.
iṉi, veḷiyē maḻikāla paṉik kālaṅkaḷiṟ kuḷir mikutiyā yirukkumpōtu uṭampiṉ uḷḷēyuṅ kuḷircci mikutippaṭumāyiṉ uḷḷaminta karuvikaḷellāmiyakkaṅ kuṟintu iṟuki niṉṟuviṭum; ataṉāl uṭampu aḻintu pōm. ākiyāl, ak kālaṅkaḷil uṭampiṟ kuḷircci mikāta vaṇṇam namatu mūcciṟ pakalavaṉ kali mikuti yākumāṟu ceytu koḷvōmāyiṉ nuṇṇiya veppam mikuntu maḻikāla paṉikkālak kuḷiriṉāl varum iṭari oḻikkum. iṅṅaṉaṅ kāla iyaṟki yiṉi aṟintu ataṟku ēṟpat tamatuṭampil taṭpa veppaṅkaḷi mikutip paṭuttik koḷḷavalla tavattōrkaḷ evviṭattil ekkālattil iruntārāṉālum avaṟṟāṟ ciṟitun tuṉpuṟamāṭṭārkaḷ.
mēlum, ñāyiṟu, tiṅkaṇ maṇṭilaṅkaḷit tavirac cevvāy, putaṉ, viyāḻaṉ, veḷḷi, caṉi maṇṭilaṅkaḷum namatu maṇṇulakattiṟku arukāmiyiliruntu koṇṭu, aviyun tammiṭattiluḷḷa taṭpa veppaṅkaḷi iṅkē varumāṟu ceyvittalāl avaṟṟālum nām tākkuṇṭu namatu uṭampiṉ nalattiyum maṉanalaṅkaḷiyum iḻantu pōkiṉ ṟōm. ākavē, avaṟṟāl uṇṭākuṅ keṭuti kaḷiyum vilakkuvataṟku avaṟṟiṉ iyalpu aṟintu, namatu uṭampil avaṟṟit taṭuttu niṟpataṟkup pōtumāṉa aḷavu taṭpaveppaṅkaḷi mikutippaṭuttik koḷḷal vēṇṭum.
iṉi, ik kōḷkaḷiṉ āṟṟalkaḷ mikutippaṭṭu nammi vantu tākkutaṟkuri yakālam irākkālattil irupatu nāḻiki mutal muppatu nāḻiki variyilum ākum. ēṉeṉṟāl, ikkālam pakaṟpoḻutiṟkum irāp poḻutiṟkum naṭuniṟpatākum, pakaṟpoḻutiṟ katiravaṉatu veppamum irāp poḻutiṟ ṟiṅkaḷiṉatu taṭpamum ulavik koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. irāp poḻutu muḻuvatum perum pālum matiyiṉatu taṭpam paravik koṇṭi runtālum, itu veppattiviṭa vali kuṟintatāyiruttalāl, iraviṉ piṟpoḻutil taṉatu valivu piṉṉuṅ kaṟintu maṟṟik kōḷkaḷiṉ āṟṟalkaḷ vantu pāyvataṟku iṭaṅkoṭuttu niṟkiṉṟatu. ākavē, veppamun taṭpamum uramāka ulavutal illāta iraviṉ piṟkālattilē tāṉ uṭampiṉ niliyi mikavum uṉṉippāy pārttal vēṇṭum. ak kālattilē tāṉ nōyi uṇṭu paṇṇutaṟku uriya naccup poruḷkaḷ uṭaluḷ vantu nuḻikiṉṟaṉa. appōtutāṉ uṭampil acatiyum uṟakkamum mikutippaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. ivviyalpi aṟintu yārum appōtu kaṇ viḻittiruttalum, vēṟu muyaṟcikaḷ ceytaluñ ciṟituṅ kūṭā. iraviṉ piṟpākattiṟ kaṇviḻitti ruppārkkum, vēṟu muyaṟcikaḷ ceyvārkkum nōyvili koṭuttu vāṅkiṉāṟ pōla vantu cērum. akkālattila āṇum peṇṇum puṇarumāṉāl av viraṇṭaṉ uṭampum nili kulintu virivil aḻintu pōvatu tiṇṇam. ākiyāl akkālattil ellārum ellā uyirkaḷum ayarntu uṟaṅkuvatē mikavum nalla tākum; viṭiyaṟ kālattiṟku muṉṉamē kaṇ viḻikka vēṇṭumeṉṟu eṇṇupavarkaḷ, av veṇṇatti viṭṭu irupattoṉpatu nāḻikikkup piṉtuyil oḻintu eḻuvatē ciṟanta nalattit taruvatākumeṉa uṇartal vēṇṭum.
iṉi, nalla uṭampōṭiruppavaṉukku iraviṉ piṟpākamākiya intak kālattilē avvak kiḻamikaḷukku icinta vakiyāka mūccu naṭakkum. cemmiyāyiruppa vaṉukkut tiṅkaḷ, putaṉ, veḷḷi eṉṉum mūṉṟu nāṭ kaḷilum mūccāṉatu iṭatu mūkkilē naṭakkum; cevvāy, caṉi, ñāyiṟu eṉṉum mūṉṟu nāṭkaḷilum mūccāṉatu valatu mūkkilē naṭakkum; vaḷarpiṟi viyāḻakkiḻamiyil iṭatu mūkkilum tēypiṟi viyāḻak kiḻamiyil valatu mūkkilu māka mūccu naṭakkum. ivvāṟu kuṟippiṭṭa kiḻamikaḷiṉ iraviṉ piṟpākattil mēṟ colliyavaṇṇam mūccu naṭakkiṉṟatā eṉpati ovvoruvarum uṉṉittup pārttal vēṇṭum. uṭampu nalla cemmiyāṉa niliyilē irukkumaḷavum mūcciṉ ōṭṭamāṉatu mēlē kuṟippiṭṭa vaṇṇan tavaṟāmal naṭakkum. aṅṅaṉam naṭavāmal māṟupaṭṭu naṭakkumāyiṉ, ētō nōy varappōkiṟa teṉpati muṉṉatākat terintu koṇṭu, avvāṟu nōyvarāmaṟ ṟaṭukkum poruṭṭu antantak kiḻami kaḷukkuc colliya mūkkut tuḷikaḷilē mūccu naṭi peṟumāṟu ceytu koḷḷal vēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉam mūccu ōṭṭatti nām niṉintavāṟu tirupputaṟku vaḻiyāteṉiṉ; ataṉic civa cittānta ñāṉapōtattiṟ pirāṇāyāmam eṉṟa ariya kaṭṭuriyiṉ kaṇ viḷakkik kāṭṭi yirukkiṉṟēm. aṅkē aṉik kaṇṭu koḷka. iṅṅaṉam mūccu ōṭṭattit tiruppik koḷvatōṭu, nīrāṭutal, nīr parukal, uṇavaruntal mutaliyavaṟṟiyum oḻuṅkākac ceytu, tīṉikkuṭal  malakkuṭalkaḷiyun tuppuravu ceytu viṭṭāl nōyp paṭāmal iṉitu vāḻalām.
iṉi, uṭampiṉ kaṇuḷḷa aiyum pittum vaḷiyumākiya mutaṟ poruḷkaḷi oppa vittuk koḷḷutaṟkum, nuriyīrali valuppaṭutti irattattit tūyatākkutaṟkum nāṭōṟum oṉpāṉ puḻikaḷ vaḻiyāka veḷippaṭun tūvā naccup poruḷkaḷ uṭampiṟ ciṟitun taṅkāmal veḷippaṭutaṟkum, namatu aṟivi virivuceytu viḷakku taṟkum, uṭampiṟ ṟaṭpa veppaṅkaḷ eṉṟum oru niliyāka iruttaṟkuṅ kaṭṭāyamākac ceyaṟpālatu mūccup paḻakkamē yākum. mūccup paḻakkamāṉatu viṭutal, iḻuttal, niṟuttal eṉṉum mūṉṟu uṟuppukaḷōṭu kūṭi naṭipeṟu vatākum. ivaṟṟuḷ, viṭutal eṉpatu uṭampiṉ uḷniṉṟa kāṟṟi veḷiyē kaḻittal; iḻuttal eṉtu veḷiyēyuḷḷa tūya kāṟṟi uṭampiṉuḷḷē vāṅkutal; niṟuttal eṉpatu aṅṅaṉam uḷḷiḻutta kāṟṟi uṭampiṉuḷ ḷēyē ciṟitu nēram nilippittal; ivvāṟu kāṟṟimūkkup puḻi kaḷāl māṟi māṟip puṟaṅ kaḻittu, uḷḷiḻuttu, uḷniṟuttu talāl uṭampil vintu nātaṅkaḷākiya mika nuṇṇiya taṭpa veppaṅkaḷ oruniliyākap paravik karuvikaḷi valup paṭutti uṭampip poṉpōṟṟūyatākkum. iṅṅaṉam uṭampu valuppaṭṭup poṉṉiyalpāṉavaṉukku uṭampiṟ paḷappaḷap pāṉa poṉṉiṟamum, miyip pōṉṟa kaṟutta mayiruṅ kāṇappaṭum. avaṉi nari, tiri, mūppu,  piṇi, cākkāṭu aṇukā. aḻiyā iyalpit tarum. im mūccup paḻakkatti meykkuru mukamākak kēṭṭup paḻakuvatē ellārkkum naṉṟākum. meykkuru mukamāka vaṉṟik kāvik kōlam mēṟkoṇṭavarkaḷi yellām meykkuruveṉa nampi avar kūṟuvaṉa kēṭṭu uyiri iḻappa varkaḷ ulakiṟ palar avvāṟu ēmāntu pōkāmal meyk kuruvit terintu avarpāl aṟivuri kēṭṭup paḻakip piḻittiṭuka. itu meykkuruviṉuri, itu poykkuruviṉuri eṉṟu pakuttaṟivutu evvāṟu kūṭumeṉil, uṇmiyāṉa mūccup paḻakkamāyiṉ atu paḻakutaṟku mikka varuttan tārātāyp paḻakun tōṟum iṉpattiṉiyum maṉakkiḷarcciyiṉiyum yākki nalattiṉiyun tarum; aṅṅaṉam allāta poyp paḻakkamāyiṟ paḻakuṅkāl aḷaviṟanta varuttattiṉit tantu nāḷēṟa nāḷēṟat tuṉpattiṉiyum nōyiṉiyum varuvikkum. itu terintu koṇṭavuṭaṉē itaṉi aṟivuṟuttiṉavaṉ poykkuru veṉṟum. itu poyp paḻakkameṉṟun teḷintu ataṉip paḻakātu uṭaṉē viṭṭiṭuka. iṉi, nalla mūccup paḻakkattiṉ iyalpi uṇaravēṇṭuvār ataṉi namatu civa cittānta ñāṉa pōtattiṟ kaṇṭu teḷiyakkaṭavar. poyp paḻakkattit toṭarntu paḻakuvārkku veṟi, mārpaṭippu, eṉpurukki, kākkivali, kikāl marattal mutaliya pala koṭiya nōykaḷ varum; meyp paḻakkattit toṭarntu paḻakuvārkku mēṟkuṟitta nōykaḷellām vilakip pōva tōṭu aṟivum valivum nāḷukkunāḷ mikuntu varum. iṉṉum inta uṇmip paḻakkattāl varum aḷappariya naṉmikaḷiṟ cila, cilanāṭ paḻakkattilēyē varuvatu nāḷaṭiviṟ kaṇṭu koḷḷalām.
4. neruppu 
      (cūṭu -oḷi)
mēli iyalkaḷil mūccup paḻakkattip paṟṟi virivākap pēci vantēm.iṉi inta iyalilumitaṟku aṭutta iyalilun tīyip paṟṟip pēcap pōkiṉṟēm. tīyiliruntu namatu paḻakkat tiṟku varuvaṉa cūṭum oḷiyum ākum. ivaṟṟuṭ cūṭṭip paṟṟi inta iyalilum oḷiyip paṟṟi aṭutta iyalilum pēcuvēm.
nīrum neruppum ulaka oḻukkattiṟku iṉṟiyamiyāta vāyilkaḷāyirukkiṉṟaṉa. ik kālattiṟ putumi yiṉum putumiyāyt tōṉṟi namakkup palavikiyiṟ payaṉpaṭṭu varum nīrāvi vaṇṭikaḷum nīrāvik kappalkaḷum nīrum neruppum illiyāṉāl irunta iṭam viṭṭum  peyara māṭṭāvē, aṅṅaṉamē nīrum neruppumilli yāṉāl namatuṭampum uyirōṭu irāmaṟ piṇamāyk kiṭakkum. uyir uṭampil ulavum variyilum uṭampiṟ cūṭāṉatu kāṇappaṭum; aḵtu uṭampi viṭṭup pōy viṭumāyiṟ cūṭum illāmaṟ pōyviṭuvatu tiṇṇam ākavē, uyir uṭampil nilipeṟutaṟku mutaṉmi yāṉa karuvi cūṭēyāku meṉpatu ciṟu makārukkun terinta toṉṟēyākum. uyirkkāṟṟiṉ ōṭṭattiṉāl uṭampiṟ cūṭu uṇṭāku talāṟṟāṉ, irattamāṉatu iṟuki uṟintu pōkāmaṟ pallāyira irattak kuḻāykaḷilum paravi ōṭi uṭampiṟku vēṇṭiya ellā mutalkaḷiyun tantu vaḷarttu varukiṉṟatu, namatu uṭampi luḷḷa uṟuppukaḷ ellā vaṟṟilum paravi niṟintirukkuñ cūṭāṉatu, ciṟcila nēraṅkaḷiṟ ciṟcila uṟuppukaḷiṟ paravāmaṟ kuṟintu pōkumāṉāl uṭaṉē avvuṟuppukaḷ nīṭṭavum muṭakkavum ākāmal timir piṭittup pōvati nām kaṇkūṭāyp pārkkalām. uṭampiluḷḷa uṟuppukaḷ eḷitiṟ cīrāka  acintu toḻiṟ paṭutaṟku avaṟṟiṟ cūṭu iruttal mutaṉmiyākum. eḵku, irumpu mutaliyavaṟṟiṟ katti mutaliya karuvikaḷ ceyvōr avikaḷ naṉṟākap patappaṭṭu varutaṟkup pala muṟiyum avaṟṟi neruppiliṭṭuk kāyccuvatip pārkkiṉṟōm allamō? neruppiṉuṭaṉ iyipu paṭṭā lallāmal avikaḷ iḻintu patappaṭṭu varāmipōla, namatu ṭampuñ cūṭṭōṭu iyipupaṭṭā lallāmaṟ patappaṭṭu varamāṭṭātu.
iṉi, ivvuṭampāṉatu nilipeṟṟu vaḷarutaṟkuc cūṭu oru peruṅkaruviyāyiruppatu pōlavē, ivvuṭampu tōṉṟutaṟkum aḵtu aḻitaṟkuñ cūṭē karuviyāyiruk kiṉṟatu. kōḻi tāṉ iṭṭa muṭṭikaḷiṉiṉṟuṅ kuñcu porippataṟku am muṭṭikaḷiṉmēl aṭi kiṭappataṉip pārttirukkiṉṟīrkaḷ allirō? etaṟkāka aḵtu am muṭṭikaḷiṉmēl iruntu aṭi kākkiṉṟatu? taṉatu uṭampiṉ cērkkiyāl am muṭṭikaḷiṟ cūṭu piṟappittu, ac cūṭṭiṉ utaviyāl muṭṭiyuḷ nīr vaṭivāyirukkuṅ karuvāṉatu muṟṟik kuñciṉ vaṭivu aṭintu veḷippaṭu taṟkākavaṉṟō? ivvāṟē oru peṇṇiṉ karuppiyuḷ ḷirukkiṉṟa karuvum, appeṇṇiṉ akaṭṭut tīyiṉ cērkki yāl vaḷarntu patappaṭṭuk ki kāl mutaliya uṟuppukaḷ porunti aḻakiya makavāy veḷippaṭu kiṉṟatu. nilattiṉuḷ ūṉṟiya vitiyum nilattiṉkaṇ uḷḷa katakatappāl uyirkkiḷarccipeṟṟu muḷi yīṉṟu vērōṭi, mēlē tuḷirum iliyuṅ kavaruṅ kiḷiyumāyc ceḻippuṟṟu ōṅki maramāy vaḷarkiṉṟatu. iṅṅaṉamē ulakat tuyir kaḷellām uṭampoṭu tōṉṟutaṟkut tīyiṉatu cūṭu periya toru karuviyāy iruntu utavi purintu varutal kāṇmiṉ!
ivvāṟu uṭampiṉit tōṟṟuvittum ataṉi nili peṟuvittum varukiṉṟa cūṭāṉatu, avvuṭampiṉuḷ aḻukkukaḷ niṟintu veḷippōkāmal taṅkiviṭumāṉāl avvaḻukkukaḷi erittu uṭampit tūyatākkutaṟkut tuvaṅkukiṉṟatu. avvāṟu tuvaṅki av vaḻukkukaḷi erittu varumpōtu. avvaḻukkukaḷ uṭampiṉaḷaviṉum mēṟpaṭu māṉāl avvuṭampu payaṉpaṭā teṉpati yuṇarntu avvuṭampiyuṅ kūṭavē cērttu erittu viṭukiṉṟatu. iṅṅaṉam uṭampiṉuḷḷē tōṉṟuñ cūṭṭiṉ mikutiyiyē curam eṉṟuṅ kāyccal eṉṟum vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟārkaḷ. uṭampil niṟinta tūvā naccup poruṭ ṭokuti ciṟitāyiruntāl ataṉi erikkum poruṭṭut tōṉṟuñ cūṭākiya curamuñ ciṟiyatāy ataṉi eḷitilē erittut taṇintupōkum; avvāṟaṉṟi naccup poruṭ ṭokuti peritāyiruntāl ataṉi erikkat tōṉṟuṅ kāyccalum peritāy vaḷarntu eriyum; appōtu ataṉ kaṭumiyāl uṭampiluḷḷa iratta mellāñ cuvaṟi vaṟaṇṭu pōka uyir ataṉuḷḷirukka iṭampeṟātāy ataṉiviṭṭu nīṅkip pōkiṉṟatu. ākiyāṟṟāṉ, kāyccaliṉ kaṭumi mikutip paṭāmal, mikavum uṉṉippāy ataṉit taṇivittaṟ kuriya muṟikaḷi uṭaṉē ceytal vēṇṭum. eṅṅaṉamāṉāluṅ kāyccal varuvatu uṭampiṉ nalattiṟkēyallāmal vēṟilli. naṉṟākak kāyccal nōy vantu pōkumāṉāl uṭampi luḷḷa aḻukkunīrkaḷellām muṟṟumē oḻintupōyviṭum. ātalāṟṟāṉ kāyccal nōy vantu pōṉa piṟaku oruvaṉṟaṉ uṭampāṉatu niṟattilum nalattilum mēm paṭṭuc ceḻippāy vaḷarkiṉṟatu. atu niṟka.
kāyccal nōy varuṅkālattu ataṉ kaṭumi mikutip paṭāmal taṇiyumāṟu ceytaṟku vaḻiyāteṉiṟ, kūṟuvām. uṭampiṉkaṇṇē tūvā naccup poruḷkaḷ cērum iṭaṅkaḷ; tīṉippi, malakkuṭal, ciṟunīrppi, mēṟṟōliṉ viyarvip puḻikaḷ, mūccu iyaṅkum nuriyīral eṉpaṉavi kaḷēyākum. kāyccal nōy kāṇumeṉṟu terintavuṭaṉē ivaṟṟit tūymip paṭutti, iraṇṭu kiḻami variyil avaṟṟit tūyavāka vittuk koṇṭu varuvamāyiṟ, kāyccalum maṟṟip piṇikaḷum nīṅki nalamāyirukka lām.avviṭaṅkaḷit tūymip paṭuttum vaḻikaḷ yāvi yeṉṟāṟ colluvām. kāyccal varumeṉṟu kaṇṭa vuṭaṉē uṇavu uṭkoḷḷu tali niṟutti viṭal vēṇṭum; orukāl mikunta paciyeṭuttāṟ kañci vakikaḷil ētāvatu ciṟitu uṭkoḷḷalām. paciyeṭāta variyil uṇavu ciṟitum uṭkoḷḷalākātu. iṅṅaṉam uṇavu uṭ koḷḷātirukkavē. vayiṟṟiluḷḷa pacit tīyāṉatu tīṉippiyiluḷḷa tūvā naccup poruḷ kaḷellā vaṟṟiyum erittu akaṟṟiviṭum. ataṉoṭu nīr ēṟṟuṅ kuḻāy vaḻiyāka malakkuṭalukku nirēṟṟiṉāl, akkuṭalil uḷḷa malakkuviyal avvaḷavum veḷippaṭṭu malakkuṭal ciṟu nīrppikaḷ tūyavākum. ataṉoṭu vetu vetuppāṉa nīri oru tuṇiyiṟṟōyttuc ciṟitu piḻintu viṭṭu uṭampiṉmēṟ ṟēyttuk kaḻuvik kaḻuviṉavuṭaṉē īratti vēṟoru tūytāṉa tuṇiyiṉāṟ pularttik, kampaḷiyiṉāṟ pōrttuk koṇṭu iruntāl irattattiṟ kalanta aḻukkukaḷ ellām mayir tuḷikaḷiṉ vaḻiyē viyarvit tuḷikaḷāka veḷivantuviṭum; avvāṟu varum viyarvi tirumpavum uḷḷē cuvaṟāmal uṭaṉukkuṭaṉ ataṉit tuṇiyiṉāl tuṭittuviṭal vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam muṟiyākac ceytukoṇṭu vantāṟ cila nāḷiṟ kāyccal nōy taṇintu uṭampu nalappaṭum. ataṉōṭu oṭukkiṭaṅkaḷil irāmal naṉṟākap pōrttuk koṇṭē tūya kāṟṟu vīcum iṭattil iruttal vēṇṭum. inta vakiyākat tīṉippi, malakkuṭal, ciṟunīrppi, mēṟṟōliṉ viyarvit tuḷi kaḷ, nuriyīral mutaliyavaṟṟi tuppuravu ceytal vēṇṭum. iṉṉum immuṟikaḷip piṉṉē varum nōy nīkkum iyalkaḷil virittuk kūṟuvām. mēṟ colliyavāṟṟāl uṭampiṉuḷḷē tōṉṟuṅ kāyccal nōy eṉṉuñ cūṭṭip patam aṟintu taṇiyac ceyyāviṭṭāl, atuvē uṭampi aḻittuviṭum eṉṉum uṇmi cevvaṉē viḷaṅkā niṟkum.
iṉi, maḻikāla paṉikkālaṅkaḷiṟ kuḷirum ūtaṟ kāṟṟum mikutiyāyirukkumpōtu uṭampiṟ cūṭu cev viyāka irukkum vaṇṇañ ceytukoḷḷal vēṇṭum. ulaka iyaṟkiyi uṉṉittup pārmiṉkaḷ! veḷiyē kuḷir mikutippaṭṭāl ataṉit taṭuttu etir niṟpataṟkup pōtumāṉa aḷavu cūṭu uṭampiṟ ṟōṉṟu kiṉṟatu. iṅṅaṉam iyaṟkiyāka uṭampiṟ ṟōṉṟukiṉṟa cūṭṭik kuṟiyāmaṟ pātukākka vēṇṭuvatu ovvoru varkkum mutaṉmiyāṉa kaṭamiyā yirukkiṉṟatu. iṅṅaṉam iyaṟkiyāka uṭampiṟ cūṭu uṇṭākiyum, ataṉip pātukāvāmal uṭampiṉmēṟ kuḷir kāṟṟuppaṭumāṟu ataṉi āṭiyiṉṟit tiṟappāka viṭṭu viṭṭāl, mēlē kuḷirkāṟṟup paṭappaṭat tōliṉ aṭiyē ōṭik koṇṭirukkum irattamāṉatu aṅkaṅkē uṟintu kaṭṭippaṭṭu niṉṟupōkum. iratta ōṭṭaṅ kuṟintu irattam iṟukiyavuṭaṉē viḻittirukkak kūṭāta tūkkam vantu cērum. avvāṟu tūkkam varuvati muṉṉuṇarntu ataṉi nīkkat takkatu ceyyāmal oruvaṉ paṭuttu uṟaṅki viṭuvāṉāṉāl, aṅṅaṉam uṟaṅkiṉavaṉ maṟupaṭiyum uyirōṭu eḻuntirukka māṭṭāṉ. muṟṟum maṭattaṉamāyk kuḷirkāṟṟil taṉ uṭampik kāṭṭi uṭampiṉ nalam iḻantavaṉ uyira tappip piḻikka vēṇṭumāṉāl, uṭaṉē cāmpalic cūṭu piṟakka vaṟuttu oru tuṇiyiṟ kaṭṭi uṭampu muḻutum palamuṟi oṟṟaṭaṅ koṭuppatuṭaṉ, karuppura neyyiyum uṭampeṅkun taṭavic cūṭu piṟakkat tēyttuk kampaḷip pōrviyāṟ pōrttuk koṇṭiruntu uṭampeṅkum viyarvi uṇṭākac ceytal vēṇṭum. atu vallāmaluṅ, kaṭuki iṭṭuk kāycciṉa vennīrip poṟukku maḷavu cūṭākki atil iraṇṭu kālkaḷiyuñ ciṟitu nēran tōyttu vittiruntu, piṟaku eṭuttu īrattit tuvarttiviṭṭuk kampaḷiyāl mūṭi vikka vēṇṭum.
mēlum ūtaṟkāṟṟil taṉṉuṭampi neṭunēran tiṟantu vittiruntavaṉukku mēṟ colliyavāṟē tōliluḷḷa irattam uṟintu pōkā viṭṭāl, vēṟoru kaṭumiyāṉa nōy uṇṭākum. tōliṉmēṟ kuḷirkāṟṟu neṭunēram vīcuvatāl tōliṉ aṭiyil ōṭum iratta mellām aṅkē ulavuvataṟku iṭampeṟāmal uṭampi ṉuḷḷē irukkum irattap pikaḷ kuḻāykaḷiṟ ṟirumpippōy av viṭaṅkaḷil aḷavukku mēl nirampit tiraḷiyāypōkum. ovvoruvar uṭampilum valivaṟṟa iṭaṅkaḷ pala iruttalāl, av valivaṟṟa iṭaṅkaḷukku avvirattam nērē ceṉṟu tiraḷiyāyp pōvatu paḻakkattil aṟiyappṭaṭatōruṇmiyākum. oruvarukku nuriyīral valuvillāta iṭamāyirukkumāyiṉ avviṭat tiṟkuk kuḷirāl turattappaṭṭa irattamāṉatu aṅkē aḷavukku mēṟpaṭṭu nirampik kaṭṭippṭaṭuppōkum. mēlē kuṟikkappaṭṭa vaṇṇam uṭampiṉuḷ eṅkē aḻukkuc cērukiṉṟatō aṅ kaataṉip pōkkutaṟ keṉṟu uṭaṉē kāyccal eṉṉum uyirt tīyāṉatu mikuntu tōṉṟā niṟkum. nuriyīralil irattamāṉatu taṅkik kaṭṭiyāki aḻukkup paṭṭuppōṉa vuṭaṉē, av vaḻukki nīkka uṭaṉē kāyccal eḻāniṟkum. ik kāyccalukku nuriyīraṟ kāyccal eṉpatu peyar. ik kāyccal mikak koṭumiyāṉatu. perum pālum maruntukaḷiṉāṟ ṟīrkkap paṭutaṟku iḵtu iṭantaruvatilli. nuriyīralil aḻukkuc ciṟitākiruntāl ik kāyccal mika mummuramāka nikaḻāmal tāṉē cila nāḷiṟ paṭintuviṭum; aḻukku aṅkē mikuti yāyc cērntiruntālō kāyccal mika mummuramāyt tōṉṟi avvaḻukki erippatoṭu kūṭac cērttu mūccōṭṭattiṟku iṭamāṉa nuriyīraliyum erittu uṭampiyum aḻittuviṭum. ākiyal, ittakiya koṭi yanōykaḷ varutaṟku iṭañ ceyyāmal ovvoruvarum mikavuṅ karuttāka iruttal iṉṟiyamiyāta teṉpataṉi yām collutalum vēṇṭumō? mēṟcolliyavāṟu maḻikāla paṉik kālaṅkaḷil veppak kuṟiccalāl uṭampukku iṭar varuvatu pōla, vēṉiṟ kālattil veppa mikuti yiṉālum uṭampukku iṭar varukiṉṟatu. veyiṟ kālattil veppam mikutiyā yirukkum pōtu uṭampit toṭṭup pārttal atu cilleṉṟu kuḷircciyāyirukkum. itaṉāl aṟiyaṟpālatu yātu? veḷiyē veppam mikutip paṭuṅkāl, uṭampiṟ kuḷircciyum mikuntirukka vēṇṭu meṉpatu aṉṟō? aṅṅaṉamallāmal uṭampilum veppam uṇṭākumāṉāl, uḷḷaminta karuvikaḷ cīrkulintu uṭampic citittuviṭum. ivvāṟu ulaka iyaṟkiyil aminta māṟutalkaḷik karutip pārāta maṭamiyiṉālēyē perumpālār ciṟupōtilēyē uyir tuṟakkiṉṟaṉar veyil mikutiyāyirukkumpōtu veḷiyē naṭattalāvatu tiritalāvatu kūṭātu; aṉṟit tirintāl mūḷiyil mayakkam ēṟik kaḷittu vīḻntu uyiri yiḻappar. maṟṟuk, kaṭṭāyamāka veyiliṟ pōka nērntāl, taṇṇiril tōytta tuṇiyip piḻintu īramākavē ataṉit taliyuṭampukaḷi mēṟ pōrttuk koṇṭu cellal naṉṟu. kaṟuppuniṟamuḷḷa tuṇikaḷi mēlē pōrttuk koṇṭu veyilil naṭattalākā. kaṟuppu niṟamāṉatu veyiliṉ veppatti iḻuttu uṭampiṟ celuttun taṉmi vāyntatu. veḷḷi niṟamō av veppattik kiṭṭa aṇukāmal taṭuttuniṟka vallatu; ākiyāl, tūveḷḷi yāṉa āṭikaḷi mēlē aṇintu celvatu veyiṟkālattil mikka naṉmiyit taruvatākum. iṉṉum veyiṟ kālattiṟkum maḻi kālattiṟkum ēṟkum vakiyāka uṇavu uṭkoḷḷum muṟikaḷip paṟṟi, aviyiṟṟi viḷakka vēṇṭum iyalkaḷiṟ piṉṉē virivākap pēcuvām. itu niṟka.
iṉic, cōmpēṟikaḷāyiruntu payaṉaṟṟa ceyalkaḷip purintu iṟakkum aṟivillā makkaḷiṟ palar nām mēlē eṭuttukkāṭṭiya arumaruntaṉṉa poruḷkaḷiṉ ciṟap piyum payaṉiyum aṟiya māṭṭātavarkaḷāy, ivika ḷellām eṉṉa? makkaḷ ceyyak kūṭiyaṉavā! veyiliṟ kāyntum maḻiyil naṉintuṅ kaṇṭa kaṇṭavaṟṟit tiṉṟum uyir vāḻkiṉṟavarkaḷ ettaṉi pēr! avar kaḷellām nōyiṉṟi evvaḷavō kālam uyirōṭiruk kiṟārkaḷē eṉṟu tamakkut tōṉṟiyavāṟellāṅ kaṇṇi mūṭik koṇṭu mikavum eḷitāyp pēciviṭṭup pōyviṭu kiṉṟārkaḷ. uṇṇac cōṟum uṭukkak kūṟiyum, irukka niḻalum iṉṟit tiripavar kaḷ tiraḷ tiraḷāka irukkiṟārkaḷ eṉpatu uṇmiyē. āṉāl, avarkaḷ nōy illāmaluñ ciṟupōtil iṟavāmalum neṭunāḷ uyirōṭiruk kiṟārkaḷ eṉpatō uṇmi aṉṟu; avarkaḷuṭaṉ kalantu pārttāl, avarkaḷ veyilukkum maḻikkuṅ kuḷirukkun tammip pātukāttuk koḷḷa muṭiyāmiyiṉālē ettaṉiyō vakiyāṉa nōykaḷukku iriyāki iḷampoḻutilēyē iṟantu pōkiṉṟaṉar eṉpatu naṉku viḷaṅkum. aṅṅaṉa māṉālum, avarkaḷiṟ palar neṭunāḷ variyil uyirōṭiruttaliyuṅ kaṇṭōmē eṉṟāl, avvāṟu uyirō ṭiruppavarkaḷ tammic cērntārilun tāñciṟitu aṟivuṭiyarāyt tamatu uṭampiṉ nalattit tammāṟ kūṭumaḷavum pātukākkak karuttuc celuttiṉavarkaḷē āṉa uṇmi avarkaḷi neruṅki kaṭṭāl terintu koḷḷalām pāruṅkaḷ! veyilukkuṅ kuḷirukkuṅ tammip pātukāttuk koḷḷak kūṭāta nilimiyiluḷḷa paṟavikaḷukkum vilaṅkukaḷukkum, avaṟṟiṉ nilimiyi naṉkuṇarnta aruṭperuṅ kaṭalākiya kaṭavuḷ, aṭarnta mayirip pōrvi pōl uṭampeṅkum vaḷarac ceytirukkiṉṟār. kuḷirnta nāṭukaḷiluḷḷa uyirkaḷukku uṭampiluḷḷa mayirāṉatu vevviya nāṭukaḷil uḷḷa uyirkaḷiṉa mayiriṉum mikutiyāyiruttali nīṅkaḷ uṟṟup pārttatilliyā? itaṉāl, iṟivaṉṟaṉ aruṭ kuṟip pāṉatu uyirkaḷi maḻikkum veyilukkun tappuvittup piḻippikka vēṇṭu meṉpatēyātal teḷivāy aṟikiṉṟō mallamō? aṅṅaṉamirukka `vāy puḷittatō, māṅkāy puḷittatō’  eṉṟavāṟāy, ivvuṇmi kaḷi āyntu ōyntu pārāmal `ivi yārāṟ ceyyamuṭiyum?’ eṉṟu mika eḷitāyp pēcip pōñ cōmpēṟikaḷ, piṟarurittu vāyil vitta vāḻippaḻattiyum uḷḷē viḻuṅkac cōmpa luṟṟu, itaṉi uḷḷē tuṟuttip pōkac ceyvār illiyā?  eṉṟu etirpārppavariyē opparākaliṉ, avari nōkki ivvarum poruḷkaḷ collappaṭa villi yeṉpati uṇarntu koḷka. ākavē, paṉik kālattiṟ kuḷiriṉāl varun tuṉpatti oḻittaṟkut takunta uṭikaḷi uṭuttuk koḷḷutal pōlavē, veyiṟ kālattiṟku icintavaṟṟi aṇivatum makkaḷiṉ pakuttaṟi vuṇarccikkup payaṉām eṉka. aṟiyāmiyāl veyiliṟṟirintu irattak kotippu uṇṭāki mayakkam aṭintu iṭarpāṭāṉa nilikku vantavarkaḷik kālantāḻttaliṉṟik kuḷirnta nīrilē ciṟitu nēran tali muḻukuvittu īrampulartti niḻal mikunta viṭattiṟ kiṭatti vittal vēṇṭum.ivṟu ceyyā viṭṭāl veyilāṟ ṟākkappaṭṭavarkaḷ tamatu uyiri iḻattal tiṇṇam. veyiliṟ ṟirintamiyāl oru noṭip poḻutil iṟantupōṉa ettaṉiyō peyarip paṟṟi yām kēḷvip paṭṭirukkiṉṟōm. veppam mikunta vēṉiṟ kālattiṟ pakaliṟ patiṉmūṉṟu nāḻikimutal irupattiraṇṭari nāḻiki variyil eṅkuntiriyalākātu; appōtu kuḷirnta niḻaluḷḷa iṭaṅkaḷilēyē iruttal vēṇṭum.
5. oḷi
oḷiyāṉatu ellā uyirkaḷiṉ uyir vāḻkkikkum iṉṟiyamiyāta tākum. veḷiccam illiyāṉāl ulakattil evvakiyāṉa toḻilum naṭipeṟātu. ōr āṭavaṉ ulakattiluḷḷa palavēṟu vakiyāṉa aḻakiya tōṟṟaṅkaḷiyuṅ kaṇṭu kaḷippatu oḷiyiṉ utaviyiṉā laṉṟō? avaṉ palavaḻiyiṟ ṟaṉṉi vantu cūḻkiṉṟa iṭarkaḷuk kellān tappip piḻippatu pakalava ṉoḷiyiṉ utaviyiṉālaṉṟō? pakalavaṉoḷi maṭṭum illi yāṉāl makkaḷellāruṅ kuruṭariṉuṅ kīḻppaṭṭavarāvar. kuruṭarāvatu piṟarutaviyiṉāl iṅkumaṅkum iyaṅka iṭampeṟuvar. katiravaṉatu oḷiyi iḻantavarō tamakku vēṟu tuṇi kiṭippatu kūṭāmiyāl iruntaviṭam viṭṭup peyartaṟkum ēlātavarāyiruppar. pāmpu, tēḷ, naṇṭut teṟukkāl mutalāṉa koṭiya naccuuyirkaḷ irukkumiṭaṅkaḷit terintu avaṟṟi vilaki oḻukal ñāyiṟṟiṉ oḷi yiṉālēyām. uḻutu payir ceyyum nilaṉ ituveṉṟum, itaṉi nīr nirappip paṇpaṭuttum vakiyituveṉṟum, pakuttaṟintu payir ceytu uṇavu peṟṟu vāḻtalum ñāyiṟṟiṉ oḷiyiṉālēyām. āṇ itu veṉṟum peṇ ituveṉṟum oruvariyoruvar uṇarntu maruvik kālvaḻi perukkutalum ñāyiṟṟiṉoḷi oṉṟiṉālēyām. eṅkum iruḷē cūḻntiruntāl uyirkaḷ tōṉṟutalum uyir vāḻtalum evvāṟṟāṉum ki kūṭātaṉavākum. itumaṭṭumā! uyirvāḻ uṭampukaḷ ceḻippuṟṟu vaḷarntu varuvataṟkum avikaḷ veyil veḷiccattil iruttal iṉṟiyamiyātatākum. piṉ varumāṟu ceytu ārāyntu pārttāl oḷiyiṉ payaṉ naṉṟākap pulappaṭum. iraṇṭu maṇ toṭṭikaḷil vittu vaḷarkkappaṭṭa ceṭikaḷil oṉṟi veyil veḷiccampaṭum iṭattilum, maṟṟoṉṟi atu paṭāmal iruḷāyirukkum maṟṟōriṭattilum vittut taṇṇīr vārttu varal vēṇṭum; iṅṅaṉam oru kiḻami allatu pattunāḷ ceytuvantapiṉ, iraṇṭu ceṭikaḷiyum ōriṭattiṟ koṇṭu vantu vittu avaṟṟi oppiṭṭup pārttāl, ñāyiṟṟi ṉoḷiyi liruntu vaḷarntu vanta ceṭiyāṉatu paccippacēl eṉṉa mikavuñ ceḻumiyāyiruttaliyum, atu paṭāmal iruḷil vaḷarnta ceṭiyāṉatu veḷiṟip pōyc ceḻumi yiṉṟi vāṭi vataṅkiyiruttaliyum eḷitiṟ kaṇṭukoḷḷa lām. iṉṉum veyil veḷiccamē paṭāta ōr iruḷmikunta iṭattil vittu vaḷartta kuḻanti yiyum, veyil veḷiccam paṭumāṟu vittu vaḷartta kuḻantiyiyum oppiṭṭup pārttāl, veḷiccattiliruntatu ceḻumiyāṉa irattam mikap peṟṟu mikavuñ curucuruppāyum aḻakāyum irukka, iruḷil vaḷarnata piḷḷiyō veḷuttuppōyc cōki piṭittu nalla iratta ōṭṭa miṉṟic cōmpik kiṭattalik kaṇṭu teḷiyalām. ākavē, makkaḷ uṭampiṉ vaḷarccikkuñ ceḻumikakum pakalavaṉiṭattiruntu varum veyil veḷiccam peritum iṉṟiyamiyātatāmeṉpatu peṟappaṭutal kāṇka.
iṉi, ñāyiṟṟiṉiṭattiruntu mika nuṇṇiya poru ḷākiya vintu eṉṉum oru miṉṉoḷiyāṉatu iṭiviṭātu tōṉṟi eṅkum paravik koṇ ṭirukkiṉṟa teṉpataṉi mūṉṟām iyalil iṉitu viḷakkiṉām. anta miṉṉoḷi yiṉālētāṉ uyirkaḷiṉ uṭampiṉiṉṟu kakkappaṭukiṉṟa naccuk kāṟṟukaḷum, nilattilum veḷiyilum uḷḷa naccup poruḷ kaḷum eppōtun tūymiyākkappaṭṭu varukiṉṟa.ṉa iv vintu voḷiyiṉ viyattakum āṟṟaliṉālē tāṉ pakaṟ kālattiṟ puṟpūṇṭu maraṅkaḷ mutalāṉa niliyiyaṟ poruḷkaḷ, maṟṟi uyirkaḷ veḷiviṭum naccuk kāṟṟit tām iḻuttuk koṇṭu, tūyatāṉa uyirkkāṟṟip puṟampē viṭukiṉṟaṉa. pakalavaṉillāta irākkālattilō avvō raṟivup poruḷkaḷ avvāṟu utaviyāṉa toḻilic ceyyak kūṭāmal, tāmum naccuk kāṟṟiyē veḷikkakkit tīṅkāṉa toḻilic ceykiṉṟaṉa, eṉavē uyirkkutaviyāṉa tūya kāṟṟut tōṉṟi ulavutaṟkuṅ katiravaṉ veḷiccam evvaḷavu iṉṟiyamiyāta tāyirukkiṉṟatu eṉṉum uṇmi uḷḷaṅki nellikkaṉipōṟ ṟeḷḷitiṟ pulappaṭukiṉṟa taṉṟō?
aṅṅaṉamāyiṉum, pakalavaṉillāta irāk kālattil nilaviṉoḷi uṇṭākiyāl, ñāyiṟṟiṉoḷiyē uyir vāḻkkikku iṉṟiyami yātatu eṉṟu colvatu evvāṟu poruntumeṉṟāl; tiṅkaḷukkuḷḷa oḷiyum pakalava ṉoḷiyiṉiṉṟum vantatēyākum kaṇṇāṭiyiṟ ṟōṉṟum mukamāṉatu ataṟku uriyatākāmal, veḷiyē meyyāka uḷḷa oruvaṉ mukattiṉatu etirt tōṟṟamāyiruttal pōlat tiṅkaḷiṉiṭattuḷḷa oḷiyum ñāyiṟṟi ṉoḷiyiṉ etir viḷakkamēyākum. nilaviṉiṭattiruntu varum ivvoḷi nērē tiṅkaḷukku uriyatākāmal, ñāyiṟṟi ṉukkē uriyatākum eṉpataṉi vāṉanūl uṇarntār iṉitu viḷakkik kāṭṭukiṉṟārkaḷ. ñāyiṟṟiliruntu ivvoḷi nērē varumpōtu itu mikunta valiyuṭiya tāy, vintu eṉappaṭum miṉṉi eṅkum paravac ceytu tulaṅkukiṉṟatu; atuvē etirt tōṟṟamāyt tiṅkaḷiṉ vaḻiyē varumpōtu taṉ āṟṟal kuṟintu nātam eṉappaṭuṅ kuḷirnta amiḻtatti eṅkum parappi oḷirkiṉṟatu. katiravaṉiṭattiruntu varuṅkāl āṟṟal mikuntiruttal paṟṟiyē ataṉiṭattuccūṭu kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟatu; tiṅkaḷiṉiṭattu etirttōṟṟamāka varumpōtō ataṉkaṭ cūṭu kāṇappaṭāmaṟ kuḷircciyē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. ākavē, irākkālattiṟ ṟulaṅkukiṉṟa nilavoḷiyum pakaloḷiyiṉ etirviḷakkamē yaṉṟi vēṟu allāmiyāl uyir vāḻkkikku ñāyiṟṟiṉoḷiyē evviṭattum ekkālattum utaviyāy iruppateṉpatu kaṭippiṭikka.
ittaṉi utaviyāyuḷḷa veyil veḷiccam nām irukkum vīṭukaḷil tārāḷamāka vantu paṭumpaṭi kūrikaḷ mēluñ cuvarkaḷilum cāḷaraṅkaḷ paṟpala amittu vittal vēṇṭum. katiravaṉoḷiyē paṭāmal iruḷ aṭintu kiṭakkum vīṭukaḷiṉ uḷḷē ennēramum iruppavarkaḷ, iruḷil vaḷarnta ceṭipōl veḷup paṭantu irattaṅ keṭṭup palvaki nōykaḷukku āḷāki iṟappārkaḷ. iṉṉum, pakalavaṉ oḷi uṭampiṉmēṟ paṭumpaṭiyāka, mēl aṇinta uṭikaḷik kaḷintuviṭṭu veyilil ulāvutal irattan tūyatāvataṟkuc ciṟatna vaḻiyākum. ēṉeṉṟāṟ, pakalavaṉ oḷiyiṟ kaṭpulaṉakkut teriyātapaṭi eṅkum paravik koṇṭirukkum vintu eṉṉum mika nuṇṇiya poruḷāṉatu mayirk kālkaḷiṉ vaḻiyē uṭampiṉuṭ pukuntu irattattit tuppuravu ceytu uḷḷaminta karuvikaḷi valivēṟac ceykiṉṟatu. nōyi uṇṭu paṇṇum nuṇṇiya puḻukkaḷ makkaḷ uṭam piṉuḷḷē palamukamāy vantu cērkiṉṟaṉa. ap puḻukkaḷ irattatiṟ kalantavuṭaṉē ataṉāl uṭampiṉuṭ pala viṭaṅkaḷukkum avi koṇṭu pōkkappaṭṭu avvavviṭaṅkaḷil valivu kuṟinta karuvikaḷik kuvittiṉṟu viṭukiṉṟaṉa. it taṉmiyavāṉa tīya puḻukkaḷ nuriyīralit tiṉṟuviṭutali ṉālēyē elumpurukki eṉṉum nōy uṇṭāki ettaṉiyō peyarkaḷiṉ uyirik koḷḷi koṇṭu pōkiṉṟatu. kōmārik kāyccal, toḻunōy mutalāṉa niṉittaṟkuṅ koṭumiyāṉa nōykaḷuṅkūṭa ippol lāta puḻukkaḷiṉ cērkki yiṉālētāṉ tōṉṟip palkōṭi yuyirkaḷi arit teṭuttuk koṇṭu pōy viṭukiṉṟaṉa! āṇ peṇ maruvutaliṟ ṟōṉṟuṅ koṟukku mutalāṉa koṭiya nōykaḷum it tīya puḻukkaḷiṉ nuḻiviṉālē tāṉ kiḷikkiṉṟaṉa! veyiliṉoḷi uṭampu muḻutum paṭumēyāṉāl, ataṉāl uḷḷē cellum vintuvāṉatu annaccup puḻukkaḷ ellā vaṟṟiyum oruṅkē koṉṟu uṭampi nōy aṇukāmaṟ kākkum. ikkālattup putu nākarikattiṉāl viḻuṅkappaṭṭa nammaṉōr tamatuṭampi uccantaliyiliruntu uḷḷantāḷ variyiṟ palavaki uṭikaḷāl naṉṟāka mūṭikkoḷkiṉ ṟārkaḷātalāl, veyiliṉoḷiyē avarkaḷmēṟ ciṟitāyiṉum paṭutaṟku iṭam illāmaṟ pōkiṉṟatu. pōkavē uṭampiṉ vaḷarc cikku iṉṟiyamiyāt tuṇi yāṉa vintuvāṉatu pōtumāṉa aḷavukku uṭcellak kūṭāmiyāl, ēṟkeṉavē uṭampiṟ pukunta naccuppuḻukkaḷ iṟavāmal oṉṟu pallāyiramāyk kiḷittu uṭampi nōykku iriyākkukiṉṟaṉa. namatuṭam pil maṟṟi iṭaṅkaḷik kāṭṭiṉum maṟivāṉa iṭaṅkaḷ ennēramum iṭiviṭāmal tuṇikaḷiṉāl maṟikkap paṭṭiruttaliṉālēyē avviṭaṅkaḷ nalla iratta ōṭṭat tiṟku iṭampeṟāvāy valivukuṉṟip pōkiṉṟaṉa, nalla irattam kuṟintu valiviṉṟi irukkum avvuṟuppukkaḷil naccup puḻukkaḷ nuḻintāl koṟukkuppuṇ mutalāṉa nōykaḷ uṇṭāki maruntukaḷiṉun tīrāvāy āṇpeṇ kuṟikaḷit tiṉṟu viṭukiṉṟaṉa. aṅṅaṉam ākāviṭṭāl uyirk kiruppiṭamāṉa avvuṟuppukkaḷiṉ valivik kuṟittu avaṟṟiṟku uriyāri alikaḷākavum pēṭikaḷākavuñ ceytuviṭukiṉṟaṉa. nākarikam mikunta nāṭukaḷil irukkum makkaḷukku varum ittaṉmiyavāṉa koṭiya nōykaḷ āppirikkā amerikkā mutalāṉa nāṭukaḷiṟ kāṭukaḷilē kālaṅkaḻittu varum nākarikamillā māntarkkuc ciṟitum varuvatilliyeṉṟu innuṇukkaṅkaḷi muṟṟum ārāyntu aṟinta pāpiṭ1 eṉṉum amerikka aṟiñar iṉitu viḷakkik kāṭṭiyirukkiṉṟār. aḵtu etaṉāl eṉṟāl, annāṭṭiṉ kāṭukaḷilē vāḻum am makkaḷ mēlē āṭi aṇivatiṉṟi muṇṭamāka ulavutaliṉāl, veyil veḷiccam avarkaḷ uṭampu muḻuvatum paṭukiṉṟatu. aṅṅaṉam paṭum veḷiccat tiṉiṉṟu paravum vintuvāṉatu avarkaḷ uṭampiṉuḷ nuḻintu irattattiṟ kalantu naccup poruḷkaḷi muṟṟun tolittu, avvuṭampip poṉ vaṭivākac ceykiṉṟatu. ōr airōppiya aṟiñar ñāyiṟṟiṉoḷiyiṉ ivviyattakum āṟṟali iṉṉum uṟutiyākat terintu koḷḷal vēṇṭi, nākarikamaṟṟa am māntarkaḷiṟ cilari aḻittu avarkaḷ uṭampic cilanāḷ āṭikaḷiṉāl mūṭac ceytu, ataṉ piṟaku koṟukkuppuṇ puḻukkaḷi avarkaḷ uṭampiṉuḷḷē nuḻiyaviṭṭār. cila nāṭkaḷ variyiṟ pakaloḷi paṭāmal maṟittu vikkappaṭṭa avarkaḷuṭampilē an naccup puḻukkaḷ nuḻintu uṭampeṅkuṅ koṟukkup puṇṇi uṇṭākkip perukiṉa. itaṉik kaṇṭatum avvaṟiñar avarkaḷ mēlē aṇintirunta āṭikaḷi yellāṅ kaḷintu vāṅkik koṇṭu maṟupaṭiyum avarkaḷik kāṭṭilē muṇṭamākavē ulavumpaṭi viṭṭu viṭṭār. appāṟ cila nāṭkaḷ ceṉṟa piṟaku tirumpavum avarkaḷi varavaḻittup pārkkiyil, avarkaḷuṭampil muṉṉē uṇṭupaṇṇappaṭṭa koṟukkup puṇkaḷ ellām muṟṟum oḻintu pōka avarkaḷ uṭampu tūya irattam mikuntu nalamuṭiya tāyirukkak kaṇṭār. atumutaṟ, pakalavaṉ oḷiyē tūya irattattiṟkum uṭampiṉ nalattiṟkum mutaṉmiyāṉa ētuvākum eṉṟu muṭivu kaṭṭiṉār. ākavē, pakalavaṉ veḷiccam nammēṟpaṭumpaṭi ceytu koḷvatu namatuṭam piṉip poṉṉuru vākkutaṟku iṉṟiyamiyāta toṉṟā meṉpatu uḷḷaṅki nellik kaṉipōl teṟṟeṉa viḷaṅkaṟpālatēyām.
avvāṟāyiṉ, nākarika vāḻkkiyiluḷḷa nammaṉōr mēlāṭiyiṉṟi muṇṭamāka ulavuvarāyiṉ, avar veṟi piṭittavareṉṟu ikaḻappaṭṭuc ciṟik kaḷattē iṭap paṭupavarē yeṉiṉ; aṟiyātu kūṟiṉāy, katirava ṉoḷi uṭampeṅkumpaṭumpaṭi ceytu koḷḷal vēṇṭum eṉṟu yām colliyatu koṇṭē yām muṇṭa māka ulavumpaṭi kūṟiṉēm eṉṟu karututal periyatōr aṟiyāmiyām. piṉṉi eṉṉiyō veṉiṉ, nām irukkum illaṅkaḷilē veyiṟ kāyvataṟkeṉṟu mēlē tiṟappākaviṭappaṭṭa taṉi aṟi oṉṟu iruttal vēṇṭum. avvaṟiyiṉuḷḷē ceṉṟu katavit tāḻ iṭṭa piṟaku āṭiyi muḻutuṅ kaḷintu viṭṭuk kuṟintatu oṉṟari nāḻiki2 yaḷavu veyil mēlē paṭumpaṭi kāya vēṇṭum, iṅṅaṉam nāṭōṟumāyiṉum oru kiḻamikku mūṉṟumuṟi yāyiṉum veyiṟ kāyntu vantāl uṭampiluḷḷa naccup poruḷkaḷellān tolintu pōkum; irattan tūyatāki uṭampi urampeṟac ceyyum; talimēl narittirunta mayirkaḷ ellām nari māṟik kaṉṉaṅkaṟēl eṉṟu ākum. kāli veyil pittattiyum ucciveyil kaḷippiyum uṇṭupaṇṇi uṭampiṟkut tīmiyi viḷikkumātalāl, irupattu nāṉku nāḻikikku mēṟ pakalavaṉ mēlpāṟ cāyumpōtu uṇṭākuñ cāyveyilē kāyvataṟku mikavum ēṟṟatākum eṉpataṉi nuṇ uṇarvāṟ kaṇṭu koḷka. naṉṟu coṉṉīr, amiḻta vuruvāṉa vintu nuṇporuḷip parappuṅ katiravaṉoḷiyil entap poḻutil iruntālum atu naṉmiyiyē ceyya vēṇṭum allāmaṟ kāliyiṟ pittattiyum naṭuppakaliṟ kaḷippiyum uṇṭupaṇṇu meṉpatu yāṅṅaṉam eṉiṉ; amiḻtamē yāṉālum aḷavu aṟintu uṇ eṉṟapaṭi nalla poruḷāyiruntālum ataṉip payaṉpaṭuttum muṟiyum poḻutum iṭamum nuṭpamāka uṇarntu koḷḷāmal uṭkoṇṭāl, atuvē tītu ceyyum. naṟumaṇaṅ kamaḻum paruppuṇavāyiruntālum niṟiya uṇṭa vayiṟṟilē ataṉic cērttāl atu tīmi ceyyumeṉa nām paḻakkattil aṟin tatilliyā? maṭavāroṭu kūṭi nukaruṅ kāmaviṉpattiṉuñ ciṟantatu ivvulakil vēṟu illiyā yiṉum, ataṉiyum poḻu taṟintu uṇarvu uruki muṟippaṭa nukarāviṭṭāl atuvun tīmiyē payattal kaṇṭāmaṉṟō? namatu uṭampu meṉmiyāṉa poruḷkaḷāl amikkappaṭṭa toṉṟākiyāl, aḵtu ep poruḷi ekkālattil ēṟṟuk koḷḷutaṟku icivu uṇṭō ataṉi aṟintu ataṟku ēṟpa oḻukutalē nīṇṭa kālam uyirvāḻvataṟkuc ciṟanta vaḻiyākum. veyil veppamillāmiyiṉālē irākkālattil nilamuṅ kāṟṟuṅ kuḷirntu kāṇappaṭum appōtu uṭampiṉuḷ niṉṟa veppamuṅ kuṟintirukkum. tirumpavum ñāyiṟu tōṉṟum viṭiyaṟ kālattilē uṭampiṉuḷ aṭaṅkiyirunta av veppamāṉatu muṉṉiluñ ciṟitu perukit tōṉṟum. veppattāl mikuvatu pittamē ākiyāl, kāli vēḷiyil ñāyiṟṟiṉetirilē uṭampiṟ pittam mikum. iyaṟki yilēyē ñāyiṟu tōṉṟumpōtu uṭampiṟ pittam mikutippaṭutalāl, piṉṉum veyil veppam uṭampiṉ mēṟ paṭumpaṭiyiruntāl atu piṉṉum mikuti yākum eṉpataṉi yām collavum vēṇṭumō? ātaliṉāṟṟāṉ, kāli veyil mēṟpaṭumpaṭi ulāvutal naṉṟākātu eṉṟu ulakattāruṅ kūṟukiṉṟārkaḷ. appaṭi yāṉāl veḷiyē kuḷir mikuntāl ataṉi etirttu niṟpataṟkup pōtumāṉa cūṭum, veḷiyē cūṭu mikuntāl ataṉi etirttaṟkup pōtumāṉa kuḷircciyum uṭampiṟ kāṇappaṭum eṉṟu muṉ iyalkaḷiṟ colliyataṟku māṟāka, ivviṭattē iravil nilaṅ kuḷirntamiyāl uṭampiluñ cūṭu kuṟiyum eṉṟum, viṭiyalilē katiravaṉ tōṉṟutalāl uṭampiṟ cūṭu mikum eṉṟuṅ kūṟiyatu eṉṉiyeṉiṉ; naṉṟu viṉāyiṉāy, veḷiyē kuḷirmikum pōtu uṭampiṟ cūṭu kāṇappaṭum eṉṟu mēl iyalkaḷiṟ kūṟiyatu, uṭampiṉ mēṟpākattiṟ ṟōliṉmēṟ cūṭu mikum eṉṟum, pakalil vemmi mikum pōtu uṭampiṟ kuḷircci kāṇappaṭum eṉṟatu uṭampiṉ mēṟṟōliṟ kuḷircci mikum eṉṟum aṟivittaṟkēyām. 
kuḷir kāṟṟu mēlē vīcumpōtu mēṟṟōlil ōṭum irattam iṟuki uṟintu pōkā vaṇṇam akaṭṭiṉ aṭiyil uḷḷa uyirt tīyāṉatu uṭampiṉ mēṟ pākattiṟ paravum; tīkkāṟṟu mēlē vīcumpōtu mēṟṟōlil ōṭum irattam cuvaṟip pōkātapaṭi mēṟṟōṟ cūṭṭiṟ perumpākam akaṭṭiṉ aṭiyiṟ pōyviṭum.  irākkālattil avvaṭiyiluḷḷa aṉaliṟ perumpākam uṭampiṉ mēṟ paraviyiruttalāl, akaṭṭiṉa ṭiyil atu kuṟintēyirukkum pakaṟkālattil uṭampiṉ mēluḷḷa cūṭṭiṉ perum pākam akaṭṭiṉaṭikkup pōtalāl aṅkē atu mikutippaṭṭu eriyum. ivvāṟu akaṭṭiṉaṭiyilē uḷḷa aṉal viṭiyaṟkālattilē mikutip paṭumpōtu, ataṟku arukāmi yilē uḷḷa pittap piyāṉatu avvaṉaṟ cuṭarāl tāvappaṭṭup pittaṅ kotittu mēlē poṅkumeṉṟaṟika. iṅṅaṉamākaliṉ mēl iyalkaḷiṟ colliyataṟkumiṅkē colliya taṟkuñ ciṟitum māṟu pāṭilliyeṉṟu cikkaṟuttu uṇarka. iṅkē urittatu koṇṭu viṭiyaṟkāliyiṟ pakalavaṉ veppam mēlē paṭumpaṭi yiruttal ākātu eṉat teḷika. veyil veppam mēlē paṭappaṭa uṭampiṉmēṟ paraviya cūṭellān tiraṇṭu akaṭṭiṉaṭikkup pōy aṅkuḷḷa uyirt tīyiṉi mikutippaṭuttum; atu mikutippaṭa ataṉ pakkattē uḷḷa pittam mikutippaṭum veyil veppam mēṟpaṭāviṭṭāṟ kāliyiṟ pittam taṉ elli kaṭavā tirukkum eṉka; aṅṅaṉamāyiṟ pakal muḻutumē veyil veppam paravik koṇṭiruttalāl, atu mēlē paṭumpaṭi eppōtirun tālum akaṭṭut tī mikuntu pittatti eḻuppumē eṉiṉ; aṟṟaṉṟu, katiravaṉ mēṟpāliṟ cāyumpōtu ataṉ veppaṅ kuṟitalōṭu eṅkuṅ kuḷir kāṟṟum vīcutalāl, akaṭṭuc cūṭu uṭampiṉ mēṟ paravutali nām paḻakkattiṟ kaṇālām; avvāṟu aṭivayiṟṟuc cūṭu kuṟintu mēlē paravuñ cāyaṅkāla vēḷiyil veyil veḷiccam mēlē paṭumāṉāl atu pittattic ciṟitāyiṉum mikutip paṭuttātu; uṭampit tūymi ceytu nalattil vikkum. āṉāluṅ, koṭiya vēṉiṟkālattil irupattu nāṉku nāḻikiyāṉa pōtilum veyil veppan taṇiyātiruttalāl atu cevviyākat taṇinta piṟakutāṉ anti veyiliṟ kāyattuvaṅkal vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉamellāṅ katiravaṉ cūṭāṟutal pārttu kāyntu koḷḷum vaki ciṟitu āḻntu eṇṇavallārk kellām iṉitu viḷaṅkumātalāl itaṉi iṉṉum virittuk kūṟātu niṟuttukiṉṟām.
aṭikkuṟippu
1.	“Syphilis that bane of the so called civilized nations which the physicians are so helpless to eradicate, is unknown among the nude nation unless introduced from abroad. This was the case in Central Africa, in Japan, in the Sandwich Islands, etc. as signified by Livingstone, Sit Samuel W.Baker and others” - Human Culture And Cure by E. D. Babbitt, M.D., LL, D., p. 57.
2.	36 maṇittuḷikaḷ (nimiṭaṅkaḷ) piṟpakal 3.30.kFnkš


6. niṟam
mēl iyaliṟ katiravaṉ oḷi makkaḷ vāḻkkikku iṉṟiyamiyātatāy irukkum iyalpum, ataṉi nām payaṉpaṭuttik koḷḷum muṟiyuñ cevviyāka viḷakkik kāṭṭiṉām. iṉi, ak katiravaṉ oḷiyil aṭaṅkiyirukkum niṟaṅkaḷip paṟṟiyum, an niṟaṅkaḷi nīṇṭa vāḻkkiyiṉ poruṭṭup payaṉpaṭuttik koḷḷum muṟikaḷip paṟṟiyum inta iyalil viḷakkip pēcap pōkiṉṟām.
pakalavaṉiṭattiliruntu varum veḷiccattip pārttāl atu veḷḷiveḷēl eṉṟutāṉ irukkiṉṟatu, namatu uṭampil uḷḷa mayirkaḷ ellāñ cilirttāl avi nērpaṭa neruṅki niṟpatu pōlak, katiravaṉ veḷiccattilum mayiriḻi yiṉum nuṭpamāṉa katirkaḷ palamukaṅkaḷilum kīḻum mēlum ākiya ellā iṭaṅkaḷilum nērpaṭa neruṅkip pāykiṉṟaṉa; iṅṅaṉam pāyum eṇṇiṟanta katirkaḷiṉ tokutiyiyē makkaḷ veḷiccam eṉṟu vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟār kaḷ. oru pāṉiyiṉ kaṇ aṭakkic camitta oru cōṟṟiṟ kuḷḷa patamē maṟṟic cōṟukaḷukkum patamā yiruttal kāṇap paṭuvatupōla, muḻu veḷiccattilum aṭaṅki yirukkuṅ katirkaḷil oru katiriṉ taṉmiyē maṟṟak katirkaḷiṭattuṅ kāṇappaṭum. nam vīṭukaḷiṉ  mēṟ kūriyiluḷḷa ciṟu tuḷiyiṉ vaḻiyē uḷ nuḻiyum oru ciṟu katirt tokutiyik kāṇpōmāyiṉ atuvum veḷḷi veḷēl eṉṟutāṉ irukkiṉṟatu. aṅṅaṉam vīṭṭiṉ akattē nuḻiyum oru ciṟukatirt tokutiyum, puṟattē kāṇappaṭum muḻu veḷiccamum veḷḷi veḷēl eṉṟē irukkak kāṇṭaliṉ; pakalavaṉ oḷiyiṟ pala niṟaṅkaḷ aṭaṅkiyirukkiṉṟaṉa eṉpatu uṇmi yātal yāṅṅaṉameṉiṟ kāṭṭutum:
